```
1→#!/usr/bin/env bash
     2→set -euo pipefail
     3→
     4→# Streamlined holistic-review pipeline using Codex subagents.
     5→# Usage:
     6→#   scripts/desloppify_holistic_subagents.sh [repo_root]
     7→#
     8→# Optional env vars:
     9→#   SCAN_PATH=src/            # path for final scan (default: src/)
    10→#   RUN_SCAN=1                # run scan after import (default: 1)
    11→#   DESLOPPIFY_PREP_ARGS=""   # extra args for `desloppify review --prepare`
    12→
    13→ROOT="${1:-.}"
    14→ROOT_ABS="$(cd "$ROOT" && pwd)"
    15→SCAN_PATH="${SCAN_PATH:-src/}"
    16→RUN_SCAN="${RUN_SCAN:-1}"
    17→PREP_ARGS="${DESLOPPIFY_PREP_ARGS:-}"
    18→
    19→QUERY="$ROOT_ABS/.desloppify/query.json"
    20→SUBAGENT_DIR="$ROOT_ABS/.desloppify/subagents"
    21→PROMPTS_DIR="$SUBAGENT_DIR/prompts"
    22→RESULTS_DIR="$SUBAGENT_DIR/results"
    23→LOGS_DIR="$SUBAGENT_DIR/logs"
    24→MERGED_JSON="$SUBAGENT_DIR/holistic_findings_subagents.json"
    25→
    26→require_cmd() {
    27→  command -v "$1" >/dev/null 2>&1 || {
    28→    echo "Missing required command: $1" >&2
    29→    exit 1
    30→  }
    31→}
    32→
    33→require_cmd jq
    34→require_cmd codex
    35→require_cmd desloppify
    36→
    37→mkdir -p "$PROMPTS_DIR" "$RESULTS_DIR" "$LOGS_DIR"
    38→rm -f "$PROMPTS_DIR"/batch-*.md "$RESULTS_DIR"/batch-*.raw.txt "$RESULTS_DIR"/batch-*.json "$LOGS_DIR"/batch-*.log
    39→
    40→echo "[1/6] Preparing holistic review packet..."
    41→(
    42→  cd "$ROOT_ABS"
    43→  # shellcheck disable=SC2086
    44→  desloppify review --prepare --holistic --refresh $PREP_ARGS >/dev/null
    45→)
    46→
    47→batch_count="$(jq '.investigation_batches | length' "$QUERY")"
    48→if [[ "$batch_count" -le 0 ]]; then
    49→  echo "No investigation batches found in $QUERY" >&2
    50→  exit 1
    51→fi
    52→
    53→echo "[2/6] Building $batch_count batch prompts..."
    54→for ((i=0; i<batch_count; i++)); do
    55→  n=$((i + 1))
    56→  name="$(jq -r ".investigation_batches[$i].name" "$QUERY")"
    57→  dims="$(jq -r ".investigation_batches[$i].dimensions | join(\",\")" "$QUERY")"
    58→  why="$(jq -r ".investigation_batches[$i].why" "$QUERY")"
    59→  files="$(jq -r ".investigation_batches[$i].files_to_read[]" "$QUERY" | sed 's/^/- /')"
    60→
    61→  cat > "$PROMPTS_DIR/batch-${n}.md" <<EOF
    62→You are a focused subagent reviewer for a single holistic batch.
    63→
    64→Repository root: $ROOT_ABS
    65→Input packet: .desloppify/query.json
    66→Batch index: $n
    67→Batch name: $name
    68→Batch dimensions: $dims
    69→Batch rationale: $why
    70→
    71→Files assigned:
    72→$files
    73→
    74→Task requirements:
    75→1. Read .desloppify/query.json and use the holistic review rules in system_prompt.
    76→2. Evaluate ONLY the listed files and ONLY the listed dimensions for this batch.
    77→3. Emit 0-5 high-quality cross-file findings for this batch (empty array allowed).
    78→4. Every finding must include related_files with at least 2 files from this batch.
    79→5. Do not edit repository files.
    80→6. Return ONLY valid JSON with this shape:
    81→{
    82→  "batch": "$name",
    83→  "assessments": {"<dimension>": <0-100>, ...},
    84→  "findings": [ ... ]
    85→}
    86→EOF
    87→done
    88→
    89→echo "[3/6] Running subagents in parallel..."
    90→pids=()
    91→for ((n=1; n<=batch_count; n++)); do
    92→  prompt_text="$(cat "$PROMPTS_DIR/batch-${n}.md"; printf '\n\nReturn ONLY valid JSON, no markdown fences.\n')"
    93→  codex -a never -s workspace-write exec --ephemeral \
    94→    -C "$ROOT_ABS" \
    95→    -o "$RESULTS_DIR/batch-${n}.raw.txt" \
    96→    "$prompt_text" >"$LOGS_DIR/batch-${n}.log" 2>&1 &
    97→  pids+=("$!")
    98→done
    99→
   100→for pid in "${pids[@]}"; do
   101→  wait "$pid"
   102→done
   103→
   104→echo "[4/6] Validating subagent JSON outputs..."
   105→for ((n=1; n<=batch_count; n++)); do
   106→  jq -e . "$RESULTS_DIR/batch-${n}.raw.txt" > "$RESULTS_DIR/batch-${n}.json"
   107→done
   108→
   109→echo "[5/6] Merging outputs..."
   110→jq -s '
   111→  {
   112→    assessments: (
   113→      [ .[] | (.assessments // {}) | to_entries[] ]
   114→      | group_by(.key)
   115→      | map({
   116→          key: .[0].key,
   117→          value: ((map(.value) | add) / (length) | (.*10 | round / 10))
   118→        })
   119→      | from_entries
   120→    ),
   121→    findings: (
   122→      [ .[] | (.findings // [])[] ]
   123→      | unique_by((.dimension // "") + "::" + (.identifier // ""))
   124→    )
   125→  }
   126→' "$RESULTS_DIR"/batch-*.json > "$MERGED_JSON"
   127→
   128→echo "[6/6] Importing merged findings..."
   129→(
   130→  cd "$ROOT_ABS"
   131→  desloppify review --import ".desloppify/subagents/holistic_findings_subagents.json" --holistic
   132→)
   133→
   134→if [[ "$RUN_SCAN" == "1" ]]; then
   135→  echo "[7/7] Running scan on $SCAN_PATH..."
   136→  (
   137→    cd "$ROOT_ABS"
   138→    desloppify scan --path "$SCAN_PATH"
   139→  )
   140→fi
   141→
   142→echo "Done."
   143→echo "Merged findings: $MERGED_JSON"
   144→
```

1→---
     2→name: desloppify
     3→description: >
     4→  Codebase health scanner and technical debt tracker. Use when the user asks
     5→  about code quality, technical debt, dead code, large files, god classes,
     6→  duplicate functions, code smells, naming issues, import cycles, or coupling
     7→  problems. Also use when asked for a health score, what to fix next, or to
     8→  create a cleanup plan. Supports TypeScript/React, Python, and C#/.NET.
     9→allowed-tools: Bash(desloppify *)
    10→---
    11→
    12→# Desloppify
    13→
    14→## 1. Your Job
    15→
    16→**Improve code quality by fixing findings and maximizing strict score honestly.**
    17→Never hide debt with suppression patterns just to improve lenient score. After
    18→every scan, show the user ALL scores:
    19→
    20→| What | How |
    21→|------|-----|
    22→| Overall health | lenient + strict |
    23→| 5 mechanical dimensions | File health, Code quality, Duplication, Test health, Security |
    24→| 7 subjective dimensions | Naming Quality, Error Consistency, Abstraction Fit, Logic Clarity, AI Generated Debt, Type Safety, Contract Coherence |
    25→
    26→Never skip scores. The user tracks progress through them.
    27→
    28→## 2. Core Loop
    29→
    30→```
    31→scan → follow the tool's strategy → fix or wontfix → rescan
    32→```
    33→
    34→1. `desloppify scan --path .` — the scan output ends with **INSTRUCTIONS FOR AGENTS**. Follow them. Don't substitute your own analysis.
    35→2. Fix the issue the tool recommends.
    36→3. `desloppify resolve fixed "<id>"` — or if it's intentional/acceptable:
    37→   `desloppify resolve wontfix "<id>" --note "reason why"`
    38→4. Rescan to verify.
    39→
    40→**Wontfix is not free.** It lowers the strict score. The gap between lenient and strict IS wontfix debt. Call it out when:
    41→- Wontfix count is growing — challenge whether past decisions still hold
    42→- A dimension is stuck 3+ scans — suggest a different approach
    43→- Auto-fixers exist for open findings — ask why they haven't been run
    44→
    45→## 3. Commands
    46→
    47→```bash
    48→desloppify scan --path .                  # full scan
    49→desloppify status                          # score summary
    50→desloppify next --count 5                  # top priorities
    51→desloppify show <pattern>                  # filter by file/detector/ID
    52→desloppify plan                            # prioritized plan
    53→desloppify fix <fixer> --dry-run           # auto-fix (dry-run first!)
    54→desloppify move <src> <dst> --dry-run      # move + update imports
    55→desloppify resolve fixed|wontfix|false_positive "<pat>"   # classify finding outcome
    56→desloppify review --prepare                # generate subjective review data
    57→desloppify review --import file.json       # import review results
    58→```
    59→
    60→## 4. Subjective Reviews (biggest score lever)
    61→
    62→Score = 75% mechanical + 25% subjective. Subjective starts at 0% until reviewed.
    63→Default dimensions:
    64→`naming_quality`, `error_consistency`, `abstraction_fitness`,
    65→`logic_clarity`, `ai_generated_debt`, `type_safety`, `contract_coherence`.
    66→
    67→1. `desloppify review --prepare` — writes review data to `query.json`
    68→2. Launch an isolated reviewer (Claude subagent, or Codex fresh thread/worktree/cloud task) to read `query.json` (or `.desloppify/review_packet_blind.json`), review files, and write assessments:
    69→   ```json
    70→   {
    71→     "assessments": {
    72→       "naming_quality": 75,
    73→       "error_consistency": 75,
    74→       "abstraction_fitness": 75,
    75→       "logic_clarity": 75,
    76→       "ai_generated_debt": 75,
    77→       "type_safety": 75,
    78→       "contract_coherence": 75
    79→     },
    80→     "findings": []
    81→   }
    82→   ```
    83→3. `desloppify review --import review_output.json`
    84→
    85→Even moderate scores (60-80) dramatically improve overall health.
    86→
    87→## 5. Quick Reference
    88→
    89→- **Tiers**: T1 auto-fix, T2 quick manual, T3 judgment call, T4 major refactor
    90→- **Zones**: production/script (scored), test/config/generated/vendor (not scored). Fix with `zone set`.
    91→- **Auto-fixers** (TS only): `unused-imports`, `unused-vars`, `debug-logs`, `dead-exports`, etc.
    92→- **query.json**: After any command, has `narrative.actions` with prioritized next steps.
    93→- `--skip-slow` skips duplicate detection for faster iteration.
    94→- `--lang python`, `--lang typescript`, or `--lang csharp` to force language.
    95→- C# defaults to `--profile objective`; use `--profile full` to include subjective review.
    96→- Score can temporarily drop after fixes (cascade effects are normal).
    97→
    98→## 6. Escalate Tool Issues Upstream
    99→
   100→When desloppify itself appears wrong or inconsistent:
   101→
   102→1. Capture a minimal repro (`command`, `path`, `expected`, `actual`).
   103→2. Open a GitHub issue in `peteromallet/desloppify`.
   104→3. If you can fix it safely, open a PR linked to that issue.
   105→4. If unsure whether it is tool bug vs user workflow, issue first, PR second.
   106→
   107→## Prerequisite
   108→
   109→`command -v desloppify >/dev/null 2>&1 && echo "desloppify: installed" || echo "NOT INSTALLED — run: pip install --upgrade git+https://github.com/peteromallet/desloppify.git"`
   110→
   111→
   112→## Codex Overlay
   113→
   114→Codex uses isolated tasks/worktrees for independent review passes.
   115→
   116→1. Run subjective review tasks in a fresh worktree/thread (or a separate Codex cloud task).
   117→2. Keep reviewer input scoped to `.desloppify/review_packet_blind.json` plus referenced source files.
   118→3. Do not use prior score history, narrative summaries, or issue labels while scoring.
   119→4. Return machine-readable JSON only for review imports:
   120→
   121→```json
   122→{
   123→  "assessments": {
   124→    "naming_quality": 0,
   125→    "error_consistency": 0,
   126→    "abstraction_fitness": 0,
   127→    "logic_clarity": 0,
   128→    "ai_generated_debt": 0,
   129→    "type_safety": 0,
   130→    "contract_coherence": 0
   131→  },
   132→  "findings": []
   133→}
   134→```
   135→

```
   218→## Wontfix Categories (with justification)
   219→
   220→### "No direct tests" (271 findings) — WONTFIX
   221→All 271 files have **transitive test coverage** via imports from tested modules. Writing direct unit tests for every file would be massive effort (weeks of work) with marginal return — the code IS tested, just indirectly. Focus test-writing effort on pure logic/utility functions that have complex branching, not on hooks and components that are integration-tested through their consumers.
   222→
   223→### Flat directories (14 findings) — WONTFIX
   224→Moving files into subdirectories doesn't improve code quality. It breaks `git blame`, creates merge conflicts, and makes existing mental models stale. The files in `src/shared/components/` (40 files) and `src/shared/hooks/` are findable via search. Directory restructuring is high-risk, zero-behavior-change work.
   225→
   226→### Magic numbers (29 findings) — WONTFIX
   227→These are domain-specific constants (animation durations, retry counts, buffer sizes, API limits). Extracting `const RETRY_DELAY_MS = 3000` is often LESS clear than `setTimeout(retry, 3000)` in context. The "magic number" rule is for business logic constants that appear in multiple places — these are all single-use.
   228→
   229→### Hardcoded URLs (13 findings) — WONTFIX
   230→API endpoint URLs, documentation links, CDN paths. These are configuration, not code smells. Moving them to a constants file adds indirection without value — the URL is the value, naming it doesn't help.
   231→
   232→### console.error without throw/return (10 findings) — WONTFIX
   233→These ARE the error handling. They log the error and continue execution. This is correct for non-fatal errors in UI code (clipboard failures, analytics failures, etc.). The alternative — throwing — would crash the UI.
   234→
   235→### Dead functions (most of 45 findings) — WONTFIX
   236→Investigated sample: nearly all are **intentional**:
   237→- `logInvalidationEvent`: empty by design, callsites preserved for debugging
   238→- `addCorruptionEvent`: guarded by `__CORRUPTION_TRACE_ENABLED__` flag
   239→- `triggerBrowserDownload`: actually has a real body (desloppify misdetected)
   240→- `transformGeneration`: actually has a real body (desloppify misdetected)
   241→- `noop`: used as default callback (could make props optional, but low-value)
   242→
   243→### Async without await (most of 20 findings) — WONTFIX
   244→Most are functions that return `runTaskCreationPipeline(...)` — they're async because the return type is `Promise<T>`, and the body is `return someAsyncCall(...)`. TypeScript doesn't require `await` before `return` in an `async` function — the promise is forwarded. This is correct code.
   245→
   246→### Subjective review stale (1051 findings) — WONTFIX
   247→"No human has reviewed this file" is metadata tracking, not a code quality issue. Running a holistic review would mark these resolved but wouldn't change any code.
   248→

   259→- [ ] Task 6: Extract useShotImagesEditorCallbacks inner hooks
   260→- [ ] Task 7: Audit .sort() without comparators
   261→- [ ] Task 8: Clean up clearAllEnhancedPrompts stub
   262→
   263→**Estimated total effort:** ~5 hours of focused work
   264→**Expected score impact:** 97.1 → ~97.5 (modest — the big win was the 208 dead exports already done)
   265→**Real impact:** Cleaner architecture in the 4 decomposed files, eliminated import cycles, honest dependency graph from barrel inlining
```

Excellent! I now have a comprehensive understanding of how desloppify is run in this project. Let me create a summary report.

## Summary: How "Desloppify" is Run in This Project

### 1. **Main Entry Point: Shell Script**

The primary way to run desloppify is via:
```bash
scripts/desloppify_holistic_subagents.sh [repo_root]
```

Located at: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/scripts/desloppify_holistic_subagents.sh` (144 lines)

**What it does:**
- Orchestrates a 7-step holistic code review pipeline using Codex subagents
- Prepares review data, distributes analysis across multiple parallel batch agents, validates outputs, and imports findings back into desloppify

**Key steps:**
1. Prepares holistic review packet: `desloppify review --prepare --holistic --refresh`
2. Reads investigation batches from `.desloppify/query.json`
3. Generates batch prompts (one per dimension/file-group)
4. Runs subagents in parallel using `codex -a never -s workspace-write exec`
5. Validates JSON outputs from each batch
6. Merges results with `jq`
7. Imports merged findings: `desloppify review --import`
8. Optionally runs final scan: `desloppify scan --path src/`

**Optional env vars:**
```bash
SCAN_PATH=src/              # path for final scan (default: src/)
RUN_SCAN=1                  # run scan after import (default: 1)
DESLOPPIFY_PREP_ARGS=""     # extra args for `desloppify review --prepare`
```

---

### 2. **Direct CLI Commands**

From the skill documentation and executed plans, key desloppify commands are:

```bash
# Core workflow
desloppify scan --path .                  # Full scan of codebase
desloppify status                         # Score summary
desloppify next --count 5                 # Top 5 priority items
desloppify plan                           # Prioritized action plan
desloppify show <pattern>                 # Filter findings by file/detector/ID

# Resolution
desloppify resolve fixed "<id>"           # Mark finding as fixed
desloppify resolve wontfix "<id>" --note "reason"  # Mark as intentional
desloppify resolve false_positive "<id>"  # Mark as false alarm

# Auto-fixes
desloppify autofix unused-imports         # Remove unused imports
desloppify autofix unused-vars            # Remove unused variables
desloppify autofix debug-logs             # Remove debug logs
desloppify autofix dead-exports           # Remove unused exports
desloppify fix <fixer> --dry-run          # Dry-run any auto-fixer

# Refactoring
desloppify move <src> <dst> --dry-run     # Move file + update imports

# Subjective reviews
desloppify review --prepare               # Generate review data packet
desloppify review --import file.json      # Import assessment results
```

---

### 3. **.desloppify/ Directory Structure**

```
.desloppify/
├── plan.json                    # Complete queue + resolution history (~3.4 MB)
├── plan.json.bak               # Backup of plan
├── plan-final.md               # Latest human-readable work queue
├── plan-triaged.md             # Detailed triage decisions
├── query.json                  # Investigation batches + scores (~49 KB)
├── review_packet_blind.json    # Files for subjective review (blind assessment)
├── state-typescript.json       # Full scan state/results (~4.1 MB)
├── state-typescript.json.bak   # Backup
├── subagents/
│   ├── runs/
│   │   ├── 20260304_034813/    # Batch run from March 4
│   │   │   ├── prompts/        # Per-batch markdown prompts
│   │   │   ├── results/        # Per-batch JSON outputs
│   │   │   ├── logs/           # Per-batch execution logs
│   │   │   └── holistic_issues_merged.json
│   │   ├── 20260305_034201/    # Latest batch run from March 5
│   │   │   └── [similar structure]
│   │   └── [other runs...]
│   └── holistic_findings_subagents.json  # Merged output from last run
├── review_packets/
│   ├── holistic_packet_20260305_034201.json
│   └── [other packet snapshots]
└── [other configuration files]
```

```
1→"""Pattern → issue-ID resolution for plan commands."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine.plan import PlanModel
     6→from desloppify.state import StateModel, match_issues
     7→
     8→
     9→def _append_unique(fid: str, seen: set[str], result: list[str]) -> None:
    10→    if fid in seen:
    11→        return
    12→    seen.add(fid)
    13→    result.append(fid)
    14→
    15→
    16→def _collect_plan_ids(plan: PlanModel | None) -> set[str]:
    17→    plan_ids: set[str] = set()
    18→    if plan is None:
    19→        return plan_ids
    20→    plan_ids.update(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    21→    plan_ids.update(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    22→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
    23→        plan_ids.update(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
    24→    return plan_ids
    25→
    26→
    27→def resolve_ids_from_patterns(
    28→    state: StateModel,
    29→    patterns: list[str],
    30→    *,
    31→    plan: PlanModel | None = None,
    32→    status_filter: str = "open",
    33→) -> list[str]:
    34→    """Resolve one or more patterns to a deduplicated list of issue IDs.
    35→
    36→    When *plan* is provided, literal IDs that exist only in the plan
    37→    (e.g. ``subjective::*`` synthetic items) are included even if they
    38→    have no corresponding entry in ``state["issues"]``.
    39→    """
    40→    seen: set[str] = set()
    41→    result: list[str] = []
    42→    plan_ids = _collect_plan_ids(plan)
    43→
    44→    for pattern in patterns:
    45→        matches = match_issues(state, pattern, status_filter=status_filter)
    46→        if matches:
    47→            for issue in matches:
    48→                _append_unique(issue["id"], seen, result)
    49→            continue
    50→        if pattern in plan_ids:
    51→            # Literal plan ID (e.g. subjective::foo) not in state issues
    52→            _append_unique(pattern, seen, result)
    53→            continue
    54→        if plan is not None and pattern in plan.get("clusters", {}):
    55→            # Cluster name → expand to member IDs
    56→            for fid in plan["clusters"][pattern].get("issue_ids", []):
    57→                _append_unique(fid, seen, result)
    58→    return result
    59→
    60→
    61→__all__ = ["resolve_ids_from_patterns"]
    62→
```

```
    10→from desloppify import state as state_mod
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.guardrails import require_triage_current_or_exit
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import resolve_lang

    16→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize

    18→from desloppify.engine.plan import (
    19→    add_uncommitted_issues,
    20→    append_log_entry,
    21→    has_living_plan,
    22→    load_plan,
    23→    purge_ids,
    24→    purge_uncommitted_ids,
    25→    save_plan,
    26→)
    27→import desloppify.intelligence.narrative.core as narrative_mod
    28→from desloppify.state import coerce_assessment_score
    29→
    30→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.persist import save_state_or_exit
    31→
    32→from .apply import _resolve_all_patterns, _write_resolve_query_entry
    33→from .queue_guard import _check_queue_order_guard
    34→from .render import (
    35→    _print_next_command,
    36→    _print_resolve_summary,
    37→    _print_subjective_reset_hint,
    38→    _print_wontfix_batch_warning,
    39→    render_commit_guidance,
    40→)
    41→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.attestation import (
    42→    show_note_length_requirement,
    43→    validate_note_length,
    44→)

    46→from .selection import (
    47→    ResolveQueryContext,
    48→    _enforce_batch_wontfix_confirmation,
    49→    _previous_score_snapshot,
    50→    _validate_resolve_inputs,
    51→)
    52→
    53→_logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    54→
    55→
    56→class ClusterContext(NamedTuple):
    57→    cluster_name: str | None
    58→    cluster_completed: bool
    59→    cluster_remaining: int
    60→
    61→
    62→def _capture_cluster_context(
    63→    plan: dict, resolved_ids: list[str],
    64→) -> ClusterContext:
    65→    """Determine cluster membership for resolved issues, pre-purge."""
    66→    clusters = plan.get("clusters") or {}
    67→    overrides = plan.get("overrides") or {}
    68→    # Find the cluster the first resolved issue belongs to
    69→    cluster_name: str | None = None
    70→    for rid in resolved_ids:
    71→        ov = overrides.get(rid)
    72→        if ov and ov.get("cluster"):
    73→            cluster_name = ov["cluster"]
    74→            break
    75→    if not cluster_name or cluster_name not in clusters:
    76→        return ClusterContext(cluster_name=None, cluster_completed=False, cluster_remaining=0)
    77→    # Count how many will remain after these ids are removed
    78→    current_ids = set(clusters[cluster_name].get("issue_ids") or [])
    79→    remaining = current_ids - set(resolved_ids)
    80→    return ClusterContext(
    81→        cluster_name=cluster_name,
    82→        cluster_completed=len(remaining) == 0,
    83→        cluster_remaining=len(remaining),
    84→    )
    85→
    86→
    87→def _validate_fixed_note(args: argparse.Namespace) -> bool:
    88→    if args.status != "fixed":
    89→        return True
    90→    note = getattr(args, "note", None)
    91→    if validate_note_length(note):
    92→        return True
    93→    show_note_length_requirement(note)
    94→    return False
    95→
    96→
    97→def _update_living_plan_after_resolve(
    98→    *,
    99→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   100→    all_resolved: list[str],
   101→    attestation: str | None,
   102→) -> tuple[dict | None, ClusterContext]:
   103→    plan = None
   104→    ctx = ClusterContext(cluster_name=None, cluster_completed=False, cluster_remaining=0)
   105→    try:
   106→        if not has_living_plan():
   107→            return None, ctx
   108→        plan = load_plan()
   109→        ctx = _capture_cluster_context(plan, all_resolved)
   110→        purged = purge_ids(plan, all_resolved)
   111→        append_log_entry(
   112→            plan,
   113→            "resolve",
   114→            issue_ids=all_resolved,
   115→            actor="user",
   116→            note=getattr(args, "note", None),
   117→            detail={"status": args.status, "attestation": attestation},
   118→        )
   119→        if ctx.cluster_completed and ctx.cluster_name:
   120→            append_log_entry(
   121→                plan,
   122→                "cluster_done",
   123→                issue_ids=all_resolved,
   124→                cluster_name=ctx.cluster_name,
   125→                actor="user",
   126→            )
   127→        # Commit tracking: add to uncommitted on fix, remove on reopen
   128→        if args.status == "fixed":
   129→            add_uncommitted_issues(plan, all_resolved)
   130→        elif args.status == "open":
   131→            purge_uncommitted_ids(plan, all_resolved)
   132→        save_plan(plan)
   133→        if purged:
   134→            print(colorize(f"  Plan updated: {purged} item(s) removed from queue.", "dim"))
   135→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   136→        _logger.debug("plan update failed after resolve", exc_info=True)
   137→        print(colorize("  Warning: could not update living plan.", "yellow"), file=sys.stderr)
   138→    return plan, ctx
   139→
   140→
   141→def cmd_resolve(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   142→    """Resolve issue(s) matching one or more patterns."""
   143→    attestation = getattr(args, "attest", None)
   144→    _validate_resolve_inputs(args, attestation)
   145→    if not _validate_fixed_note(args):
   146→        return
   147→
   148→    state_file = state_path(args)
   149→    state = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   150→
   151→    if _check_queue_order_guard(state, args.patterns, args.status):
   152→        return
   153→
   154→    if args.status == "fixed":
   155→        require_triage_current_or_exit(
   156→            state=state,
   157→            bypass=bool(getattr(args, "force_resolve", False)),
   158→            attest=getattr(args, "attest", "") or "",
   159→        )
   160→
   161→    _enforce_batch_wontfix_confirmation(
   162→        state,
   163→        args,
   164→        attestation=attestation,
   165→        resolve_all_patterns_fn=_resolve_all_patterns,
   166→    )
   167→    prev = _previous_score_snapshot(state)
   168→    prev_subjective_scores = {
   169→        str(dim): (coerce_assessment_score(payload) or 0.0)
   170→        for dim, payload in (state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}).items()
   171→        if isinstance(dim, str)
   172→    }
   173→
   174→    all_resolved = _resolve_all_patterns(state, args, attestation=attestation)
   175→    if not all_resolved:
   176→        status_label = "resolved" if args.status == "open" else "open"
   177→        print(colorize(f"No {status_label} issues matching: {' '.join(args.patterns)}", "yellow"))
   178→        return
   179→
   180→    save_state_or_exit(state, state_file)
   181→
   182→    plan, cluster_ctx = _update_living_plan_after_resolve(
   183→        args=args,
   184→        all_resolved=all_resolved,
   185→        attestation=attestation,
   186→    )
   187→    mid_cluster = (
   188→        cluster_ctx.cluster_name is not None and not cluster_ctx.cluster_completed
   189→    )
   190→
   191→    _print_resolve_summary(status=args.status, all_resolved=all_resolved)
   192→    _print_wontfix_batch_warning(
   193→        state,
   194→        status=args.status,
   195→        resolved_count=len(all_resolved),
   196→    )
   197→    show_score_with_plan_context(state, prev)
   198→    if not mid_cluster:
   199→        render_commit_guidance(state, plan, all_resolved, args.status)
   200→    _print_subjective_reset_hint(
   201→        args=args,
   202→        state=state,
   203→        all_resolved=all_resolved,
   204→        prev_subjective_scores=prev_subjective_scores,
   205→    )
   206→
   207→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   208→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   209→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
   210→        state,
   211→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="resolve"),
   212→    )
   213→    if narrative.get("milestone"):
   214→        print(colorize(f"  → {narrative['milestone']}", "green"))
   215→
   216→    next_command = _print_next_command(state)
   217→    _write_resolve_query_entry(
   218→        ResolveQueryContext(
   219→            patterns=args.patterns,
   220→            status=args.status,
   221→            resolved=all_resolved,
   222→            next_command=next_command,
   223→            prev_overall=prev.overall,
   224→            prev_objective=prev.objective,
   225→            prev_strict=prev.strict,
   226→            prev_verified=prev.verified,
   227→            attestation=attestation,
   228→            narrative=narrative,
   229→            state=state,
   230→        )
   231→    )
   232→
   233→    if args.status == "fixed" and plan is not None and next_command == "desloppify next":
   234→        if mid_cluster:
   235→            print_user_message(
   236→                f"Hey — task done, {cluster_ctx.cluster_remaining} left in"
   237→                f" cluster '{cluster_ctx.cluster_name}'. Run `desloppify next`"
   238→                " to pick up the next one. Keep going — don't stop until the"
   239→                " cluster is finished."
   240→            )
   241→        elif cluster_ctx.cluster_completed and cluster_ctx.cluster_name:
   242→            print_user_message(
   243→                f"Hey — nice, you just finished cluster '{cluster_ctx.cluster_name}'."
   244→                " Check the commit tracking above — this is a good time to"
   245→                " commit and push your work. Then run `desloppify next` to"
   246→                " pick up your next task."
   247→            )
   248→        else:
   249→            print_user_message(
   250→                "Hey — nice, on to the next one. Run `desloppify next`"
   251→                " to pick up your next task. Before moving on, glance at the"
   252→                " commit tracking above — if there's a decent chunk of"
   253→                " uncommitted work, consider whether now's a good time"
   254→                " to commit and push. Otherwise just keep going."
   255→            )
   256→
   257→
   258→__all__ = ["_check_queue_order_guard", "cmd_resolve"]
   259→
```

```
1→"""Queue-order enforcement helpers for resolve command flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
     8→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     9→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.context import queue_context
    10→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import (
    11→    QueueBuildOptions,
    12→    build_work_queue,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order import collapse_clusters
    15→from desloppify.engine.plan import has_living_plan, load_plan
    16→
    17→_logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    18→
    19→
    20→def _front_item_ids(front_item: dict) -> tuple[str, set[str]]:
    21→    front_id = front_item["id"]
    22→    front_ids = {front_id}
    23→    if front_item.get("kind") != "cluster":
    24→        return front_id, front_ids
    25→    for member in front_item.get("members", []):
    26→        front_ids.add(member["id"])
    27→    return front_id, front_ids
    28→
    29→
    30→def _resolve_target_ids(patterns: list[str], clusters: dict) -> set[str]:
    31→    resolved_ids: set[str] = set()
    32→    for pattern in patterns:
    33→        if pattern in clusters:
    34→            resolved_ids.update(clusters[pattern].get("issue_ids", []))
    35→            resolved_ids.add(pattern)
    36→            continue
    37→        resolved_ids.add(pattern)
    38→    return resolved_ids
    39→
    40→
    41→def _filter_open_or_cluster_targets(
    42→    resolved_ids: set[str],
    43→    *,
    44→    clusters: dict,
    45→    issues: dict,
    46→) -> set[str]:
    47→    return {
    48→        issue_id
    49→        for issue_id in resolved_ids
    50→        if issue_id in clusters
    51→        or (issue_id in issues and issues[issue_id].get("status") == "open")
    52→    }
    53→
    54→
    55→def _prune_front_covered_clusters(
    56→    out_of_order: set[str],
    57→    *,
    58→    clusters: dict,
    59→    issues: dict,
    60→    front_ids: set[str],
    61→) -> None:
    62→    for cluster_id in list(out_of_order):
    63→        if cluster_id not in clusters:
    64→            continue
    65→        alive_members = {
    66→            issue_id
    67→            for issue_id in clusters[cluster_id].get("issue_ids", [])
    68→            if issue_id in issues and issues[issue_id].get("status") == "open"
    69→        }
    70→        if alive_members and alive_members <= front_ids:
    71→            out_of_order.discard(cluster_id)
    72→
    73→
    74→def _print_queue_order_violation(front_id: str, out_of_order: set[str]) -> None:
    75→    print(colorize("\n  Queue order violation: these items are not next in the plan queue:\n", "yellow"))
    76→    for issue_id in sorted(out_of_order):
    77→        print(f"    {issue_id}")
    78→    print(colorize(f"\n  The current next item is: {front_id}", "dim"))
    79→    print(colorize("  Items must be resolved in plan order. If you need to reprioritize:", "dim"))
    80→    print(colorize("    desloppify plan reorder <pattern> top            # move to front", "dim"))
    81→    print(colorize("    desloppify plan skip <pattern> --reason '...'    # skip for now", "dim"))
    82→    print(colorize("    desloppify next                                  # see what's next\n", "dim"))
    83→
    84→
    85→def _check_queue_order_guard(
    86→    state: dict,
    87→    patterns: list[str],
    88→    status: str,
    89→) -> bool:
    90→    """Warn and block if resolving items not at the front of the plan queue."""
    91→    if status != "fixed":
    92→        return False
    93→    try:
    94→        if not has_living_plan():
    95→            return False
    96→        plan = load_plan()
    97→        queue_order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    98→        if not queue_order:
    99→            return False
   100→
   101→        ctx = queue_context(state, plan=plan)
   102→        result = build_work_queue(
   103→            state,
   104→            options=QueueBuildOptions(
   105→                count=None,
   106→                include_subjective=True,
   107→                context=ctx,
   108→            ),
   109→        )
   110→        items = result["items"]
   111→        if not plan.get("active_cluster"):
   112→            items = collapse_clusters(items, plan)
   113→        if not items:
   114→            return False
   115→
   116→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   117→        issues = state.get("issues", {})
   118→        resolved_ids = _resolve_target_ids(patterns, clusters)
   119→        resolved_ids = _filter_open_or_cluster_targets(
   120→            resolved_ids,
   121→            clusters=clusters,
   122→            issues=issues,
   123→        )
   124→        if not resolved_ids:
   125→            return False
   126→
   127→        # Collect IDs from the first N queue positions where N is the
   128→        # number of items being resolved.  This allows batch-resolving
   129→        # a contiguous prefix (e.g. the first 9 items in one command).
   130→        prefix_depth = max(len(resolved_ids), 1)
   131→        front_ids: set[str] = set()
   132→        front_id = items[0]["id"]
   133→        for item in items[:prefix_depth]:
   134→            _, item_ids = _front_item_ids(item)
   135→            front_ids.update(item_ids)
   136→
   137→        out_of_order = resolved_ids - front_ids
   138→        _prune_front_covered_clusters(
   139→            out_of_order,
   140→            clusters=clusters,
   141→            issues=issues,
   142→            front_ids=front_ids,
   143→        )
   144→        if not out_of_order:
   145→            return False
   146→
   147→        _print_queue_order_violation(front_id, out_of_order)
   148→        return True
   149→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   150→        _logger.debug("queue order guard skipped", exc_info=True)
   151→        return False
   152→
   153→
   154→__all__ = ["_check_queue_order_guard"]
   155→
```

```
     2→

     7→
     8→def build_score_checkpoint_item(plan: dict, state: dict) -> WorkQueueItem | None:
     9→    """Build a synthetic work item for ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` if queued."""
    10→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID
    11→
    12→    if WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
    13→        return None
    14→
    15→    from desloppify import state as state_mod
    16→
    17→    snapshot = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    18→    strict = snapshot.strict if snapshot.strict is not None else 0.0
    19→    plan_start = (plan.get("plan_start_scores") or {}).get("strict")
    20→    delta = round(strict - plan_start, 1) if plan_start is not None else None
    21→    delta_str = f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})" if delta else ""
    22→
    23→    return {
    24→        "id": WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID,
    25→        "tier": 1,
    26→        "confidence": "high",

   101→
   102→    from desloppify import state as state_mod
   103→
   104→    snapshot = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   105→    strict = snapshot.strict if snapshot.strict is not None else 0.0
   106→    plan_start = (plan.get("plan_start_scores") or {}).get("strict")
   107→    delta = round(strict - plan_start, 1) if plan_start is not None else None
   108→    delta_str = f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})" if delta else ""
   109→
   110→    return {
   111→        "id": WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID,
   112→        "tier": 1,
   113→        "confidence": "high",
   114→        "detector": "workflow",
   115→        "file": ".",
   116→        "kind": "workflow_action",
   117→        "summary": f"Communicate updated score to user: strict {strict:.1f}/100{delta_str}",
   118→        "detail": {
   119→            "strict": strict,
   120→            "plan_start_strict": plan_start,
   121→            "delta": delta,
   122→        },
   123→        "primary_command": (
   124→            f'desloppify plan resolve "{WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID}" '
   125→            '--note "Score communicated" --confirm'
   126→        ),
   127→        "blocked_by": [],
   128→        "is_blocked": False,
   129→    }
   130→
   131→
   132→__all__ = [
   133→    "build_communicate_score_item",
   134→    "build_create_plan_item",
   135→    "build_import_scores_item",
   136→    "build_score_checkpoint_item",
   137→]
   138→
```

```
1→"""Sync subjective dimensions into the plan queue.
     2→
     3→Two independent sync functions:
     4→
     5→- **sync_unscored_dimensions** — prepend never-scored (placeholder) dimensions
     6→  to the *front* of the queue unconditionally (onboarding priority).
     7→- **sync_stale_dimensions** — append stale (previously-scored) dimensions to
     8→  the *back* of the queue when no objective items remain.
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    13→
    14→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
    15→from desloppify.engine._plan import stale_policy as stale_policy_mod
    16→from desloppify.engine._plan.promoted_ids import promoted_insertion_index
    17→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    18→from desloppify.engine._plan.subjective_policy import (
    19→    NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS as _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS,
    20→    SubjectiveVisibility,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    23→
    24→SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX = "subjective::"
    25→TRIAGE_ID = "triage::pending"  # deprecated, kept for migration
    26→
    27→TRIAGE_PREFIX = "triage::"
    28→TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS = (
    29→    "triage::observe",
    30→    "triage::reflect",
    31→    "triage::organize",
    32→    "triage::commit",
    33→)
    34→TRIAGE_IDS = set(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS)
    35→WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID = "workflow::create-plan"
    36→WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID = "workflow::score-checkpoint"
    37→WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID = "workflow::import-scores"
    38→WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID = "workflow::communicate-score"
    39→WORKFLOW_PREFIX = "workflow::"
    40→SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES = ("triage::", "workflow::", "subjective::")
    41→
    42→
    43→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    44→# Result dataclasses
    45→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    46→
    47→@dataclass
    48→class StaleDimensionSyncResult:
    49→    """What changed during a stale-dimension sync."""
    50→
    51→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    52→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    53→
    54→    @property
    55→    def changes(self) -> int:
    56→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    57→
    58→
    59→@dataclass
    60→class UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
    61→    """What changed during an unscored-dimension sync."""
    62→
    63→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    64→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    65→
    66→    @property
    67→    def changes(self) -> int:
    68→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    69→
    70→
    71→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    72→# ID helpers
    73→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    74→
    75→def _current_stale_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    76→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently stale."""
    77→    return stale_policy_mod.current_stale_ids(
    78→        state,
    79→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    80→    )
    81→
    82→
    83→def current_unscored_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    84→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored (placeholder).
    85→
    86→    Checks ``subjective_assessments`` first; when that dict is empty
    87→    (common before any reviews have been run), falls through to
    88→    ``dimension_scores`` which carries placeholder metadata from scan.
    89→    """
    90→    return stale_policy_mod.current_unscored_ids(
    91→        state,
    92→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    93→    )
    94→
    95→
    96→def current_under_target_ids(
    97→    state: StateModel,
    98→    *,
    99→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
   100→) -> set[str]:
   101→    """Return ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are under target but not stale or unscored.
   102→
   103→    These are dimensions whose assessment is still current (not needing refresh)
   104→    but whose score hasn't reached the target yet.
   105→    """
   106→    return stale_policy_mod.current_under_target_ids(
   107→        state,
   108→        target_strict=target_strict,
   109→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
   110→    )
   111→
   112→
   113→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   114→# Helpers for sync_stale_dimensions / sync_unscored_dimensions
   115→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   116→
   117→def _has_objective_backlog(
   118→    state: StateModel,
   119→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
   120→) -> bool:
   121→    """Return whether an objective backlog exists (open non-subjective issues)."""
   122→    if policy is not None:
   123→        return policy.has_objective_backlog
   124→    return any(
   125→        f.get("status") == "open"
   126→        and f.get("detector") not in _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   127→        and not f.get("suppressed")
   128→        for f in state.get("issues", {}).values()
   129→    )
   130→
   131→
   132→def _prune_subjective_ids(
   133→    order: list[str],
   134→    *,
   135→    keep_ids: set[str],
   136→    pruned: list[str],
   137→) -> None:
   138→    """Remove subjective IDs from *order* that are not in *keep_ids*, appending removed to *pruned*."""
   139→    to_remove = [
   140→        fid for fid in order
   141→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   142→        and fid not in keep_ids
   143→    ]
   144→    for fid in to_remove:
   145→        order.remove(fid)
   146→        pruned.append(fid)
   147→
   148→
   149→def _inject_subjective_ids(
   150→    order: list[str],
   151→    plan: PlanModel,
   152→    *,
   153→    inject_ids: set[str],
   154→    to_front: bool,
   155→    injected: list[str],
   156→) -> None:
   157→    """Inject subjective IDs into *order* if not already present.
   158→
   159→    When *to_front* is True, inserts after promoted items (front of queue).
   160→    Otherwise appends to the back.
   161→    """
   162→    existing = set(order)
   163→    if to_front:
   164→        insert_at = promoted_insertion_index(order, plan)
   165→        for sid in reversed(sorted(inject_ids)):
   166→            if sid not in existing:
   167→                order.insert(insert_at, sid)
   168→                injected.append(sid)
   169→    else:
   170→        for sid in sorted(inject_ids):
   171→            if sid not in existing:
   172→                order.append(sid)
   173→                injected.append(sid)
   174→
   175→
   176→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   177→# Helpers for sync_triage_needed
   178→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   179→
   180→def _open_review_issue_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   181→    """Return set of open issue IDs whose detector is 'review' or 'concerns'."""
   182→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   183→    return {
   184→        fid for fid, f in issues.items()
   185→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   186→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   187→    }
   188→
   189→
   190→def _new_review_ids_since_triage(
   191→    state: StateModel,
   192→    meta: dict,
   193→) -> set[str]:
   194→    """Return review issue IDs that are new since the last triage."""
   195→    current_review_ids = _open_review_issue_ids(state)
   196→    triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
   197→    return current_review_ids - triaged_ids
   198→
   199→
   200→def _prune_all_triage_stages(order: list[str]) -> None:
   201→    """Remove all ``triage::*`` stage IDs from *order*."""
   202→    for sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:
   203→        while sid in order:
   204→            order.remove(sid)
   205→
   206→
   207→def _inject_pending_triage_stages(
   208→    order: list[str],
   209→    plan: PlanModel,
   210→    confirmed: set[str],
   211→) -> bool:
   212→    """Inject triage stages for pending (unconfirmed) items.
   213→
   214→    Returns True if any stages were injected.
   215→    """
   216→    insert_at = promoted_insertion_index(order, plan)
   217→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "commit")
   218→    existing = set(order)
   219→    injected_count = 0
   220→    for sid, name in zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names, strict=False):
   221→        if name not in confirmed and sid not in existing:
   222→            order.insert(insert_at + injected_count, sid)
   223→            injected_count += 1
   224→    return injected_count > 0
   225→
   226→
   227→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   228→# Unscored dimension sync (front of queue, unconditional)
   229→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   230→
   231→def sync_unscored_dimensions(
   232→    plan: PlanModel,
   233→    state: StateModel,
   234→) -> UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
   235→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions.
   236→
   237→    1. **Prune** — remove ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are
   238→       no longer unscored AND not stale (avoids pruning stale IDs — that is
   239→       ``sync_stale_dimensions``' responsibility).
   240→    2. **Inject** — unconditionally prepend currently-unscored IDs to the
   241→       *front* of ``queue_order`` so initial reviews are the first priority.
   242→    """
   243→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   244→    result = UnscoredDimensionSyncResult()
   245→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   246→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   247→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   248→
   249→    # --- Cleanup: prune subjective IDs that are no longer unscored --------
   250→    # Only prune IDs that are neither unscored nor stale (stale sync owns those).
   251→    _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=unscored_ids | stale_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   252→
   253→    # --- Inject: prepend unscored IDs after any promoted items -------------
   254→    _inject_subjective_ids(order, plan, inject_ids=unscored_ids, to_front=True, injected=result.injected)
   255→
   256→    return result
   257→
   258→
   259→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   260→# Stale dimension sync (back of queue, conditional)
   261→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   262→
   263→def sync_stale_dimensions(
   264→    plan: PlanModel,
   265→    state: StateModel,
   266→    *,
   267→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   268→    cycle_just_completed: bool = False,
   269→) -> StaleDimensionSyncResult:
   270→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with stale and under-target subjective dimensions.
   271→
   272→    1. Remove any ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are no
   273→       longer stale/under-target and not unscored (avoids pruning IDs owned
   274→       by ``sync_unscored_dimensions``).
   275→    2. Inject stale and under-target dimension IDs when either:
   276→       a. No objective items remain (mid-cycle: append to back), OR
   277→       b. A cycle just completed (post-cycle: insert at front so subjective
   278→          review takes priority over new objective issues).
   279→    """
   280→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   281→    result = StaleDimensionSyncResult()
   282→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   283→    under_target_ids = current_under_target_ids(state)
   284→    injectable_ids = stale_ids | under_target_ids
   285→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   286→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   287→
   288→    # --- Cleanup: prune resolved subjective IDs --------------------------
   289→    # Only prune IDs that are no longer injectable and not unscored.
   290→    _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=injectable_ids | unscored_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   291→
   292→    # --- Inject or evict stale + under-target dimensions -----------------
   293→    has_real_items = _has_objective_backlog(state, policy)
   294→    should_inject = not has_real_items or cycle_just_completed
   295→
   296→    if not should_inject:
   297→        # Mid-cycle with objective backlog: evict any stale/under-target IDs
   298→        # that are present in the queue.  They may have been grandfathered from
   299→        # the unscored phase and should not be visible until the objective
   300→        # backlog clears or a cycle completes.
   301→        to_evict = [
   302→            fid for fid in order
   303→            if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   304→            and fid in injectable_ids
   305→        ]
   306→        for fid in to_evict:
   307→            order.remove(fid)
   308→            result.pruned.append(fid)
   309→
   310→    if should_inject and injectable_ids:
   311→        to_front = cycle_just_completed and has_real_items
   312→        _inject_subjective_ids(order, plan, inject_ids=injectable_ids, to_front=to_front, injected=result.injected)
   313→
   314→    return result
   315→
   316→
   317→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   318→# Triage snapshot hash + sync
   319→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   320→
   321→def review_issue_snapshot_hash(state: StateModel) -> str:
   322→    """Hash open review issue IDs to detect changes.
   323→
   324→    Returns empty string when there are no open review issues.
   325→    """
   326→    return stale_policy_mod.review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   327→
   328→
   329→@dataclass
   330→class TriageSyncResult:
   331→    """What changed during a triage sync."""
   332→
   333→    injected: bool = False
   334→    pruned: bool = False
   335→
   336→    @property
   337→    def changes(self) -> int:
   338→        return int(self.injected) + int(self.pruned)
   339→
   340→
   341→def sync_triage_needed(
   342→    plan: PlanModel,
   343→    state: StateModel,
   344→) -> TriageSyncResult:
   345→    """Inject 4 triage stage IDs at front of queue when review issues change.
   346→
   347→    Only injects stages not already confirmed in ``epic_triage_meta``.
   348→
   349→    When stages are already present but all new issues have been resolved
   350→    since injection, auto-prunes the stale stages and updates the hash.
   351→
   352→    When issues are *resolved* (current IDs are a subset of previously
   353→    triaged IDs), the snapshot hash is updated silently — no re-triage
   354→    is needed since the user is working through the plan.
   355→    """
   356→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   357→    result = TriageSyncResult()
   358→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   359→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   360→    confirmed = set(meta.get("triage_stages", {}).keys())
   361→
   362→    # Check if any triage stage is already in queue
   363→    already_present = any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS)
   364→
   365→    current_hash = review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   366→    last_hash = meta.get("issue_snapshot_hash", "")
   367→
   368→    if already_present:
   369→        # Stages present — check if the reason for injection still applies.
   370→        # Only auto-prune when triage was completed before (hash exists),
   371→        # all new issues have been resolved, and no triage work is in
   372→        # progress.  This avoids pruning the initial triage or a
   373→        # user-started triage session.
   374→        if last_hash and not confirmed:
   375→            new_since_triage = _new_review_ids_since_triage(state, meta)
   376→
   377→            if not new_since_triage:
   378→                # No new issues remain — prune stale stages
   379→                _prune_all_triage_stages(order)
   380→                if current_hash:
   381→                    meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   382→                    plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   383→                result.pruned = True
   384→        return result
   385→
   386→    if current_hash and current_hash != last_hash:
   387→        # Distinguish "new issues appeared" from "issues were resolved".
   388→        # Only re-triage when genuinely new issues exist.
   389→        new_since_triage = _new_review_ids_since_triage(state, meta)
   390→
   391→        if new_since_triage:
   392→            # New review issues appeared — re-triage needed
   393→            result.injected = _inject_pending_triage_stages(order, plan, confirmed)
   394→        else:
   395→            # Only resolved issues changed the hash — update silently
   396→            meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   397→            plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   398→
   399→    return result
   400→
   401→
   402→@dataclass
   403→class ScoreCheckpointSyncResult:
   404→    """What changed during a score-checkpoint sync."""
   405→
   406→    injected: bool = False
   407→
   408→    @property
   409→    def changes(self) -> int:
   410→        return int(self.injected)
   411→
   412→
   413→def sync_score_checkpoint_needed(
   414→    plan: PlanModel,
   415→    state: StateModel,
   416→    *,
   417→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   418→) -> ScoreCheckpointSyncResult:
   419→    """Inject ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` when all initial reviews complete.
   420→
   421→    Injects when:
   422→    - No unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain
   423→    - ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` is not already in the queue
   424→
   425→    Positioned after subjective items but before triage/create-plan
   426→    so the user sees their updated strict score right after reviews finish.
   427→    """
   428→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   429→    result = ScoreCheckpointSyncResult()
   430→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   431→
   432→    if WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID in order:
   433→        return result
   434→
   435→    # Check that no unscored dimensions remain
   436→    if policy is not None:
   437→        if policy.unscored_ids:
   438→            return result
   439→    else:
   440→        unscored = current_unscored_ids(state)
   441→        if unscored:
   442→            return result
   443→
   444→    # Insert after any subjective items, before triage/workflow/issues
   445→    insert_at = 0
   446→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   447→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX):
   448→            insert_at = i + 1
   449→    order.insert(insert_at, WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID)
   450→    result.injected = True
   451→    return result
   452→
   453→
   454→@dataclass
   455→class CreatePlanSyncResult:
   456→    """What changed during a create-plan sync."""
   457→
   458→    injected: bool = False
   459→
   460→    @property
   461→    def changes(self) -> int:
   462→        return int(self.injected)
   463→
   464→
   465→def sync_create_plan_needed(
   466→    plan: PlanModel,
   467→    state: StateModel,
   468→    *,
   469→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   470→) -> CreatePlanSyncResult:
   471→    """Inject ``workflow::create-plan`` when reviews complete + objective backlog exists.
   472→
   473→    Only injects when:
   474→    - No unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain
   475→    - At least one objective issue exists
   476→    - ``workflow::create-plan`` is not already in the queue
   477→    - No triage stages are pending
   478→    """
   479→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   480→    result = CreatePlanSyncResult()
   481→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   482→
   483→    if WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID in order:
   484→        return result
   485→
   486→    # Don't inject if triage stages are pending
   487→    if any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS):
   488→        return result
   489→
   490→    # Check that no unscored dimensions remain
   491→    if policy is not None:
   492→        if policy.unscored_ids:
   493→            return result
   494→        has_objective = policy.has_objective_backlog
   495→    else:
   496→        unscored = current_unscored_ids(state)
   497→        if unscored:
   498→            return result
   499→        issues = state.get("issues", {})
   500→        has_objective = any(
   501→            f.get("status") == "open"
   502→            and f.get("detector") not in _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   503→            for f in issues.values()
   504→        )
   505→    if not has_objective:
   506→        return result
   507→
   508→    # Insert after any subjective/workflow items, at the end of the
   509→    # synthetic block (so create-plan comes after score-checkpoint).
   510→    insert_at = 0
   511→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   512→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) or fid.startswith(TRIAGE_PREFIX) or fid.startswith(WORKFLOW_PREFIX):
   513→            insert_at = i + 1
   514→    order.insert(insert_at, WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID)
   515→    result.injected = True
   516→    return result
   517→
   518→
   519→def compute_new_issue_ids(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   520→    """Return the set of open review/concerns issue IDs added since last triage.
   521→
   522→    Returns an empty set when no prior triage has recorded ``triaged_ids``.
   523→    """
   524→    return stale_policy_mod.compute_new_issue_ids(plan, state)
   525→
   526→
   527→def is_triage_stale(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> bool:
   528→    """Side-effect-free check: is triage needed?
   529→
   530→    Returns True when genuinely *new* review issues appeared since the
   531→    last triage.  Triage stage IDs being in the queue alone is not
   532→    sufficient — the new issues that triggered injection may have been
   533→    resolved since then.
   534→
   535→    When issues are merely resolved (current IDs are a subset of
   536→    previously triaged IDs), triage is NOT stale — the user is working
   537→    through the plan.
   538→    """
   539→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   540→    return stale_policy_mod.is_triage_stale(plan, state, triage_ids=TRIAGE_IDS)
   541→

   543→@dataclass
   544→class ImportScoresSyncResult:
   545→    """What changed during an import-scores sync."""
   546→
   547→    injected: bool = False
   548→
   549→    @property
   550→    def changes(self) -> int:
   551→        return int(self.injected)

   553→
   554→def sync_import_scores_needed(
   555→    plan: PlanModel,
   556→    state: StateModel,
   557→    *,
   558→    assessment_mode: str | None = None,
   559→) -> ImportScoresSyncResult:
   560→    """Inject ``workflow::import-scores`` after issues-only import.
   561→
   562→    Only injects when:
   563→    - Assessment mode was ``issues_only`` (scores were skipped)
   564→    - ``workflow::import-scores`` is not already in the queue
   565→    - There are assessments in the payload that could be imported
   566→
   567→    Positioned after score-checkpoint, before create-plan.
   568→    """
   569→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   570→    result = ImportScoresSyncResult()
   571→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   572→
   573→    if WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID in order:
   574→        return result
   575→
   576→    # Only inject when scores were skipped (issues-only mode)
   577→    if assessment_mode != "issues_only":
   578→        return result
   579→
   580→    # Insert after any subjective/workflow items
   581→    insert_at = 0
   582→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   583→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) or fid.startswith(WORKFLOW_PREFIX):
   584→            insert_at = i + 1
   585→    order.insert(insert_at, WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID)
   586→    result.injected = True
   587→    return result
   588→
   589→
   590→@dataclass
   591→class CommunicateScoreSyncResult:
   592→    """What changed during a communicate-score sync."""
   593→
   594→    injected: bool = False
   595→
   596→    @property
   597→    def changes(self) -> int:
   598→        return int(self.injected)
   599→
   600→
   601→def sync_communicate_score_needed(
   602→    plan: PlanModel,
   603→    state: StateModel,
   604→    *,
   605→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   606→    scores_just_imported: bool = False,
   607→) -> CommunicateScoreSyncResult:
   608→    """Inject ``workflow::communicate-score`` when scores should be shown.
   609→
   610→    Injects when either:
   611→    - All initial subjective reviews are complete (no unscored dimensions), OR
   612→    - Scores were just imported (trusted/attested/override)
   613→
   614→    And ``workflow::communicate-score`` is not already in the queue.
   615→    Positioned after subjective items but before triage/create-plan.
   616→    """
   617→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   618→    result = CommunicateScoreSyncResult()
   619→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   620→
   621→    # Also treat legacy score-checkpoint as already-present
   622→    if WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID in order or WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID in order:
   623→        return result
   624→
   625→    # Trigger 1: scores just imported
   626→    should_inject = scores_just_imported
   627→
   628→    # Trigger 2: all initial reviews complete (no unscored dimensions)
   629→    if not should_inject:
   630→        if policy is not None:
   631→            should_inject = not policy.unscored_ids
   632→        else:
   633→            should_inject = not current_unscored_ids(state)
   634→
   635→    if not should_inject:
   636→        return result
   637→
   638→    # Insert after any subjective items, before triage/workflow/issues
   639→    insert_at = 0
   640→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   641→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX):
   642→            insert_at = i + 1
   643→    order.insert(insert_at, WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID)
   644→    result.injected = True
   645→    return result
   646→
   647→
   648→__all__ = [
   649→    "SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX",
   650→    "TRIAGE_ID",
   651→    "TRIAGE_IDS",
   652→    "TRIAGE_PREFIX",
   653→    "TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS",
```

```
    49→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    50→class QueueBuildOptions:
    51→    """Configuration for queue construction.
    52→
    53→    ``scan_path`` defaults to reading from ``state["scan_path"]`` so callers
    54→    don't need to thread it manually.  Pass an explicit ``str`` or ``None``
    55→    to override (``None`` disables scope filtering).
    56→    """
    57→
    58→    # Output control
    59→    count: int | None = 1
    60→    explain: bool = False
    61→
    62→    # Scope filtering
    63→    scan_path: str | None | object = _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE
    64→    scope: str | None = None
    65→    status: str = "open"
    66→    chronic: bool = False
    67→
    68→    # Subjective gating
    69→    include_subjective: bool = True
    70→    subjective_threshold: float = 100.0
    71→
    72→    # Plan integration
    73→    plan: dict | None = None
    74→    include_skipped: bool = False
    75→    cluster: str | None = None
    76→
    77→    # Pre-computed context (overrides plan)
    78→    context: QueueContext | None = None
    79→
    80→
    81→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    82→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    83→
    84→    items: list[WorkQueueItem]
    85→    total: int
    86→    grouped: dict[str, list[WorkQueueItem]]
    87→    new_ids: set[str]
    88→
    89→
    90→def build_work_queue(
    91→    state: StateModel,
    92→    *,
    93→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    94→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    95→    """Build a ranked work queue from state issues.
    96→
    97→    Pipeline:
    98→    1. Gather    — issue items, subjective dimensions, workflow stages
    99→    2. Score     — estimate impact from dimension headroom, apply floor
   100→    3. Presort   — stamp plan positions, separate skipped items
   101→    4. Lifecycle — filter endgame-only items when objective work remains
   102→    5. Sort      — rank by impact/confidence, apply plan order
   103→    6. Limit     — truncate to count, optionally add explain metadata
   104→    """
   105→    opts = options or QueueBuildOptions()
   106→    plan, scan_path, status, threshold = _resolve_inputs(opts, state)
   107→
   108→    # 1. Gather
   109→    items = build_issue_items(
   110→        state, scan_path=scan_path, status_filter=status,
   111→        scope=opts.scope, chronic=opts.chronic,
   112→    )
   113→    items += _gather_subjective_items(state, opts, threshold)
   114→    items += _gather_workflow_items(state, plan, status)
   115→
   116→    # 2. Score & filter
   117→    enrich_with_impact(items, state.get("dimension_scores", {}))
   118→    items = [i for i in items if _passes_impact_floor(i)]
   119→
   120→    # 3. Plan-aware ordering (part 1: separate skipped items)
   121→    new_ids, skipped = _plan_presort(items, state, plan)
   122→
   123→    # 4. Lifecycle filter — endgame-only items filtered when objective work remains
   124→    items = _apply_lifecycle_filter(items)
   125→
   126→    # 5. Sort & plan post-processing
   127→    items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   128→    _plan_postsort(items, skipped, plan, opts)
   129→
   130→    # 6. Finalize
   131→    if not items:
   132→        items += _empty_queue_fallback(plan)
   133→    total = len(items)
   134→    if opts.count is not None and opts.count > 0:
   135→        items = items[:opts.count]
   136→    if opts.explain:
   137→        for item in items:
   138→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   139→
   140→    return {
   141→        "items": items,
   142→        "total": total,
   143→        "grouped": group_queue_items(items, "item"),
   144→        "new_ids": new_ids,
   145→    }
   146→
   147→
   148→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   149→# Pipeline helpers (private to this module)
   150→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
```

```
    46→def cmd_plan_output(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    47→    """Generate a prioritized markdown plan from state."""
    48→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    49→    state = runtime.state

    51→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    52→        return

    59→    next_command = "desloppify next --count 20"
    60→
    61→    output = getattr(args, "output", None)
    62→    if output:
    63→        try:
    64→            from pathlib import Path
    65→            output_path = Path(output)
    66→            if output_path.exists():
    67→                print(colorize(
    68→                    f"  {output} already exists — refusing to overwrite.",
    69→                    "red",
    70→                ))
    71→                print(colorize(

    73→                    f"or choose a different filename.",

    76→                return
```
