```
   210→def cmd_plan_skip(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   211→    """Skip issues — unified command for temporary/permanent/false-positive."""
   212→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   213→    state = runtime.state
   214→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   215→        return
   216→
   217→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   218→    reason: str | None = getattr(args, "reason", None)
   219→    review_after: int | None = getattr(args, "review_after", None)
   220→    permanent: bool = getattr(args, "permanent", False)
   221→    false_positive: bool = getattr(args, "false_positive", False)
   222→    note: str | None = getattr(args, "note", None)
   223→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attest", None)
   224→
   225→    kind = skip_kind_from_flags(permanent=permanent, false_positive=false_positive)
   226→    if not _validate_skip_requirements(
   227→        kind=kind,
   228→        attestation=attestation,
   229→        note=note,
   230→    ):
   231→        return
   232→
   233→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   234→    plan_file = _plan_file_for_state(state_file)
   235→    plan = load_plan(plan_file)
   236→    issue_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
   237→    if not issue_ids:
   238→        print(colorize("  No matching issues found.", "yellow"))
   239→        return
   240→
   241→    # For permanent/false_positive: delegate to state layer for score impact
   242→    state_data = _apply_state_skip_resolution(
   243→        kind=kind,
   244→        state_file=state_file,
   245→        issue_ids=issue_ids,
   246→        note=note,
   247→        attestation=attestation,
   248→    )
   249→
   250→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   251→    count = skip_items(
   252→        plan,
   253→        issue_ids,
   254→        kind=kind,
   255→        reason=reason,
   256→        note=note,
   257→        attestation=attestation,
   258→        review_after=review_after,
   259→        scan_count=scan_count,
   260→    )
   261→
   262→    # Log the skip action
   263→    append_log_entry(
   264→        plan,
   265→        "skip",
   266→        issue_ids=issue_ids,
   267→        actor="user",
   268→        note=note,
   269→        detail={"kind": kind, "reason": reason},
   270→    )
   271→    if state_data is not None:
   272→        _save_plan_state_transactional(
   273→            plan=plan,
   274→            plan_path=plan_file,
   275→            state_data=state_data,
   276→            state_path_value=state_file,
   277→        )
   278→    else:
   279→        save_plan(plan, plan_file)
   280→
   281→    print(colorize(f"  {SKIP_KIND_LABELS[kind]} {count} item(s).", "green"))
   282→    if review_after:
   283→        print(colorize(f"  Will re-surface after {review_after} scan(s).", "dim"))
   284→    print_user_message(
   285→        "Hey — if skipping was the right call, just continue with"
   286→        " what you were doing. If you think a broader re-triage is"
   287→        " needed, use `desloppify plan triage`. Run `desloppify"
   288→        " plan --help` to see all available plan tools. Otherwise"
   289→        " no need to reply, just keep going."
   290→    )
   291→
   292→
   293→def cmd_plan_unskip(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   294→    """Unskip issues — bring back to queue."""
   295→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   296→    state = runtime.state
   297→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   298→        return
   299→
   300→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   301→
   302→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   303→    plan_file = _plan_file_for_state(state_file)
   304→    plan = load_plan(plan_file)
   305→    # For unskip we need to match against all statuses (skipped items may be wontfix/fp)
   306→    issue_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan, status_filter="all")
   307→    if not issue_ids:
   308→        print(colorize("  No matching issues found.", "yellow"))
   309→        return
   310→
   311→    count, need_reopen = unskip_items(plan, issue_ids)
   312→    append_log_entry(
   313→        plan, "unskip", issue_ids=issue_ids, actor="user",
   314→        detail={"need_reopen": need_reopen},
   315→    )
   316→
   317→    # Reopen permanent/false_positive items in state
   318→    reopened: list[str] = []
   319→    if need_reopen:
   320→        state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   321→        for fid in need_reopen:
   322→            reopened.extend(state_mod.resolve_issues(state_data, fid, "open"))
   323→        _save_plan_state_transactional(
   324→            plan=plan,
   325→            plan_path=plan_file,
   326→            state_data=state_data,
   327→            state_path_value=state_file,
   328→        )
   329→        print(colorize(f"  Reopened {len(reopened)} issue(s) in state.", "dim"))
   330→    else:
   331→        save_plan(plan, plan_file)
   332→
   333→    print(colorize(f"  Unskipped {count} item(s) — back in queue.", "green"))
   334→
   335→
   336→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   337→# Reopen
   338→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   339→
   340→def cmd_plan_reopen(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   341→    """Reopen resolved issues from plan context."""
   342→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   343→
   344→    raw_state_path = state_path(args)
   345→    state_file = raw_state_path if isinstance(raw_state_path, Path) else Path(raw_state_path) if raw_state_path else None
   346→    state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   347→    plan_file = _plan_file_for_state(state_file)
   348→
   349→    reopened: list[str] = []
   350→    for pattern in patterns:
   351→        reopened.extend(
   352→            state_mod.resolve_issues(state_data, pattern, "open")
   353→        )
   354→
   355→    if not reopened:
   356→        print(colorize("  No resolved issues matching: " + " ".join(patterns), "yellow"))
   357→        return
   358→
   359→    # Remove from skipped if present, and ensure all reopened IDs are in queue
   360→    plan = load_plan(plan_file)
   361→
   362→    # Remove from commit tracking uncommitted list
   363→    purge_uncommitted_ids(plan, reopened)
   364→
   365→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   366→    count = 0
   367→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   368→    for fid in reopened:
   369→        if fid in skipped:
   370→            skipped.pop(fid)
   371→            count += 1
   372→        if fid not in order:
   373→            plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   374→            order.add(fid)
   375→            count += 1
   376→    append_log_entry(
   377→        plan, "reopen", issue_ids=reopened, actor="user",
   378→    )
   379→    _save_plan_state_transactional(
   380→        plan=plan,
   381→        plan_path=plan_file,
   382→        state_data=state_data,
   383→        state_path_value=state_file,
   384→    )
   385→
   386→    print(colorize(f"  Reopened {len(reopened)} issue(s).", "green"))
   387→    if count:
   388→        print(colorize("  Plan updated: items moved back to queue.", "dim"))
   389→
   390→
   391→_CLUSTER_INDIVIDUAL_THRESHOLD = 10
   392→
   393→
   394→def _check_cluster_guard(patterns: list[str], plan: dict, state: dict) -> bool:
   395→    """Return True if blocked by cluster guard, False if OK to proceed."""
   396→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   397→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   398→    for pattern in patterns:
   399→        if pattern in clusters:
   400→            cluster = clusters[pattern]
   401→            # Filter to alive issues only — stale IDs should not count
   402→            ids = [
   403→                fid for fid in cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   404→                if fid in issues and issues[fid].get("status") == "open"
   405→            ]
   406→            if len(ids) == 0:
   407→                print(colorize(
   408→                    f"\n  Cluster '{pattern}' is empty — add items before marking it done.\n",
   409→                    "yellow",
   410→                ))
   411→                print(colorize(
   412→                    f"  Use: desloppify plan cluster add {pattern} <issue-id>",
   413→                    "dim",
   414→                ))
   415→                return True  # blocked
   416→            if len(ids) <= _CLUSTER_INDIVIDUAL_THRESHOLD:
   417→                _print_cluster_guard(pattern, ids, state)
   418→                return True  # blocked
   419→    return False  # OK
   420→
   421→
   422→def _print_cluster_guard(cluster_name: str, issue_ids: list[str], state: dict) -> None:
   423→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   424→    print(colorize(
   425→        f"\n  Cluster '{cluster_name}' has {len(issue_ids)} item(s) — mark them done individually first:\n",
   426→        "yellow",
   427→    ))
   428→    for fid in issue_ids:
   429→        f = issues.get(fid, {})
   430→        summary = f.get("summary", "(no summary)")[:80]
   431→        detector = f.get("detector", "?")
   432→        print(f"    {fid}  [{detector}]  {summary}")
   433→    print(colorize(
   434→        "\n  Use: desloppify resolve <id> --status fixed --note '...' --attest '...'",
   435→        "dim",
   436→    ))
   437→    print(colorize(
   438→        "  Or mark each resolved: desloppify plan resolve <id> --note '...' --confirm\n",
   439→        "dim",
   440→    ))
   441→
   442→
   443→def _is_synthetic_id(fid: str) -> bool:
   444→    """Return True if the ID is a synthetic workflow/triage item, not a real issue."""
   445→    return fid.startswith("triage::") or fid.startswith("workflow::") or fid.startswith("subjective::")
   446→
   447→
   448→def _resolve_synthetic_ids(patterns: list[str]) -> tuple[list[str], list[str]]:
   449→    """Separate synthetic IDs from real issue patterns.
   450→
   451→    Returns (synthetic_ids, remaining_patterns).
   452→    """
   453→    synthetic = [p for p in patterns if _is_synthetic_id(p)]
   454→    remaining = [p for p in patterns if not _is_synthetic_id(p)]
   455→    return synthetic, remaining
   456→
   457→
   458→def _blocked_triage_stages(plan: dict) -> dict[str, list[str]]:
   459→    """Return ``{stage_id: [blocked_by_ids]}`` for triage stages that can't run yet.
   460→
   461→    Uses the dependency graph and confirmed-stage metadata directly —
   462→    no state needed, no queue item construction.
   463→    """
   464→    order_set = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   465→    present = order_set & TRIAGE_IDS
   466→    if not present:
   467→        return {}
   468→
   469→    confirmed = set(plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {}).get("triage_stages", {}).keys())
   470→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "commit")
   471→
   472→    blocked: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
   473→    for sid, name in zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names, strict=False):
   474→        if sid not in present or name in confirmed:
   475→            continue
   476→        deps = TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES.get(name, set())
   477→        unmet = sorted(
   478→            f"triage::{dep}" for dep in deps
   479→            if f"triage::{dep}" in present and dep not in confirmed
   480→        )
   481→        if unmet:
   482→            blocked[sid] = unmet
   483→    return blocked
```

```
   153→def _resolve_inputs(
   154→    opts: QueueBuildOptions, state: StateModel,
   155→) -> tuple[dict | None, str | None, str, float]:
   156→    """Resolve plan, scan_path, status, and subjective threshold from options."""
   157→    ctx = opts.context
   158→    plan = ctx.plan if ctx is not None else opts.plan
   159→
   160→    scan_path: str | None = (
   161→        state.get("scan_path")
   162→        if opts.scan_path is _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE
   163→        else opts.scan_path  # type: ignore[assignment]
   164→    )
   165→
   166→    status = opts.status
   167→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
   168→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
   169→
   170→    try:
   171→        threshold = float(opts.subjective_threshold)
   172→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   173→        threshold = 100.0
   174→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, threshold))
   175→
   176→    return plan, scan_path, status, threshold
   177→
   178→
   179→def _gather_subjective_items(
   180→    state: StateModel,
   181→    opts: QueueBuildOptions,
   182→    threshold: float,
   183→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   184→    """Build subjective dimension candidates.
   185→
   186→    Lifecycle filtering (endgame gating) happens in _apply_lifecycle_filter,
   187→    not here. This function only handles configuration and scope.
   188→    """
   189→    if not opts.include_subjective:
   190→        return []
   191→    if opts.status not in {"open", "all"}:
   192→        return []
   193→    if opts.chronic:
   194→        return []
   195→
   196→    candidates = build_subjective_items(
   197→        state, state.get("issues", {}), threshold=threshold,
   198→    )
   199→    return [item for item in candidates if scope_matches(item, opts.scope)]
   200→

   203→    state: StateModel, plan: dict | None, status: str,
   204→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   205→    """Inject triage stages, checkpoints, and create-plan when plan is active."""
   206→    if not plan or status not in {"open", "all"}:

   208→
   209→    items: list[WorkQueueItem] = list(build_triage_stage_items(plan, state))

   226→
   227→def _passes_impact_floor(item: WorkQueueItem) -> bool:
   228→    """Return True if item should survive the impact floor filter."""
   229→    if item.get("kind") != "issue":
   230→        return True
   231→    if item.get("is_review") or item.get("is_subjective"):
   232→        return True
   233→    impact = item.get("estimated_impact")

   248→    new_ids = _new_item_ids(state)
```

## Summary: Desloppify Source Code - Workflow Steps & Plan Resolve Architecture

I found the desloppify source code at `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify`. Here's a detailed breakdown of the key components you asked about:

### 1. **Desloppify Binary & Installation**
- **Binary location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/.pyenv/shims/desloppify`
- **Source location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify` (editable install)
- **Package location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/.pyenv/versions/3.11.11/lib/python3.11/site-packages`
- **Version:** 0.9.0

---

### 2. **Workflow Steps (`score-checkpoint` and `create-plan`)**

These are **synthetic workflow items** injected into the plan queue automatically based on state conditions. They're defined in:
- **`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/stale_dimensions.py`** — sync functions that determine when to inject/prune workflow steps
- **`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/synthetic_workflow.py`** — builds the display items for the UI/CLI

#### **Key Workflow IDs:**
```python
WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID = "workflow::score-checkpoint"
WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID = "workflow::create-plan"
WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID = "workflow::import-scores"
WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID = "workflow::communicate-score"
```

#### **When `score-checkpoint` is Injected:**
- File: `stale_dimensions.py`, function: `sync_score_checkpoint_needed()`
- **Conditions:**
  - No unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain
  - `workflow::score-checkpoint` is not already in the queue
- **Positioning:** After subjective items, before triage/create-plan
- **Purpose:** Show user their updated strict score right after initial reviews finish

#### **When `create-plan` is Injected:**
- File: `stale_dimensions.py`, function: `sync_create_plan_needed()`
- **Conditions:**
  - No unscored subjective dimensions remain
  - At least one objective issue exists
  - No triage stages are pending (must not have `triage::*` IDs)
  - `workflow::create-plan` is not already in the queue
- **Positioning:** After subjective/triage items, at end of synthetic block
- **Purpose:** Signal that reviews are complete and objective work needs prioritization

---

### 3. **`plan resolve` Command Flow**

The `plan resolve` command is the primary interface for marking items complete. Located at:
- **`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/override_handlers.py`** — main handler: `cmd_plan_resolve()`
- **`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/resolve/cmd.py`** — rich resolve UX: `cmd_resolve()`

#### **`cmd_plan_resolve()` Flow (lines 486-583):**

1. **Extract synthetic IDs** — Separate `triage::*`, `workflow::*`, `subjective::*` from real patterns
2. **Handle synthetic items** (lines 500-523):
   - Validate triage dependency chain: block if blocked by unmet triage stages (unless `--force-resolve`)
   - Purge IDs from plan queue
   - Log "done" entry
   - Return early if only synthetic items
3. **Validate inputs** (lines 526-534):
   - Check note length requirement
   - Validate attestation format
4. **Cluster completion guard** (lines 536-542):
   - Block bulk-completing small clusters (< 10 items)
5. **Delegate to `cmd_resolve()`** (lines 569-583):
   - Convert plan-specific args to `resolve` command args
   - Call rich resolve handler for real issues

#### **Synthetic Item Resolution (lines 500-523):**
```python
synthetic_ids, real_patterns = _resolve_synthetic_ids(patterns)
if synthetic_ids:
    plan = load_plan()
    blocked_map = _blocked_triage_stages(plan)  # Check triage deps
    for sid in synthetic_ids:
        if sid in blocked_map:
            # Block unless --force-resolve
            deps = blocked_map[sid]  # e.g., ["triage::observe", ...]
            raise_or_return_error(deps)
    purge_ids(plan, synthetic_ids)  # Remove from queue_order
    append_log_entry(plan, "done", issue_ids=synthetic_ids, ...)
    save_plan(plan)
```

---

### 4. **Workflow Step Enforcement & Gating Logic**

#### **Triage Stage Dependencies:**
- File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage_playbook.py`
- Function: `_blocked_triage_stages()` (lines 458-483 in override_handlers.py)
- **Graph:** Stages have prerequisites defined in `TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES`
  - `observe` → no deps
  - `reflect` → depends on `observe`
  - `organize` → depends on `reflect`
  - `commit` → depends on `organize`

#### **No Hard Enforcement for `create-plan` or `score-checkpoint`:**
- These are **soft gating** — they're injected when conditions are met, but:
  - They don't block other work
  - They're resolved by simply running: `desloppify plan resolve "workflow::create-plan" --note "..." --confirm`
  - No validation of prerequisite completion (unlike triage stages)
  - Can be skipped with `--force-resolve` if blocked

---

### 5. **Work Queue Item Building**

File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py`, function: `_gather_workflow_items()` (lines 202-221)

```python
def _gather_workflow_items(state, plan, status):
    """Inject triage stages, checkpoints, and create-plan when plan is active."""
    items = list(build_triage_stage_items(plan, state))
    for builder in (
        build_score_checkpoint_item,
        build_import_scores_item,
        build_communicate_score_item,
    ):
        item = builder(plan, state)
        if item is not None:
            items.append(item)
    plan_item = build_create_plan_item(plan)
    if plan_item is not None:
        items.append(plan_item)
    return items
```

---

### 6. **Synthetic Item Display Templates**

File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/synthetic_workflow.py`

Each workflow item is built with:
- `id`: e.g., `"workflow::score-checkpoint"`
- `kind`: `"workflow_action"`
- `summary`: Human-readable task description
- `primary_command`: CLI command to resolve it
- `detail`: Metadata (e.g., score delta, plan start score)

**Example `score-checkpoint` item (lines 8-39):**
```python
{
    "id": "workflow::score-checkpoint",
    "tier": 1,
```

### 7. **Key Files Summary**

| File | Purpose |
|------|---------|
| `stale_dimensions.py` | Sync functions: when to inject/prune `score-checkpoint`, `create-plan`, triage stages |
| `synthetic_workflow.py` | Build display items for workflow steps |
| `override_handlers.py` | `cmd_plan_resolve()` — plan resolve entry point; triage dep validation |
| `resolve/cmd.py` | `cmd_resolve()` — rich UX for marking real issues fixed |
| `core.py` (work queue) | `_gather_workflow_items()` — inject synthetic items into queue |

---

### Summary

**Workflow steps are *soft-gated, condition-based synthetic items*, not hard gates:**
- `score-checkpoint` appears when all initial reviews complete
- `create-plan` appears when reviews complete AND objective work remains
- Both are resolved via `desloppify plan resolve <id> --note "..." --confirm`
- **Triage stages** are the only hard-enforced workflow step with dependency blocking
- No validation that workflow steps were actually completed — they're advisory/progress markers

```
    56→def _resolve_state_file(path: Path | None) -> Path:
    57→    return path if path is not None else state_mod.STATE_FILE
    58→
    59→
    60→def _resolve_plan_file(path: Path | None) -> Path:
    61→    return path if path is not None else PLAN_FILE
    62→
    63→
    64→def _plan_file_for_state(state_file: Path | None) -> Path | None:
    65→    if state_file is None:
    66→        return None
    67→    return plan_path_for_state(state_file)
    68→
    69→
    70→def _snapshot_file(path: Path) -> str | None:
    71→    if not path.exists():
    72→        return None
    73→    return path.read_text()
    74→
    75→
    76→def _restore_file_snapshot(path: Path, snapshot: str | None) -> None:
    77→    if snapshot is None:
    78→        try:
    79→            path.unlink()
    80→        except FileNotFoundError:
    81→            return
    82→        return
    83→    safe_write_text(path, snapshot)
    84→
    85→
    86→def _save_plan_state_transactional(
    87→    *,
    88→    plan: dict,
    89→    plan_path: Path | None,
    90→    state_data: dict,
    91→    state_path_value: Path | None,
    92→) -> None:
    93→    """Persist plan+state together; rollback both files on partial write failure."""
    94→    effective_plan_path = _resolve_plan_file(plan_path)
    95→    effective_state_path = _resolve_state_file(state_path_value)
    96→    plan_snapshot = _snapshot_file(effective_plan_path)
    97→    state_snapshot = _snapshot_file(effective_state_path)
    98→
    99→    try:
   100→        state_mod.save_state(state_data, effective_state_path)
   101→        save_plan(plan, effective_plan_path)
   102→    except Exception:
   103→        _restore_file_snapshot(effective_state_path, state_snapshot)
   104→        _restore_file_snapshot(effective_plan_path, plan_snapshot)
   105→        raise

   111→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   112→        return
   113→
   114→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   115→    text: str = getattr(args, "text", "")
   116→
   117→    plan = load_plan()

   119→    if not issue_ids:

   121→        return

   123→    for fid in issue_ids:

   163→def _validate_skip_requirements(
   164→    *,
   165→    kind: str,
   166→    attestation: str | None,
   167→    note: str | None,
   168→) -> bool:
   169→    if not skip_kind_requires_attestation(kind):
   170→        return True
   171→    if not validate_attestation(attestation):
   172→        show_attestation_requirement(
   173→            "Permanent skip" if kind == "permanent" else "False positive",
   174→            attestation,
   175→            ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
   176→        )
   177→        return False
   178→    if skip_kind_requires_note(kind) and not note:
   179→        print(
   180→            colorize("  --permanent requires --note to explain the decision.", "yellow"),
   181→            file=sys.stderr,
   182→        )
   183→        return False
   184→    return True

   187→def _apply_state_skip_resolution(
   188→    *,
   189→    kind: str,
   190→    state_file: Path | None,
   191→    issue_ids: list[str],
   192→    note: str | None,
   193→    attestation: str | None,
   194→) -> dict | None:
   195→    status = skip_kind_state_status(kind)
   196→    if status is None:
   197→        return None
   198→    state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   199→    for fid in issue_ids:
   200→        state_mod.resolve_issues(
   201→            state_data,
   202→            fid,
   203→            status,
   204→            note or "",
   205→            attestation=attestation,
   206→        )
   207→    return state_data

   210→def cmd_plan_skip(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   211→    """Skip issues — unified command for temporary/permanent/false-positive."""
   212→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   213→    state = runtime.state
   214→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   215→        return
   216→
   217→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   218→    reason: str | None = getattr(args, "reason", None)
   219→    review_after: int | None = getattr(args, "review_after", None)
   220→    permanent: bool = getattr(args, "permanent", False)
   221→    false_positive: bool = getattr(args, "false_positive", False)
   222→    note: str | None = getattr(args, "note", None)
   223→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attest", None)
   224→
   225→    kind = skip_kind_from_flags(permanent=permanent, false_positive=false_positive)
   226→    if not _validate_skip_requirements(
   227→        kind=kind,
   228→        attestation=attestation,
   229→        note=note,
   230→    ):
   231→        return
   232→
   233→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   234→    plan_file = _plan_file_for_state(state_file)
   235→    plan = load_plan(plan_file)
   236→    issue_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
   237→    if not issue_ids:
   238→        print(colorize("  No matching issues found.", "yellow"))
   239→        return
   240→
   241→    # For permanent/false_positive: delegate to state layer for score impact
   242→    state_data = _apply_state_skip_resolution(
   243→        kind=kind,
   244→        state_file=state_file,
   245→        issue_ids=issue_ids,
   246→        note=note,
   247→        attestation=attestation,
   248→    )
   249→
   250→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   251→    count = skip_items(
   252→        plan,
   253→        issue_ids,
   254→        kind=kind,
   255→        reason=reason,
   256→        note=note,
   257→        attestation=attestation,
   258→        review_after=review_after,
   259→        scan_count=scan_count,
   260→    )
   261→
   262→    # Log the skip action
   263→    append_log_entry(
   264→        plan,
   265→        "skip",
   266→        issue_ids=issue_ids,
   267→        actor="user",
   268→        note=note,
   269→        detail={"kind": kind, "reason": reason},
   270→    )
   271→    if state_data is not None:
   272→        _save_plan_state_transactional(
   273→            plan=plan,
   274→            plan_path=plan_file,
   275→            state_data=state_data,
   276→            state_path_value=state_file,
   277→        )
   278→    else:
   279→        save_plan(plan, plan_file)
   280→
   281→    print(colorize(f"  {SKIP_KIND_LABELS[kind]} {count} item(s).", "green"))
   282→    if review_after:
   283→        print(colorize(f"  Will re-surface after {review_after} scan(s).", "dim"))
   284→    print_user_message(
   285→        "Hey — if skipping was the right call, just continue with"
   286→        " what you were doing. If you think a broader re-triage is"
   287→        " needed, use `desloppify plan triage`. Run `desloppify"
   288→        " plan --help` to see all available plan tools. Otherwise"
   289→        " no need to reply, just keep going."
   290→    )
   291→

   293→def cmd_plan_unskip(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   294→    """Unskip issues — bring back to queue."""
   295→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   296→    state = runtime.state
   297→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   298→        return
   299→
   300→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   301→
   302→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   303→    plan_file = _plan_file_for_state(state_file)
   304→    plan = load_plan(plan_file)
   305→    # For unskip we need to match against all statuses (skipped items may be wontfix/fp)
   306→    issue_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan, status_filter="all")
   307→    if not issue_ids:
   308→        print(colorize("  No matching issues found.", "yellow"))
   309→        return
   310→
   311→    count, need_reopen = unskip_items(plan, issue_ids)
   312→    append_log_entry(
   313→        plan, "unskip", issue_ids=issue_ids, actor="user",
   314→        detail={"need_reopen": need_reopen},
   315→    )
   316→
   317→    # Reopen permanent/false_positive items in state
   318→    reopened: list[str] = []
   319→    if need_reopen:
   320→        state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   321→        for fid in need_reopen:
   322→            reopened.extend(state_mod.resolve_issues(state_data, fid, "open"))
   323→        _save_plan_state_transactional(

   325→            plan_path=plan_file,

   327→            state_path_value=state_file,
   328→        )
   329→        print(colorize(f"  Reopened {len(reopened)} issue(s) in state.", "dim"))
   330→    else:
   331→        save_plan(plan, plan_file)
   332→
   333→    print(colorize(f"  Unskipped {count} item(s) — back in queue.", "green"))

   340→def cmd_plan_reopen(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   341→    """Reopen resolved issues from plan context."""
   342→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   343→
   344→    raw_state_path = state_path(args)
   345→    state_file = raw_state_path if isinstance(raw_state_path, Path) else Path(raw_state_path) if raw_state_path else None
   346→    state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   347→    plan_file = _plan_file_for_state(state_file)
   348→
   349→    reopened: list[str] = []
   350→    for pattern in patterns:
   351→        reopened.extend(
   352→            state_mod.resolve_issues(state_data, pattern, "open")
   353→        )
   354→
   355→    if not reopened:
   356→        print(colorize("  No resolved issues matching: " + " ".join(patterns), "yellow"))
   357→        return
   358→
   359→    # Remove from skipped if present, and ensure all reopened IDs are in queue
   360→    plan = load_plan(plan_file)
   361→
   362→    # Remove from commit tracking uncommitted list
   363→    purge_uncommitted_ids(plan, reopened)
   364→
   365→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   366→    count = 0
   367→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   368→    for fid in reopened:
   369→        if fid in skipped:
   370→            skipped.pop(fid)
   371→            count += 1
   372→        if fid not in order:
   373→            plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   374→            order.add(fid)
   375→            count += 1
   376→    append_log_entry(
   377→        plan, "reopen", issue_ids=reopened, actor="user",
   378→    )
   379→    _save_plan_state_transactional(
   380→        plan=plan,
   381→        plan_path=plan_file,
   382→        state_data=state_data,
   383→        state_path_value=state_file,
   384→    )
   385→
   386→    print(colorize(f"  Reopened {len(reopened)} issue(s).", "green"))
   387→    if count:
   388→        print(colorize("  Plan updated: items moved back to queue.", "dim"))

   391→_CLUSTER_INDIVIDUAL_THRESHOLD = 10
   392→
   393→
   394→def _check_cluster_guard(patterns: list[str], plan: dict, state: dict) -> bool:
   395→    """Return True if blocked by cluster guard, False if OK to proceed."""
   396→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   397→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   398→    for pattern in patterns:
   399→        if pattern in clusters:
   400→            cluster = clusters[pattern]
   401→            # Filter to alive issues only — stale IDs should not count
   402→            ids = [
   403→                fid for fid in cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   404→                if fid in issues and issues[fid].get("status") == "open"
   405→            ]
   406→            if len(ids) == 0:
   407→                print(colorize(
   408→                    f"\n  Cluster '{pattern}' is empty — add items before marking it done.\n",
   409→                    "yellow",
   410→                ))
   411→                print(colorize(
   412→                    f"  Use: desloppify plan cluster add {pattern} <issue-id>",
   413→                    "dim",
   414→                ))
   415→                return True  # blocked
   416→            if len(ids) <= _CLUSTER_INDIVIDUAL_THRESHOLD:
   417→                _print_cluster_guard(pattern, ids, state)
   418→                return True  # blocked
   419→    return False  # OK
   420→
   421→
   422→def _print_cluster_guard(cluster_name: str, issue_ids: list[str], state: dict) -> None:
   423→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   424→    print(colorize(
   425→        f"\n  Cluster '{cluster_name}' has {len(issue_ids)} item(s) — mark them done individually first:\n",
   426→        "yellow",
   427→    ))
   428→    for fid in issue_ids:
   429→        f = issues.get(fid, {})
   430→        summary = f.get("summary", "(no summary)")[:80]
   431→        detector = f.get("detector", "?")
   432→        print(f"    {fid}  [{detector}]  {summary}")
   433→    print(colorize(
   434→        "\n  Use: desloppify resolve <id> --status fixed --note '...' --attest '...'",
   435→        "dim",
   436→    ))
   437→    print(colorize(
   438→        "  Or mark each resolved: desloppify plan resolve <id> --note '...' --confirm\n",
   439→        "dim",
   440→    ))
   441→
   442→
   443→def _is_synthetic_id(fid: str) -> bool:
   444→    """Return True if the ID is a synthetic workflow/triage item, not a real issue."""
   445→    return fid.startswith("triage::") or fid.startswith("workflow::") or fid.startswith("subjective::")
   446→
   447→
   448→def _resolve_synthetic_ids(patterns: list[str]) -> tuple[list[str], list[str]]:
   449→    """Separate synthetic IDs from real issue patterns.
   450→
   451→    Returns (synthetic_ids, remaining_patterns).
   452→    """
   453→    synthetic = [p for p in patterns if _is_synthetic_id(p)]
   454→    remaining = [p for p in patterns if not _is_synthetic_id(p)]
   455→    return synthetic, remaining
   456→
   457→
   458→def _blocked_triage_stages(plan: dict) -> dict[str, list[str]]:
   459→    """Return ``{stage_id: [blocked_by_ids]}`` for triage stages that can't run yet.
   460→
   461→    Uses the dependency graph and confirmed-stage metadata directly —
   462→    no state needed, no queue item construction.
   463→    """
   464→    order_set = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   465→    present = order_set & TRIAGE_IDS
   466→    if not present:
   467→        return {}
   468→
   469→    confirmed = set(plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {}).get("triage_stages", {}).keys())
   470→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "commit")
   471→
   472→    blocked: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
   473→    for sid, name in zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names, strict=False):
   474→        if sid not in present or name in confirmed:
   475→            continue
   476→        deps = TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES.get(name, set())
   477→        unmet = sorted(
   478→            f"triage::{dep}" for dep in deps
   479→            if f"triage::{dep}" in present and dep not in confirmed
   480→        )
   481→        if unmet:
   482→            blocked[sid] = unmet
   483→    return blocked
   484→
   485→
   486→def cmd_plan_resolve(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   487→    """Mark issues as fixed — delegates to cmd_resolve for rich UX."""
   488→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   489→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attest", None)
   490→    note: str | None = getattr(args, "note", None)
   491→
   492→    # --confirm: auto-generate attestation from --note
   493→    if getattr(args, "confirm", False):
   494→        if not note:
   495→            print(colorize("  --confirm requires --note to describe what you did.", "red"))
   496→            return
   497→        attestation = f"I have actually {note} and I am not gaming the score."
   498→        args.attest = attestation
   499→
   500→    # Handle synthetic IDs (triage::*, workflow::*, subjective::*) directly
   501→    synthetic_ids, real_patterns = _resolve_synthetic_ids(patterns)
   502→    if synthetic_ids:
   503→        plan = load_plan()
   504→        # Validate triage dependency chain
   505→        blocked_map = _blocked_triage_stages(plan)
   506→        for sid in synthetic_ids:
   507→            if sid in blocked_map:
   508→                deps = ", ".join(b.replace("triage::", "") for b in blocked_map[sid])
   509→                print(colorize(f"  Cannot resolve {sid} — blocked by: {deps}", "red"))
   510→                print(colorize("  Complete those stages first, or use --force-resolve to override.", "dim"))
   511→                if not getattr(args, "force_resolve", False):
   512→                    return
   513→        purge_ids(plan, synthetic_ids)
   514→        append_log_entry(
   515→            plan, "done", issue_ids=synthetic_ids, actor="user", note=note,
   516→        )
   517→        save_plan(plan)
   518→        for sid in synthetic_ids:
   519→            print(colorize(f"  Resolved: {sid}", "green"))
   520→        if not real_patterns:
   521→            return
   522→        # Continue with remaining real patterns
   523→        patterns = real_patterns
   524→        args.patterns = patterns
   525→
   526→    # Validate note length
   527→    if not validate_note_length(note):
   528→        show_note_length_requirement(note)
   529→        return
   530→
   531→    # Pre-validate attestation before delegating (avoids stale hint in resolve)
   532→    if not validate_attestation(attestation):
   533→        show_attestation_requirement("Plan resolve", attestation, ATTEST_EXAMPLE)
   534→        return
   535→
   536→    # Cluster completion guard: block bulk-completing small clusters
   537→    try:
   538→        runtime = command_runtime(args)
   539→        state = runtime.state
   540→        plan = load_plan()
   541→        if _check_cluster_guard(patterns, plan, state):
   542→            return
   543→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   544→        plan = None
   545→
   546→    # Log the done action (best-effort)
   547→    try:
   548→        if plan is None:
   549→            plan = load_plan()
   550→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   551→        cluster_name = None
   552→        for p in patterns:
   553→            if p in clusters:
   554→                cluster_name = p
   555→                break
   556→        append_log_entry(
   557→            plan,
   558→            "done",
   559→            issue_ids=patterns,
   560→            cluster_name=cluster_name,
   561→            actor="user",
   562→            note=note,
   563→        )
   564→        save_plan(plan)
   565→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   566→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, "append plan resolve log entry", exc)
   567→        print(colorize(f"  Note: unable to append plan resolve log entry ({exc}).", "dim"))
   568→
   569→    # Build a Namespace that cmd_resolve expects
   570→    resolve_args = argparse.Namespace(
   571→        status="fixed",
   572→        patterns=patterns,
   573→        note=note,
   574→        attest=attestation,
   575→        confirm_batch_wontfix=False,
   576→        force_resolve=bool(getattr(args, "force_resolve", False)),
   577→        state=getattr(args, "state", None),
   578→        lang=getattr(args, "lang", None),
   579→        path=getattr(args, "path", None),
   580→        exclude=getattr(args, "exclude", None),
   581→    )
   582→
   583→    cmd_resolve(resolve_args)
   584→
   585→
   586→def cmd_plan_focus(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   587→    """Set or clear the active cluster focus."""
   588→    clear_flag = getattr(args, "clear", False)
   589→    cluster_name: str | None = getattr(args, "cluster_name", None)
   590→
   591→    plan = load_plan()
   592→    if clear_flag:
   593→        prev = plan.get("active_cluster")
   594→        clear_focus(plan)
   595→        append_log_entry(
   596→            plan, "focus", actor="user",
   597→            detail={"action": "clear", "previous": prev},
   598→        )
   599→        save_plan(plan)
   600→        print(colorize("  Focus cleared.", "green"))
   601→        return
   602→
   603→    if not cluster_name:
   604→        active = plan.get("active_cluster")
   605→        if active:
   606→            print(f"  Focused on: {active}")
   607→        else:
   608→            print("  No active focus.")
   609→        return
   610→
   611→    try:
   612→        set_focus(plan, cluster_name)
   613→    except ValueError as ex:
   614→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
   615→        return
   616→    append_log_entry(
   617→        plan, "focus", cluster_name=cluster_name, actor="user",
   618→        detail={"action": "set"},
   619→    )
   620→    save_plan(plan)
   621→    print(colorize(f"  Focused on: {cluster_name}", "green"))
   622→
   623→
   624→__all__ = [
```

```
    24→def _validate_attestation(
    25→    attestation: str,
    26→    stage: str,
    27→    *,
    28→    dimensions: list[str] | None = None,
    29→    cluster_names: list[str] | None = None,
    30→) -> str | None:
    31→    """Return error message if attestation doesn't reference required data, else None."""
    32→    text = attestation.lower()
    33→
    34→    if stage == "observe":
    35→        if dimensions:
    36→            found = [d for d in dimensions if d.lower().replace("_", " ") in text or d.lower() in text]
    37→            if not found:
    38→                dim_list = ", ".join(dimensions[:6])
    39→                return f"Attestation must reference at least one dimension from the summary. Mention one of: {dim_list}"
    40→
    41→    elif stage == "reflect":
    42→        refs: list[str] = []
    43→        if dimensions:
    44→            refs.extend(d for d in dimensions if d.lower().replace("_", " ") in text or d.lower() in text)
    45→        if cluster_names:
    46→            refs.extend(n for n in cluster_names if n.lower() in text)
    47→        if not refs and (dimensions or cluster_names):
    48→            return (
    49→                f"Attestation must reference at least one dimension or cluster name.\n"
    50→                f"  Valid dimensions: {', '.join((dimensions or [])[:6])}\n"
    51→                f"  Valid clusters: {', '.join((cluster_names or [])[:6]) if cluster_names else '(none yet)'}"
    52→            )
    53→
    54→    elif stage == "organize":
    55→        if cluster_names:
    56→            found = [n for n in cluster_names if n.lower() in text]
    57→            if not found:
    58→                names = ", ".join(cluster_names[:6])
    59→                return f"Attestation must reference at least one cluster from the plan. Mention one of: {names}"
    60→
    61→    return None

   315→
   316→def _cmd_confirm_stage(
   317→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   318→    *,
   319→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   320→) -> None:
   321→    """Router for ``--confirm observe/reflect/organize``."""
   322→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   323→    confirm_stage = getattr(args, "confirm", None)
   324→    attestation = getattr(args, "attestation", None)
   325→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   326→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   327→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   328→
   329→    if confirm_stage == "observe":
   330→        _confirm_observe(args, plan, stages, attestation, services=resolved_services)
   331→    elif confirm_stage == "reflect":
   332→        _confirm_reflect(args, plan, stages, attestation, services=resolved_services)
   333→    elif confirm_stage == "organize":
   334→        _confirm_organize(args, plan, stages, attestation, services=resolved_services)
   335→
   336→
   337→MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN = _MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN
   338→validate_attestation = _validate_attestation
   339→
   340→
   341→def cmd_confirm_stage(
   342→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   343→    *,
   344→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   345→) -> None:
   346→    """Public triage confirmation entrypoint."""
   347→    _cmd_confirm_stage(args, services=services)
   348→
```

```
1→"""Validation and guardrail helpers for triage stage workflow."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.plan.triage_playbook import TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE

    18→from .confirmations import _MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN, _validate_attestation

    25→def _auto_confirm_observe_if_attested(
    26→    *,
    27→    plan: dict,
    28→    stages: dict,
    29→    attestation: str | None,
    30→    triage_input,
    31→) -> bool:
    32→    if stages["observe"].get("confirmed_at"):
    33→        return True
    34→    if not attestation or len(attestation.strip()) < _MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN:
    35→        print(colorize("  Cannot reflect: observe stage not confirmed.", "red"))
    36→        print(colorize("  Run: desloppify plan triage --confirm observe", "dim"))
    37→        print(colorize("  Or pass --attestation to auto-confirm observe inline.", "dim"))
    38→        return False
    39→    _by_dim, dim_names = observe_dimension_breakdown(triage_input)
    40→    validation_err = _validate_attestation(
    41→        attestation.strip(),
    42→        "observe",
    43→        dimensions=dim_names,
    44→    )
    45→    if validation_err:
    46→        print(colorize(f"  {validation_err}", "red"))
    47→        return False
    48→    stages["observe"]["confirmed_at"] = utc_now()
    49→    stages["observe"]["confirmed_text"] = attestation.strip()
    50→    save_plan(plan)
    51→    print(colorize("  ✓ Observe auto-confirmed via --attestation.", "green"))
    52→    return True
    53→
    54→
    55→def _validate_recurring_dimension_mentions(
    56→    *,
    57→    report: str,
    58→    recurring_dims: list[str],
    59→    recurring: dict,
    60→) -> bool:
    61→    if not recurring_dims:
    62→        return True
    63→    report_lower = report.lower()
    64→    mentioned = [dim for dim in recurring_dims if dim.lower() in report_lower]
    65→    if mentioned:
    66→        return True
    67→    print(colorize("  Recurring patterns detected but not addressed in report:", "red"))
    68→    for dim in recurring_dims:
    69→        info = recurring[dim]
    70→        print(
    71→            colorize(
    72→                f"    {dim}: {len(info['resolved'])} resolved, "
    73→                f"{len(info['open'])} still open — potential loop",
    74→                "yellow",
    75→            )
    76→        )
    77→    print(
    78→        colorize(
    79→            "  Your report must mention at least one recurring dimension name.",
    80→            "dim",
    81→        )
    82→    )
    83→    return False
    84→
    85→
    86→def _require_reflect_stage_for_organize(stages: dict) -> bool:
    87→    if "reflect" in stages:
    88→        return True
    89→    if "observe" not in stages:
    90→        print(colorize("  Cannot organize: observe stage not complete.", "red"))
    91→        print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report "..."', "dim"))
    92→        return False
    93→    print(colorize("  Cannot organize: reflect stage not complete.", "red"))
    94→    print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage reflect --report "..."', "dim"))
    95→    return False
    96→
    97→
    98→def _auto_confirm_reflect_for_organize(
    99→    *,
   100→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   101→    plan: dict,
   102→    stages: dict,
   103→    attestation: str | None,
   104→) -> bool:
   105→    if stages["reflect"].get("confirmed_at"):
   106→        return True
   107→    if not attestation or len(attestation.strip()) < _MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN:
   108→        print(colorize("  Cannot organize: reflect stage not confirmed.", "red"))
   109→        print(colorize("  Run: desloppify plan triage --confirm reflect", "dim"))
   110→        print(colorize("  Or pass --attestation to auto-confirm reflect inline.", "dim"))
   111→        return False
   112→
   113→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   114→    triage_input = collect_triage_input(plan, runtime.state)
   115→    recurring = detect_recurring_patterns(
   116→        triage_input.open_issues,
   117→        triage_input.resolved_issues,
   118→    )
   119→    _by_dim, observe_dims = observe_dimension_breakdown(triage_input)
   120→    reflect_dims = sorted(set((list(recurring.keys()) if recurring else []) + observe_dims))
   121→    reflect_clusters = [
   122→        name for name in plan.get("clusters", {}) if not plan["clusters"][name].get("auto")
   123→    ]
   124→    validation_err = _validate_attestation(
   125→        attestation.strip(),
   126→        "reflect",
   127→        dimensions=reflect_dims,
   128→        cluster_names=reflect_clusters,
   129→    )
   130→    if validation_err:
   131→        print(colorize(f"  {validation_err}", "red"))
   132→        return False
   133→    stages["reflect"]["confirmed_at"] = utc_now()
   134→    stages["reflect"]["confirmed_text"] = attestation.strip()
   135→    save_plan(plan)
   136→    print(colorize("  ✓ Reflect auto-confirmed via --attestation.", "green"))
   137→    return True
   138→
   139→
   140→def _manual_clusters_or_error(plan: dict) -> list[str] | None:
   141→    manual_clusters = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   142→    if manual_clusters:
   143→        return manual_clusters
   144→    any_clusters = [
   145→        name for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items() if cluster.get("issue_ids")
   146→    ]
   147→    if any_clusters:
   148→        print(colorize("  Cannot organize: only auto-clusters exist.", "red"))
   149→        print(colorize("  Create manual clusters that group issues by root cause:", "dim"))
   150→    else:
   151→        print(colorize("  Cannot organize: no clusters with issues exist.", "red"))
   152→    print(colorize('    desloppify plan cluster create <name> --description "..."', "dim"))
   153→    print(colorize("    desloppify plan cluster add <name> <issue-patterns>", "dim"))
   154→    return None
   155→
   156→
   157→def _clusters_enriched_or_error(plan: dict) -> bool:
   158→    gaps = _unenriched_clusters(plan)
   159→    if not gaps:
   160→        return True
   161→    print(colorize(f"  Cannot organize: {len(gaps)} cluster(s) need enrichment.", "red"))
   162→    for name, missing in gaps:
   163→        print(colorize(f"    {name}: missing {', '.join(missing)}", "yellow"))
   164→    print()
   165→    print(colorize("  Each cluster needs a description and action steps:", "dim"))
   166→    print(
   167→        colorize(
   168→            '    desloppify plan cluster update <name> --description "what this cluster addresses" '
   169→            '--steps "step 1" "step 2"',
   170→            "dim",
   171→        )
   172→    )
   173→    return False
   174→
   175→
   176→def _organize_report_or_error(report: str | None) -> str | None:
   177→    if not report:
   178→        print(colorize("  --report is required for --stage organize.", "red"))
   179→        print(colorize("  Summarize your prioritized organization:", "dim"))
   180→        print(colorize("  - Did you defer contradictory issues before clustering?", "dim"))
   181→        print(colorize("  - What clusters did you create and why?", "dim"))
   182→        print(
   183→            colorize(
   184→                "  - Explicit priority ordering: which cluster 1st, 2nd, 3rd and why?",
   185→                "dim",
   186→            )
   187→        )
   188→        print(colorize("  - What depends on what? What unblocks the most?", "dim"))
   189→        return None
   190→    if len(report) < 100:
   191→        print(colorize(f"  Report too short: {len(report)} chars (minimum 100).", "red"))
   192→        print(colorize("  Explain what you organized, your priorities, and focus order.", "dim"))
   193→        return None
   194→    return report
   195→
   196→
   197→def _require_organize_stage_for_complete(
   198→    *,
   199→    plan: dict,
   200→    meta: dict,
   201→    stages: dict,
   202→) -> bool:
   203→    if "organize" in stages:
   204→        return True
   205→    if "observe" not in stages:
   206→        print(colorize("  Cannot complete: no stages done yet.", "red"))
   207→        print(colorize('  Start with: desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report "..."', "dim"))
   208→        return False
   209→
   210→    print(colorize("  Cannot complete: organize stage not done.", "red"))

   214→        for name, missing in gaps:

   216→        print(
   217→            colorize(

   220→            )
   221→        )
   222→        print(colorize(f"  Then: {TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE}", "dim"))
   223→    else:

   227→            print(colorize(f"    {TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE}", "dim"))
   228→        else:
   229→            print(colorize("  Create enriched clusters first, then record organize:", "dim"))
   230→            print(colorize(f"    {TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE}", "dim"))
   231→    if meta.get("strategy_summary"):

   233→            colorize(

   235→                "dim",
   236→            )
   237→        )
   238→    return False

   301→def _resolve_completion_strategy(
   302→    strategy: str | None,
   303→    *,
   304→    meta: dict,
   305→) -> str | None:
   306→    if strategy:
   307→        return strategy
   308→    print(colorize("  --strategy is required.", "red"))
   309→    existing = meta.get("strategy_summary", "")
   310→    if existing:
   311→        print(colorize(f"  Current strategy: {existing}", "dim"))
   312→        print(colorize('  Use --strategy "same" to keep it, or provide a new summary.', "dim"))
   313→    else:
   314→        print(
   315→            colorize(
   316→                '  Provide --strategy "execution plan describing priorities, ordering, and verification approach"',
   317→                "dim",
   318→            )
   319→        )
   320→    return None
   321→
   322→
   323→def _completion_strategy_valid(strategy: str) -> bool:
   324→    if strategy.strip().lower() == "same":
   325→        return True
   326→    if len(strategy.strip()) >= 200:
   327→        return True
   328→    print(colorize(f"  Strategy too short: {len(strategy.strip())} chars (minimum 200).", "red"))
   329→    print(colorize("  The strategy should describe:", "dim"))
   330→    print(colorize("    - Execution order and priorities", "dim"))
   331→    print(colorize("    - What each cluster accomplishes", "dim"))
   332→    print(colorize("    - How to verify the work is correct", "dim"))
   333→    return False
   334→
   335→
   336→def _require_prior_strategy_for_confirm(meta: dict) -> bool:
   337→    if meta.get("strategy_summary", ""):
   338→        return True
   339→    print(colorize("  Cannot confirm existing: no prior triage has been completed.", "red"))
   340→    print(colorize("  The full OBSERVE → REFLECT → ORGANIZE → COMMIT flow is required the first time.", "dim"))
   341→    print(colorize(f"  Create and enrich clusters, then: {TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE}", "dim"))
   342→    return False
   343→
   344→
   345→def _confirm_existing_stages_valid(
   346→    *,
   347→    stages: dict,
   348→    has_only_additions: bool,
   349→    si,
   350→) -> bool:
   351→    if has_only_additions:
   352→        _print_new_issues_since_last(si)
   353→        return True
   354→    if "observe" not in stages:
   355→        print(colorize("  Cannot confirm existing: observe stage not complete.", "red"))
   356→        print(colorize("  You must read issues first.", "dim"))
   357→        print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report "..."', "dim"))
   358→        return False
   359→    if "reflect" not in stages:
   360→        print(colorize("  Cannot confirm existing: reflect stage not complete.", "red"))
   361→        print(colorize("  You must compare against completed work first.", "dim"))
   362→        print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage reflect --report "..."', "dim"))
   363→        return False
   364→    return True
   365→
   366→

   371→        return False

   375→    return True

   377→
   378→def _resolve_confirm_existing_strategy(
   379→    strategy: str | None,
   380→    *,
   381→    has_only_additions: bool,
   382→    meta: dict,
   383→) -> str | None:
   384→    if strategy:
   385→        return strategy
   386→    if has_only_additions:
   387→        return "same"
   388→    print(colorize("  --strategy is required.", "red"))
   389→    existing = meta.get("strategy_summary", "")
   390→    if existing:
   391→        print(colorize('  Use --strategy "same" to keep it, or provide a new summary.', "dim"))
   392→    return None
   393→
   394→
   395→def _confirm_strategy_valid(strategy: str) -> bool:
   396→    if strategy.strip().lower() == "same":
   397→        return True
   398→    if len(strategy.strip()) >= 200:
   399→        return True
   400→    print(colorize(f"  Strategy too short: {len(strategy.strip())} chars (minimum 200).", "red"))
   401→    return False
   402→
   403→
   404→def _confirmed_text_or_error(
   405→    *,
   406→    plan: dict,
   407→    state: dict,
   408→    confirmed: str | None,
   409→) -> str | None:
   410→    if confirmed and len(confirmed.strip()) >= _MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN:
   411→        return confirmed.strip()
   412→    print(colorize("  Current plan:", "bold"))
   413→    show_plan_summary(plan, state)
   414→    if confirmed:
   415→        print(
   416→            colorize(
   417→                f"\n  --confirmed text too short ({len(confirmed.strip())} chars, min {_MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN}).",
   418→                "red",
   419→            )
   420→        )
   421→    print(colorize('\n  Add --confirmed "I validate this plan..." to proceed.', "dim"))
   422→    return None
   423→
   424→
   425→def _note_cites_new_issues_or_error(note: str, si) -> bool:
   426→    new_ids = si.new_since_last
   427→    if not new_ids:
   428→        return True
   429→    valid_ids = set(si.open_issues.keys())
   430→    cited = extract_issue_citations(note, valid_ids)
   431→    new_cited = cited & new_ids
   432→    if new_cited:
   433→        return True
   434→    print(colorize("  Note must cite at least 1 new/changed issue.", "red"))
   435→    print(colorize(f"  {len(new_ids)} new issue(s) since last triage:", "dim"))
   436→    for fid in sorted(new_ids)[:5]:
   437→        print(colorize(f"    {fid}", "dim"))
   438→    if len(new_ids) > 5:
   439→        print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(new_ids) - 5} more", "dim"))
   440→    return False
   441→
   442→

   444→    "_auto_confirm_observe_if_attested",
   445→    "_auto_confirm_organize_for_complete",
```

```
    46→def cmd_plan_output(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    47→    """Generate a prioritized markdown plan from state."""
    48→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    49→    state = runtime.state
    50→
    51→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    52→        return
    53→
    54→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(runtime.config)
    55→    if config_warning:
    56→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
    57→
    58→    plan_md = planning_mod.generate_plan_md(state)
    59→    next_command = "desloppify next --count 20"
    60→
    61→    output = getattr(args, "output", None)
    62→    if output:
    63→        try:
    64→            from pathlib import Path
    65→            output_path = Path(output)
    66→            if output_path.exists():
    67→                print(colorize(
    68→                    f"  {output} already exists — refusing to overwrite.",
    69→                    "red",
    70→                ))
    71→                print(colorize(
    72→                    f"  Delete it first (`desloppify plan reset` or `rm {output}`), "
    73→                    f"or choose a different filename.",
    74→                    "dim",
    75→                ))
    76→                return
    77→            safe_write_text(output, plan_md)
    78→            print(colorize(f"Plan written to {output}", "green"))
    79→            print_agent_plan(
    80→                ["Inspect and execute the generated plan."],
    81→                next_command=next_command,
    82→            )
    83→        except OSError as e:
    84→            warn_best_effort(f"Could not write plan to {output}: {e}")
    85→    else:
    86→        print(plan_md)
    87→        print()
    88→        print_agent_plan(
    89→            ["Start from the top-ranked action in this plan."],
    90→            next_command=next_command,
    91→        )
    92→
    93→
    94→def _cmd_plan_generate(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    95→    """Generate the prioritized markdown plan (existing behavior)."""
    96→    cmd_plan_output(args)
    97→
    98→
    99→def _cmd_plan_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   100→    """Show plan metadata summary."""
   101→    plan = load_plan()
   102→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   103→
   104→    # Dynamic queue count — matches what `next` and `plan queue` show.
   105→    try:
   106→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(runtime.state, plan)
   107→        queue_line = format_queue_headline(breakdown)
   108→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   109→        # Fallback to raw plan data if queue build fails
   110→        ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   111→        queue_line = f"Queue: {ordered} items prioritized"
   112→
   113→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   114→    total_skipped = len(skipped)
   115→    kind_counts = {
   116→        kind: sum(1 for entry in skipped.values() if entry.get("kind") == kind)
   117→        for kind in USER_SKIP_KINDS
   118→    }
   119→    temp_count = kind_counts["temporary"]
   120→    perm_count = kind_counts["permanent"]
   121→    fp_count = kind_counts["false_positive"]
   122→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   123→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
   124→    superseded = len(plan.get("superseded", {}))
   125→
   126→    described, noted = annotation_counts(plan)
   127→
   128→    print(colorize("  Living Plan Status", "bold"))
   129→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 40, "dim"))
   130→    print(f"  {queue_line}")
   131→    if total_skipped:
   132→        print(f"  Skipped:          {total_skipped} (temp: {temp_count}, wontfix: {perm_count}, fp: {fp_count})")
   133→    else:
   134→        print("  Skipped:          0")
   135→    print(f"  Clusters:         {len(clusters)}")
   136→    if clusters:
   137→        for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   138→            desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   139→            member_count = len(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   140→            marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
   141→            desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
   142→            print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{desc_str}{marker}")
   143→    if described or noted:
   144→        print(f"  Annotations:      {described} described, {noted} noted")
   145→    if active:
   146→        print(f"  Focus:            {active}")
   147→    if superseded:
   148→        print(f"  Disappeared:      {superseded} (resolved or removed since last scan)")
   149→
   150→    # Commit tracking summary
   151→    _cfg = load_config()
   152→    if _cfg.get("commit_tracking_enabled", True):
   153→        ct = commit_tracking_summary(plan)
   154→        if ct["total"] > 0:
   155→            pr_num = _cfg.get("commit_pr", 0)
   156→            pr_str = f"  PR: #{pr_num}" if pr_num else ""
   157→            print(
   158→                f"  Commit tracking:  {ct['uncommitted']} uncommitted, "
   159→                f"{ct['committed']} committed ({ct['total']} issues){pr_str}"
   160→            )
   161→
   162→
   163→def _cmd_plan_reset(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   164→    """Reset the plan to empty."""
   165→    plan = load_plan()
   166→    queue_len = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   167→    cluster_count = len(plan.get("clusters", {}))
   168→    reset_plan(plan)
   169→    append_log_entry(
   170→        plan, "reset", actor="user",
   171→        detail={"previous_queue_size": queue_len, "previous_cluster_count": cluster_count},
   172→    )
   173→    save_plan(plan)
   174→    print(colorize("  Plan reset to empty.", "green"))
   175→
   176→
   177→_PLAN_ACTION_HANDLERS = {
   178→    "show": _cmd_plan_show,
   179→    "queue": cmd_plan_queue,
   180→    "reset": _cmd_plan_reset,
   181→    "reorder": cmd_plan_reorder,
   182→    "describe": cmd_plan_describe,
   183→    "resolve": cmd_plan_resolve,
   184→    "note": cmd_plan_note,
   185→    "focus": cmd_plan_focus,
   186→    "skip": cmd_plan_skip,
   187→    "unskip": cmd_plan_unskip,
   188→    "reopen": cmd_plan_reopen,
   189→    "cluster": cmd_cluster_dispatch,
   190→    "triage": cmd_plan_triage,
   191→    "commit-log": cmd_commit_log_dispatch,
   192→}
   193→
   194→
   195→def cmd_plan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   196→    """Dispatch plan subcommand or generate markdown output."""
   197→    plan_action = getattr(args, "plan_action", None)
   198→    if plan_action is None:
   199→        _cmd_plan_generate(args)
   200→        return
   201→
   202→    handler = _PLAN_ACTION_HANDLERS.get(plan_action)
   203→    if handler is None:
   204→        print(f"Unknown plan action: {plan_action}")
   205→        return
   206→    handler(args)
   207→
   208→__all__ = ["cmd_plan", "cmd_plan_output"]
   209→
```

```
   322→def add_plan_parser(sub) -> None:
   323→    p_plan = sub.add_parser(
   324→        "plan",
   325→        help="Living plan: generate, show, resolve, skip, cluster, triage",
   326→        description="""\
   327→Manage the living plan — a persistent layer on top of the work queue.
   328→Track custom ordering, clusters, skips, and per-issue annotations.
   329→Run with no subcommand to generate a full prioritized markdown plan.""",
   330→        epilog="""\
   331→typical workflow:
   332→  desloppify scan                       # detect issues
   333→  desloppify plan                       # full prioritized markdown
   334→  desloppify plan queue                 # compact table of all items
   335→  desloppify plan cluster create ...    # group related issues
   336→  desloppify plan focus <cluster>       # narrow scope
   337→  desloppify next                       # work on the next item
   338→  desloppify plan resolve <id> --attest .. # mark as fixed
   339→
   340→patterns (used by reorder, skip, resolve, describe, note, etc.):
   341→  Patterns match issues by detector, file, ID prefix, glob, or name.
   342→  Cluster names also work as patterns — they expand to all member IDs.
   343→  Examples: "security", "src/foo.py", "unused::*React*", "my-cluster"
   344→
   345→subcommands:
   346→  show       Show plan metadata summary
   347→  queue      Compact table of upcoming queue items
   348→  reset      Reset plan to empty
   349→  reorder    Reposition issues or clusters in the queue
   350→  resolve    Mark issues as fixed (score movement + next-step)
   351→  describe   Set augmented description
   352→  note       Set note on issues
   353→  skip       Skip issues (temporary/permanent/false_positive)
   354→  unskip     Bring skipped issues back to queue
   355→  reopen     Reopen resolved issues
   356→  focus      Set or clear active cluster focus
   357→  cluster    Manage issue clusters
   358→  triage     Staged triage workflow (after review)""",
   359→        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
   360→    )
   361→    p_plan.add_argument("--state", type=str, default=None, help="Path to state file")
   362→    p_plan.add_argument(
   363→        "--output", type=str, metavar="FILE",
   364→        help="Write to file instead of stdout (refuses to overwrite existing files)",
   365→    )
   366→
   367→    plan_sub = p_plan.add_subparsers(dest="plan_action")
   368→
   369→    # plan show
   370→    plan_sub.add_parser("show", help="Show plan metadata summary")
   371→
   372→    _add_queue_subparser(plan_sub)
   373→
   374→    # plan reset
   375→    plan_sub.add_parser("reset", help="Reset plan to empty")
   376→
   377→    _add_reorder_subparser(plan_sub)
   378→    _add_annotation_subparsers(plan_sub)
   379→    _add_skip_subparsers(plan_sub)
```

```
   130→

   145→class PlanModel(TypedDict, total=False):
   146→    version: Required[int]
   147→    created: Required[str]
   148→    updated: Required[str]
   149→    queue_order: list[str]
   150→    deferred: list[str]  # kept empty for migration compat
   151→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry]
   152→    active_cluster: str | None
   153→    overrides: dict[str, ItemOverride]
   154→    clusters: dict[str, Cluster]
   155→    superseded: dict[str, SupersededEntry]
   156→    promoted_ids: list[str]  # IDs user explicitly positioned via move_items()
   157→    plan_start_scores: PlanStartScores
   158→    execution_log: list[ExecutionLogEntry]
   159→    epic_triage_meta: EpicTriageMeta
   160→    commit_log: list[CommitRecord]
   161→    uncommitted_issues: list[str]
   162→    commit_tracking_branch: str | None
   163→    completed_clusters: NotRequired[list[dict[str, Any]]]  # legacy snapshot key
   164→
   165→
   166→def empty_plan() -> PlanModel:
   167→    """Return a new empty plan payload."""
   168→    now = utc_now()
   169→    return {
   170→        "version": PLAN_VERSION,
   171→        "created": now,
   172→        "updated": now,
   173→        "queue_order": [],
   174→        "deferred": [],
   175→        "skipped": {},
   176→        "active_cluster": None,
   177→        "overrides": {},
   178→        "clusters": {},
   179→        "superseded": {},
   180→        "promoted_ids": [],
   181→        "plan_start_scores": {},
   182→        "execution_log": [],
   183→        "epic_triage_meta": {},
   184→        "commit_log": [],
   185→        "uncommitted_issues": [],
   186→        "commit_tracking_branch": None,
   187→    }
   188→
   189→
   190→def ensure_plan_defaults(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   191→    """Normalize a loaded plan to ensure all keys exist.
   192→
   193→    Runtime contract is v7-only. Legacy payloads are upgraded in-place once.
   194→    """
   195→    defaults = empty_plan()
   196→    for key, value in defaults.items():
   197→        plan.setdefault(key, value)
   198→    _upgrade_plan_to_v7(plan)
   199→
   200→
   201→def triage_clusters(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Cluster]:
   202→    """Return clusters whose name starts with ``EPIC_PREFIX``."""
   203→    return {
   204→        name: cluster
   205→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
   206→        if name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX)
   207→    }
   208→
   209→
   210→def validate_plan(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   211→    """Raise ValueError when plan invariants are violated."""
   212→    if not isinstance(plan.get("version"), int):
   213→        raise ValueError("plan.version must be an int")
   214→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   215→        raise ValueError("plan.queue_order must be a list")
   216→
   217→    # No ID should appear in both queue_order and skipped
   218→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   219→    overlap = set(plan["queue_order"]) & skipped_ids
   220→    if overlap:
   221→        raise ValueError(
   222→            f"IDs cannot appear in both queue_order and skipped: {sorted(overlap)}"
   223→        )
   224→
   225→    # Validate skip entry kinds
   226→    for fid, entry in plan.get("skipped", {}).items():
   227→        kind = entry.get("kind")
   228→        if kind not in VALID_SKIP_KINDS:
   229→            raise ValueError(
   230→                f"Invalid skip kind {kind!r} for {fid}; must be one of {sorted(VALID_SKIP_KINDS)}"
   231→            )
   232→
   233→
   234→__all__ = [
   235→    "EPIC_PREFIX",
   236→    "EpicTriageMeta",
   237→    "ExecutionLogEntry",
   238→    "PLAN_VERSION",
   239→    "Cluster",
   240→    "CommitRecord",
   241→    "ItemOverride",
   242→    "LastTriageSnapshot",
   243→    "PlanModel",

   250→    "empty_plan",
   251→    "ensure_plan_defaults",
   252→    "triage_clusters",
   253→    "validate_plan",
   254→]
   255→
```
