```
   312→STATE_DIR = get_project_root() / ".desloppify"
   313→STATE_FILE = STATE_DIR / "state.json"
   314→CURRENT_VERSION = 1
   315→
   316→
   317→def utc_now() -> str:
   318→    """Return current UTC timestamp with second-level precision."""
   319→    return datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   320→
   321→
   322→def empty_state() -> StateModel:
   323→    """Return a new empty state payload."""
   324→    return {
   325→        "version": CURRENT_VERSION,
   326→        "created": utc_now(),
   327→        "last_scan": None,
   328→        "scan_count": 0,
   329→        "overall_score": 0,
   330→        "objective_score": 0,
   331→        "strict_score": 0,
   332→        "verified_strict_score": 0,
   333→        "stats": {},
   334→        "issues": {},
   335→        "scan_coverage": {},
   336→        "score_confidence": {},
   337→        "subjective_integrity": {},
   338→        "subjective_assessments": {},
   339→    }
   340→
   341→
   342→def _as_non_negative_int(value: Any, default: int = 0) -> int:
   343→    try:
   344→        parsed = int(value)
   345→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   346→        return default
   347→    return parsed if parsed >= 0 else 0
   348→
   349→
   350→def _rename_key(d: dict, old: str, new: str) -> bool:
   351→    if old not in d:
   352→        return False
   353→    d.setdefault(new, d.pop(old))
   354→    return True
   355→
   356→
   357→def migrate_state_keys(state: StateModel | dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   358→    """Migrate legacy key names in-place.
   359→
   360→    - ``"findings"`` → ``"issues"``
   361→    - ``dimension_scores[dim]["issues"]`` → ``"failing"``
   362→    """
   363→    state_dict = cast(dict[str, Any], state)
   364→    _rename_key(state_dict, "findings", "issues")
   365→
   366→    for ds in state_dict.get("dimension_scores", {}).values():
   367→        if isinstance(ds, dict):
   368→            _rename_key(ds, "issues", "failing")
   369→
   370→    for entry in state_dict.get("scan_history", []):
   371→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   372→            continue
   373→        _rename_key(entry, "raw_findings", "raw_issues")
   374→        for ds in (entry.get("dimension_scores") or {}).values():
   375→            if isinstance(ds, dict):
   376→                _rename_key(ds, "issues", "failing")
   377→
   378→
   379→def ensure_state_defaults(state: StateModel | dict) -> None:
   380→    """Normalize loose/legacy state payloads to a valid base shape in-place."""
   381→    migrate_state_keys(state)
   382→
   383→    mutable_state = cast(dict[str, Any], state)
   384→    for key, value in empty_state().items():
   385→        mutable_state.setdefault(key, value)
   386→
   387→    if not isinstance(state.get("issues"), dict):
   388→        state["issues"] = {}
   389→    if not isinstance(state.get("stats"), dict):
   390→        state["stats"] = {}
   391→    if not isinstance(state.get("scan_history"), list):
   392→        state["scan_history"] = []
   393→    if not isinstance(state.get("scan_coverage"), dict):
   394→        state["scan_coverage"] = {}
   395→    if not isinstance(state.get("score_confidence"), dict):
   396→        state["score_confidence"] = {}
   397→    if not isinstance(state.get("subjective_integrity"), dict):
   398→        state["subjective_integrity"] = {}
   399→
   400→    all_issues = state["issues"]
   401→    to_remove: list[str] = []
   402→    for issue_id, issue in all_issues.items():
   403→        if not isinstance(issue, dict):
   404→            to_remove.append(issue_id)
   405→            continue
   406→
   407→        issue.setdefault("id", issue_id)
   408→        issue.setdefault("detector", "unknown")
   409→        issue.setdefault("file", "")
   410→        issue.setdefault("tier", 3)
   411→        issue.setdefault("confidence", "low")
   412→        issue.setdefault("summary", "")
   413→        issue.setdefault("detail", {})
   414→        issue.setdefault("status", "open")
   415→        issue["status"] = canonical_issue_status(
   416→            issue.get("status"),
   417→            default="open",
   418→        )
   419→        issue.setdefault("note", None)
   420→        issue.setdefault("first_seen", state.get("created") or utc_now())
   421→        issue.setdefault("last_seen", issue["first_seen"])
   422→        issue.setdefault("resolved_at", None)
   423→        issue["reopen_count"] = _as_non_negative_int(
   424→            issue.get("reopen_count", 0), default=0
   425→        )
   426→        issue.setdefault("suppressed", False)
   427→        issue.setdefault("suppressed_at", None)
   428→        issue.setdefault("suppression_pattern", None)
   429→
   430→    for issue_id in to_remove:
   431→        all_issues.pop(issue_id, None)
   432→
   433→    for entry in state["scan_history"]:
   434→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   435→            continue
   436→        integrity = entry.get("subjective_integrity")
   437→        if integrity is not None and not isinstance(integrity, dict):
   438→            entry["subjective_integrity"] = None
   439→
   440→    state["scan_count"] = _as_non_negative_int(state.get("scan_count", 0), default=0)
   441→    return None
   442→
   443→
   444→def validate_state_invariants(state: StateModel) -> None:
   445→    """Raise ValueError when core state invariants are violated."""
   446→    if not isinstance(state.get("issues"), dict):
   447→        raise ValueError("state.issues must be a dict")
   448→    if not isinstance(state.get("stats"), dict):
   449→        raise ValueError("state.stats must be a dict")
   450→
   451→    all_issues = state["issues"]
   452→    for issue_id, issue in all_issues.items():
   453→        if not isinstance(issue, dict):
   454→            raise ValueError(f"issue {issue_id!r} must be a dict")
   455→        if issue.get("id") != issue_id:
   456→            raise ValueError(f"issue id mismatch for {issue_id!r}")
   457→        if issue.get("status") not in _ALLOWED_ISSUE_STATUSES:
   458→            raise ValueError(
   459→                f"issue {issue_id!r} has invalid status {issue.get('status')!r}"
   460→            )
   461→
   462→        tier = issue.get("tier")
   463→        if not isinstance(tier, int) or tier < 1 or tier > 4:
   464→            raise ValueError(f"issue {issue_id!r} has invalid tier {tier!r}")
   465→
   466→        reopen_count = issue.get("reopen_count")
   467→        if not isinstance(reopen_count, int) or reopen_count < 0:
   468→            raise ValueError(
   469→                f"issue {issue_id!r} has invalid reopen_count {reopen_count!r}"
   470→            )
   471→
```

```
1→"""Shared attestation and note validation helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import sys
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     8→
     9→_REQUIRED_ATTESTATION_PHRASES = ("i have actually", "not gaming")
    10→_ATTESTATION_KEYWORD_HINT = ("I have actually", "not gaming")
    11→_MIN_NOTE_LENGTH = 50
    12→
    13→
    14→def _emit_warning(message: str) -> None:
    15→    """Write resolve preflight warnings to stderr consistently."""
    16→    print(colorize(message, "yellow"), file=sys.stderr)
    17→
    18→
    19→def _missing_attestation_keywords(attestation: str | None) -> list[str]:
    20→    normalized = " ".join((attestation or "").strip().lower().split())
    21→    return [
    22→        phrase for phrase in _REQUIRED_ATTESTATION_PHRASES if phrase not in normalized
    23→    ]
    24→
    25→
    26→def validate_attestation(attestation: str | None) -> bool:
    27→    return not _missing_attestation_keywords(attestation)
    28→
    29→
    30→def show_attestation_requirement(
    31→    label: str,
    32→    attestation: str | None,
    33→    example: str,
    34→) -> None:
    35→    missing = _missing_attestation_keywords(attestation)
    36→    if not attestation:
    37→        _emit_warning(f"{label} requires --attest.")
    38→    elif missing:
    39→        missing_str = ", ".join(f"'{keyword}'" for keyword in missing)
    40→        _emit_warning(
    41→            f"{label} attestation is missing required keyword(s): {missing_str}."
    42→        )
    43→    _emit_warning(
    44→        f"Required keywords: '{_ATTESTATION_KEYWORD_HINT[0]}' and '{_ATTESTATION_KEYWORD_HINT[1]}'."
    45→    )
    46→    print(colorize(f'Example: --attest "{example}"', "dim"), file=sys.stderr)
    47→
    48→
    49→def validate_note_length(note: str | None) -> bool:
    50→    """Return True if the note meets the minimum length requirement."""
    51→    return note is not None and len(note.strip()) >= _MIN_NOTE_LENGTH
    52→
    53→
    54→def show_note_length_requirement(note: str | None) -> None:
    55→    """Emit a warning about minimum note length."""
    56→    current = len((note or "").strip())
    57→    _emit_warning(
    58→        f"Note must be at least {_MIN_NOTE_LENGTH} characters (got {current}). "
    59→        f"Describe what you actually did."
    60→    )
    61→
    62→
    63→__all__ = [
    64→    "_MIN_NOTE_LENGTH",
    65→    "show_attestation_requirement",
    66→    "show_note_length_requirement",
    67→    "validate_attestation",
    68→    "validate_note_length",
    69→]
    70→
```

```
     3→from __future__ import annotations

     5→from pathlib import Path
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→
     9→def json_default(obj: Any) -> Any:
    10→    """JSON serializer fallback for known non-JSON-native state values."""
    11→    if isinstance(obj, set):
    12→        return sorted(obj)
    13→    if isinstance(obj, Path):
    14→        return str(obj).replace("\\", "/")
    15→    if hasattr(obj, "isoformat"):
    16→        return obj.isoformat()
    17→    raise TypeError(
    18→        f"Object of type {type(obj).__name__} is not JSON serializable: {obj!r}"
    19→    )
    20→
    21→
    22→def get_overall_score(state: dict[str, Any]) -> float | None:
    23→    value = state.get("overall_score")
    24→    return float(value) if isinstance(value, int | float) else None
```

```
1→"""Central skip-kind policy for plan and CLI behavior."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→USER_SKIP_KINDS = ("temporary", "permanent", "false_positive")
     6→SYSTEM_SKIP_KINDS = ("triaged_out",)
     7→VALID_SKIP_KINDS = set(USER_SKIP_KINDS + SYSTEM_SKIP_KINDS)
     8→
     9→SKIP_KIND_LABELS = {
    10→    "temporary": "Skipped",
    11→    "permanent": "Wontfixed",
    12→    "false_positive": "Marked false positive",
    13→}
    14→
    15→SKIP_KIND_SECTION_LABELS = {
    16→    "temporary": "Skipped Temporarily",
    17→    "permanent": "Wontfix (permanent)",
    18→    "false_positive": "False Positives",
    19→}
    20→
    21→
    22→def skip_kind_from_flags(*, permanent: bool, false_positive: bool) -> str:
    23→    """Map CLI flags to a canonical skip kind."""
    24→    if false_positive:
    25→        return "false_positive"
    26→    if permanent:
    27→        return "permanent"
    28→    return "temporary"
    29→
    30→
    31→def skip_kind_requires_attestation(kind: str) -> bool:
    32→    """Return True when the skip kind requires attestation text."""
    33→    return kind in {"permanent", "false_positive"}
    34→
    35→
    36→def skip_kind_requires_note(kind: str) -> bool:
    37→    """Return True when the skip kind requires a user note."""
    38→    return kind == "permanent"
    39→
    40→
    41→def skip_kind_state_status(kind: str) -> str | None:
    42→    """Return corresponding state status, if any."""
    43→    if kind == "permanent":
    44→        return "wontfix"
    45→    if kind == "false_positive":
    46→        return "false_positive"
    47→    return None
    48→
    49→
    50→def skip_kind_needs_state_reopen(kind: str) -> bool:
    51→    """Return True when unskip should reopen state-layer status."""
    52→    return kind in {"permanent", "false_positive"}
    53→
    54→
    55→__all__ = [

    57→    "SKIP_KIND_SECTION_LABELS",
```

```
1→"""Predicates and small utilities for work queue items."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→from fnmatch import fnmatch
     7→from typing import Any
     8→
     9→from desloppify.base.enums import issue_status_tokens
    10→from desloppify.base.registry import DETECTORS
    11→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    12→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.types import WorkQueueItem
    13→
    14→ALL_STATUSES = set(issue_status_tokens(include_all=True))
    15→ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "refactor": 1, "manual_fix": 2, "reorganize": 3}
    16→ATTEST_EXAMPLE = (
    17→    "I have actually [DESCRIBE THE CONCRETE CHANGE YOU MADE] "
    18→    "and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
    19→)
    20→
    21→
    22→def detail_dict(item: WorkQueueItem | dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    23→    """Return issue detail as a dict; tolerate legacy/non-dict payloads."""
    24→    detail = item.get("detail")
    25→    return detail if isinstance(detail, dict) else {}
    26→
    27→
    28→def status_matches(item_status: str, status_filter: str) -> bool:
    29→    return status_filter == "all" or item_status == status_filter
    30→
    31→
    32→def is_subjective_issue(item: WorkQueueItem | dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
    33→    detector = item.get("detector")
    34→    if detector in {"subjective_assessment"}:
    35→        return True
    36→    if detector == "holistic_review":
    37→        return True
    38→    return False
    39→
    40→
    41→def is_review_issue(item: WorkQueueItem | dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
    42→    return item.get("detector") == "review"
    43→
    44→
    45→def is_subjective_queue_item(item: WorkQueueItem | dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
    46→    """True for subjective work items, including collapsed subjective clusters."""
    47→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
    48→        return True
    49→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    50→        members = item.get("members", [])
    51→        return bool(members) and all(
    52→            m.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" for m in members
    53→        )
    54→    return False
    55→
    56→
    57→def review_issue_weight(item: WorkQueueItem | dict[str, Any]) -> float:
    58→    """Return review issue weight aligned with issues list ordering."""
    59→    confidence = str(item.get("confidence", "low")).lower()
    60→    weight_by_confidence = {
    61→        "high": 1.0,
    62→        "medium": 0.7,
    63→        "low": 0.3,
    64→    }
    65→    weight = weight_by_confidence.get(confidence, 0.3)
    66→    if detail_dict(item).get("holistic"):
    67→        weight *= 10.0
    68→    return float(weight)
    69→
    70→
    71→def scope_matches(item: WorkQueueItem | dict[str, Any], scope: str | None) -> bool:
    72→    """Apply show-style pattern matching against a queue item."""
    73→    if not scope:
    74→        return True
    75→
    76→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    77→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    78→    filepath = item.get("file", "")
    79→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    80→    dimension = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    81→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    82→
    83→    if "*" in scope:
    84→        return any(
    85→            fnmatch(candidate, scope)
    86→            for candidate in (item_id, filepath, detector, dimension, summary)
    87→        )
    88→
    89→    if "::" in scope:
    90→        return item_id.startswith(scope)
    91→
    92→    lowered = scope.lower()
    93→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
    94→        return (
    95→            lowered in item_id.lower()
    96→            or lowered in dimension.lower()
    97→            or lowered in summary.lower()
    98→        )
    99→
   100→    # Hash suffix: 8+ hex chars matches the tail segment of a issue ID.
```

```
1→"""Plan-order and cluster-collapse helpers for work queues."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.registry import DETECTORS
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.annotations import (
     9→    get_issue_description,
    10→    get_issue_note,
    11→    get_issue_override,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.types import WorkQueueItem
    14→from desloppify.state import StateModel
    15→
    16→
    17→def new_item_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    18→    """Return issue IDs added in the most recent scan."""
    19→    scan_history = state.get("scan_history", [])
    20→    if not scan_history:
    21→        return set()
    22→    threshold = scan_history[-1].get("timestamp", "")
    23→    if not threshold:
    24→        return set()
    25→    return {
    26→        issue_id
    27→        for issue_id, issue in state.get("issues", {}).items()
    28→        if issue.get("first_seen", "") >= threshold
    29→    }
    30→
    31→
    32→def enrich_plan_metadata(items: list[WorkQueueItem], plan: dict) -> None:
    33→    """Stamp plan description, note, and cluster info from overrides."""
    34→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    35→
    36→    for item in items:
    37→        issue_id = item["id"]
    38→        description = get_issue_description(plan, issue_id)
    39→        note = get_issue_note(plan, issue_id)
    40→        override = get_issue_override(plan, issue_id)
    41→        if description:
    42→            item["plan_description"] = description
    43→        if note:
    44→            item["plan_note"] = note
    45→        if override.get("cluster"):
    46→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
    47→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
    48→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
    49→                "name": cluster_name,
    50→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
    51→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("issue_ids", [])),
    52→                "action_steps": cluster_data.get("action_steps") or [],
    53→            }
    54→
    55→
    56→def stamp_plan_sort_keys(
    57→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
    58→    plan: dict,
    59→    new_ids: set[str],
    60→) -> None:
    61→    """Stamp ``_plan_position`` and ``_is_new`` on each item.
    62→
    63→    These fields are consumed by :func:`item_sort_key` in ``ranking.py``
    64→    to produce the correct final ordering in a single sort pass.
    65→    """
    66→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    67→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    68→
    69→    position_map: dict[str, int] = {}
    70→    for idx, issue_id in enumerate(queue_order):
    71→        if issue_id not in skipped_ids:
    72→            position_map[issue_id] = idx
    73→
    74→    for item in items:
    75→        item_id = item["id"]
    76→        pos = position_map.get(item_id)
    77→        item["_plan_position"] = pos  # None if not in queue_order
    78→        item["_is_new"] = item_id in new_ids
    79→
    80→
    81→def separate_skipped(
    82→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
    83→    plan: dict,
    84→) -> tuple[list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
    85→    """Separate skipped items from the main list.
    86→
    87→    Returns ``(non_skipped, skipped)`` so callers can optionally re-append.
    88→    """
    89→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    90→    if not skipped_ids:
    91→        return items, []
    92→    non_skipped: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
    93→    skipped: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
    94→    for item in items:
    95→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
    96→            skipped.append(item)
    97→        else:
    98→            non_skipped.append(item)
    99→    return non_skipped, skipped
   100→
   101→
   102→def filter_cluster_focus(
   103→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   104→    plan: dict,
   105→    cluster: str | None,
   106→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   107→    """Filter to only cluster members when a cluster focus is active."""
   108→    effective_cluster = cluster or plan.get("active_cluster")
   109→    if not effective_cluster:
   110→        return items
   111→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
   112→    cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
   113→    cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("issue_ids", []))
   114→    if not cluster_member_ids:
   115→        return items
   116→    return [item for item in items if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids]
   117→
   118→
   119→def stamp_positions(items: list[WorkQueueItem], plan: dict) -> None:
   120→    """Stamp queue_position and plan_skipped metadata on each item."""
   121→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
   122→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
   123→
   124→    for position, item in enumerate(items):
   125→        item["queue_position"] = position + 1
   126→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
   127→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
   128→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
   129→            if skip_entry:
   130→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   131→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
   132→                if skip_reason:
   133→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
   134→
   135→
   136→def action_type_for_detector(detector: str) -> str:
   137→    """Look up the action_type for a detector from the registry."""
   138→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   139→    if meta:
   140→        return meta.action_type
   141→    return "manual_fix"
   142→
   143→
   144→def _build_cluster_meta(
   145→    cluster_name: str, members: list[WorkQueueItem], cluster_data: dict[str, Any]
   146→) -> WorkQueueItem:
   147→    """Build a cluster meta-item from its member items."""
   148→    detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   149→    action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
   150→    if "desloppify autofix" in action:
   151→        action_type = "auto_fix"
   152→    elif "desloppify move" in action:
   153→        action_type = "reorganize"
   154→    else:
   155→        action_type = action_type_for_detector(detector)
   156→        if action_type == "auto_fix" and "desloppify autofix" not in action:
   157→            action_type = "refactor"
   158→
   159→    stored_desc = cluster_data.get("description") or ""
   160→    total_in_cluster = len(cluster_data.get("issue_ids", []))
   161→    if stored_desc and total_in_cluster != len(members):
   162→        summary = stored_desc.replace(str(total_in_cluster), str(len(members)))
   163→    else:
   164→        summary = stored_desc or f"{len(members)} issues"
   165→
   166→    primary_command = cluster_data.get("action")
   167→    if not primary_command:
   168→        primary_command = f"desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10"
   169→
   170→    estimated_impact = max(
   171→        (m.get("estimated_impact", 0.0) for m in members), default=0.0
   172→    )
   173→
   174→    return {
   175→        "id": cluster_name,
   176→        "kind": "cluster",
   177→        "action_type": action_type,
   178→        "summary": summary,
   179→        "members": members,
   180→        "member_count": len(members),
   181→        "primary_command": primary_command,
   182→        "cluster_name": cluster_name,
   183→        "cluster_auto": True,
   184→        "cluster_optional": bool(cluster_data.get("optional")),
   185→        "confidence": "high",
   186→        "detector": detector,
   187→        "file": "",
   188→        "estimated_impact": estimated_impact,
   189→        "action_steps": cluster_data.get("action_steps") or [],
   190→    }
   191→
   192→
   193→def collapse_clusters(items: list[WorkQueueItem], plan: dict) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   194→    """Replace cluster member items with single cluster meta-items.
   195→
   196→    Walks the list in order: the first member of each collapsed cluster is
   197→    replaced with its meta-item, subsequent members are skipped.  This
   198→    preserves the ordering established by sort + plan-order.
   199→    """
   200→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   201→    if not clusters:
   202→        return items
   203→
   204→    fid_to_cluster: dict[str, str] = {}
   205→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   206→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   207→            continue
   208→        for issue_id in cluster.get("issue_ids", []):
   209→            fid_to_cluster[issue_id] = name
   210→
   211→    if not fid_to_cluster:
   212→        return items
   213→
   214→    # Collect members per cluster (preserving encounter order)
   215→    cluster_members: dict[str, list[WorkQueueItem]] = {}
   216→    for item in items:
   217→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   218→        if cname:
   219→            cluster_members.setdefault(cname, []).append(item)
   220→
   221→    # Build meta-items only for clusters with 2+ members in the queue
   222→    meta_items: dict[str, WorkQueueItem] = {}
   223→    for cname, members in cluster_members.items():
   224→        if len(members) < 2:
   225→            continue
   226→        meta_items[cname] = _build_cluster_meta(
   227→            cname, members, clusters.get(cname, {})
   228→        )
   229→
   230→    # Walk in order: replace first member of each collapsed cluster
   231→    # with its meta-item, skip subsequent members
   232→    seen_clusters: set[str] = set()
   233→    result: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   234→    for item in items:
   235→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   236→        if cname and cname in meta_items:
   237→            if cname not in seen_clusters:
   238→                seen_clusters.add(cname)
   239→                result.append(meta_items[cname])
   240→            # skip individual member
   241→        else:
   242→            result.append(item)
   243→    return result
   244→
   245→
   246→__all__ = [
   247→    "collapse_clusters",
   248→    "enrich_plan_metadata",
   249→    "filter_cluster_focus",
   250→    "new_item_ids",
   251→    "separate_skipped",
   252→    "stamp_plan_sort_keys",
   253→    "stamp_positions",
   254→]
   255→
```

```
   130→def triage_phase_banner(plan: PlanModel) -> str:
   131→    """Return a banner string when triage stage IDs are in the queue."""
   132→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   133→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   134→    has_triage = any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS)
   135→    if not has_triage:
   136→        return ""
   137→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   138→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   139→    completed = [s for s in ("observe", "reflect", "organize") if s in stages]
   140→    if completed:
   141→        return (
   142→            f"TRIAGE MODE ({len(completed)}/4 stages complete) — "
   143→            "complete all stages to exit. Run: desloppify plan triage"
   144→        )
   145→    return (
   146→        "TRIAGE MODE — review issues need analysis before fixing. "
   147→        "Run: desloppify plan triage"
   148→    )
```

```
1→"""Synthetic work-queue item builders and dimension scoring.
     2→
     3→Builds workflow stage items, score checkpoint items, create-plan items,
     4→subjective dimension items, and subjective score lookups.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→from typing import Any
    10→
    11→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    12→from desloppify.engine._scoring.subjective.core import DISPLAY_NAMES
    13→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
    14→    detail_dict,
    15→    slugify,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.synthetic_workflow import (
    18→    build_communicate_score_item,
    19→    build_create_plan_item,
    20→    build_import_scores_item,
    21→    build_score_checkpoint_item,
    22→)
    23→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.types import WorkQueueItem
    24→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    25→    all_subjective_entries,

    28→    unassessed_subjective_dimensions,

    30→
    31→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    32→# Dimension key normalization
    33→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    34→
    35→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
    36→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
    37→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    38→    target = cleaned.lower()
    39→
    40→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
    41→        if str(label).lower() == target:
    42→            return str(dim_key)
    43→    return slugify(cleaned)
    44→
    45→
    46→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
    47→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with issue dimension keys."""
    48→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    49→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
    50→    return {
    51→        cleaned.lower(),
    52→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
    53→        slugify(cleaned),
    54→        canonical.lower(),
    55→        slugify(canonical),
    56→    }
    57→
    58→
    59→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    60→# Subjective strict scores
    61→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    62→
    63→def subjective_strict_scores(state: StateModel | dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, float]:
    64→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    65→    if not dim_scores:
    66→        return {}
    67→
    68→    entries = all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
    69→    scores: dict[str, float] = {}
    70→    for entry in entries:
    71→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
    72→        if not name:
    73→            continue
    74→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    75→        [REDACTED](name)
    76→        aliases = _subjective_dimension_aliases(name)
    77→        for cli_key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
    78→            key = str(cli_key).strip().lower()
    79→            if not key:
    80→                continue
    81→            aliases.add(key)
    82→            aliases.add(slugify(key))
    83→        aliases.add(dim_key.lower())
    84→        aliases.add(slugify(dim_key))
    85→        for alias in aliases:
    86→            scores[alias] = strict_val
    87→    return scores
    88→
    89→
    90→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    91→# Synthetic item builders
    92→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    93→
    94→def build_triage_stage_items(plan: dict, state: dict) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
    95→    """Build synthetic work items for each ``triage::*`` stage ID in the queue.
    96→
    97→    Returns an empty list when no triage stages are pending.
    98→    """
    99→    from desloppify.app.commands.plan.triage_playbook import (
   100→        TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES,
   101→        TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS,
   102→    )
   103→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
   104→        TRIAGE_IDS,
   105→        TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS,
   106→    )
   107→
   108→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   109→    order_set = set(order)
   110→    present = order_set & TRIAGE_IDS
   111→    if not present:
   112→        return []
   113→
   114→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   115→    confirmed = set(meta.get("triage_stages", {}).keys())
   116→
   117→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   118→    open_review_count = sum(
   119→        1 for f in issues.values()
   120→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   121→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   122→    )
   123→
   124→    label_map = dict(TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS)
   125→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "commit")
   126→
   127→    items: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   128→    for sid, name in zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names, strict=False):
   129→        if sid not in present:
   130→            continue
   131→        if name in confirmed:
   132→            continue
   133→
   134→        # Compute blocked_by: dependency stages that are still in the queue
   135→        deps = TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES.get(name, set())
   136→        blocked_by = sorted(
   137→            f"triage::{dep}" for dep in deps
   138→            if f"triage::{dep}" in present and dep not in confirmed
   139→        )
   140→
   141→        cmd = f"desloppify plan triage --stage {name}"
   142→        if name == "commit":
   143→            cmd = 'desloppify plan triage --complete --strategy "..."'
   144→
   145→        item: WorkQueueItem = {
   146→            "id": sid,
   147→            "tier": 1,
   148→            "confidence": "high",
   149→            "detector": "triage",
   150→            "file": ".",
   151→            "kind": "workflow_stage",
   152→            "summary": f"Triage: {label_map.get(name, name)}",
   153→            "detail": {
   154→                "total_review_issues": open_review_count,
   155→                "stage": name,
   156→                "stage_label": label_map.get(name, name),
   157→            },
   158→            "blocked_by": blocked_by,
   159→            "is_blocked": bool(blocked_by),
   160→        }
   161→        item["primary_command"] = cmd
   162→        items.append(item)
   163→    return items
   164→
   165→
   166→def build_subjective_items(
   167→    state: dict, issues: dict, *, threshold: float = 100.0
   168→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   169→    """Create synthetic subjective work items."""
   170→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   171→    if not dim_scores:
   172→        return []
   173→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(threshold)))
   174→
   175→    subjective_entries = all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   176→    if not subjective_entries:
   177→        return []
   178→    unassessed_dims = {
   179→        str(name).strip()
   180→        for name in unassessed_subjective_dimensions(
   181→            dim_scores
   182→        )
   183→    }
   184→
   185→    # Review issues are keyed by raw dimension name (snake_case).
   186→    review_open_by_dim: dict[str, int] = {}
   187→    for issue in issues.values():
   188→        if issue.get("status") != "open":
   189→            continue
   190→        if issue.get("detector") == "review":
   191→            dim_key = str(detail_dict(issue).get("dimension", "")).strip().lower()
   192→            if dim_key:
   193→                review_open_by_dim[dim_key] = review_open_by_dim.get(dim_key, 0) + 1
   194→
   195→    items: list[WorkQueueItem] = []

   197→        cli_keys: list[str],
   198→        *,
   199→        force_review_rerun: bool = False,
   200→    ) -> str:
```

```
1→"""Cluster mutations for plan operations."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.operations_queue import move_items
     6→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import Cluster, PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
     7→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
     8→
     9→
    10→def create_cluster(
    11→    plan: PlanModel,
    12→    name: str,
    13→    description: str | None = None,
    14→    action: str | None = None,
    15→) -> Cluster:
    16→    """Create a named cluster. Raises ValueError if it already exists."""
    17→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    18→    if name.startswith("auto/"):
    19→        raise ValueError(
    20→            f"Cluster names starting with 'auto/' are reserved for auto-clusters: {name!r}"
    21→        )
    22→    if name.startswith("epic/"):
    23→        raise ValueError(
    24→            f"Cluster names starting with 'epic/' are reserved for triage epics: {name!r}"
    25→        )
    26→    if name in plan["clusters"]:
    27→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {name!r} already exists")
    28→    now = utc_now()
    29→    cluster: Cluster = {
    30→        "name": name,
    31→        "description": description,
    32→        "issue_ids": [],
    33→        "created_at": now,
    34→        "updated_at": now,
    35→        "auto": False,
    36→        "cluster_key": "",
    37→        "action": action,
    38→        "user_modified": False,
    39→    }
    40→    plan["clusters"][name] = cluster
    41→    return cluster
    42→
    43→
    44→def add_to_cluster(
    45→    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, issue_ids: list[str]
    46→) -> int:
    47→    """Add issue IDs to a cluster. Returns count added."""
    48→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    49→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
    50→    if cluster is None:
    51→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
    52→
    53→    member_ids: list[str] = cluster["issue_ids"]
    54→    count = 0
    55→    now = utc_now()
    56→    for fid in issue_ids:
    57→        if fid not in member_ids:
    58→            member_ids.append(fid)
    59→            count += 1
    60→        overrides = plan["overrides"]
    61→        if fid not in overrides:
    62→            overrides[fid] = {"issue_id": fid, "created_at": now}
    63→        overrides[fid]["cluster"] = cluster_name
    64→        overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
    65→
    66→    cluster["updated_at"] = now
    67→    return count
    68→
    69→
    70→def remove_from_cluster(
    71→    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, issue_ids: list[str]
    72→) -> int:
    73→    """Remove issue IDs from a cluster. Returns count removed."""
    74→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    75→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
    76→    if cluster is None:
    77→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
    78→
    79→    member_ids: list[str] = cluster["issue_ids"]
    80→    now = utc_now()
    81→    count = 0
    82→    for fid in issue_ids:
    83→        if fid in member_ids:
    84→            member_ids.remove(fid)
    85→            count += 1
    86→        override = plan["overrides"].get(fid)
    87→        if override and override.get("cluster") == cluster_name:
    88→            override["cluster"] = None
    89→            override["updated_at"] = now
    90→
    91→    if count > 0 and cluster.get("auto"):
    92→        cluster["user_modified"] = True
    93→
    94→    cluster["updated_at"] = now
    95→    return count
    96→
    97→
    98→def delete_cluster(plan: PlanModel, name: str) -> list[str]:
    99→    """Delete a cluster and clear cluster refs from overrides. Returns orphaned IDs."""
   100→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   101→    cluster = plan["clusters"].pop(name, None)
   102→    if cluster is None:
   103→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {name!r} does not exist")
   104→
   105→    orphaned = list(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   106→    now = utc_now()
   107→    for fid in orphaned:
   108→        override = plan["overrides"].get(fid)
   109→        if override and override.get("cluster") == name:
   110→            override["cluster"] = None
   111→            override["updated_at"] = now
   112→
   113→    if plan.get("active_cluster") == name:
   114→        plan["active_cluster"] = None
   115→
   116→    return orphaned
   117→

   120→    plan: PlanModel, source_name: str, target_name: str
```

```
1→"""Sync subjective dimensions into the plan queue.
     2→
     3→Two independent sync functions:
     4→
     5→- **sync_unscored_dimensions** — prepend never-scored (placeholder) dimensions
     6→  to the *front* of the queue unconditionally (onboarding priority).
     7→- **sync_stale_dimensions** — append stale (previously-scored) dimensions to
     8→  the *back* of the queue when no objective items remain.
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    13→
    14→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
    15→from desloppify.engine._plan import stale_policy as stale_policy_mod
    16→from desloppify.engine._plan.promoted_ids import promoted_insertion_index
    17→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    18→from desloppify.engine._plan.subjective_policy import (
    19→    NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS as _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS,
    20→    SubjectiveVisibility,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    23→
    24→SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX = "subjective::"
    25→TRIAGE_ID = "triage::pending"  # deprecated, kept for migration
    26→
    27→TRIAGE_PREFIX = "triage::"
    28→TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS = (
    29→    "triage::observe",
    30→    "triage::reflect",
    31→    "triage::organize",
    32→    "triage::commit",
    33→)
    34→TRIAGE_IDS = set(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS)
    35→WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID = "workflow::create-plan"
    36→WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID = "workflow::score-checkpoint"
    37→WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID = "workflow::import-scores"
    38→WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID = "workflow::communicate-score"
    39→WORKFLOW_PREFIX = "workflow::"
    40→SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES = ("triage::", "workflow::", "subjective::")
    41→
    42→
    43→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    44→# Result dataclasses
    45→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    46→
    47→@dataclass
    48→class StaleDimensionSyncResult:
    49→    """What changed during a stale-dimension sync."""
    50→
    51→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    52→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    53→
    54→    @property
    55→    def changes(self) -> int:
    56→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    57→
    58→
    59→@dataclass
    60→class UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
    61→    """What changed during an unscored-dimension sync."""
    62→
    63→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    64→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    65→
    66→    @property
    67→    def changes(self) -> int:
    68→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    69→
    70→
    71→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    72→# ID helpers
    73→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    74→
    75→def _current_stale_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    76→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently stale."""
    77→    return stale_policy_mod.current_stale_ids(
    78→        state,
    79→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    80→    )
    81→
    82→
    83→def current_unscored_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    84→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored (placeholder).
    85→
    86→    Checks ``subjective_assessments`` first; when that dict is empty
    87→    (common before any reviews have been run), falls through to
    88→    ``dimension_scores`` which carries placeholder metadata from scan.
    89→    """
    90→    return stale_policy_mod.current_unscored_ids(
    91→        state,
    92→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    93→    )
    94→
    95→
    96→def current_under_target_ids(
    97→    state: StateModel,
    98→    *,
    99→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
   100→) -> set[str]:
   101→    """Return ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are under target but not stale or unscored.
   102→
   103→    These are dimensions whose assessment is still current (not needing refresh)
   104→    but whose score hasn't reached the target yet.
   105→    """
   106→    return stale_policy_mod.current_under_target_ids(
   107→        state,
   108→        target_strict=target_strict,
   109→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
   110→    )
   111→
   112→
   113→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   114→# Helpers for sync_stale_dimensions / sync_unscored_dimensions
   115→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   116→
   117→def _has_objective_backlog(
   118→    state: StateModel,
   119→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
   120→) -> bool:
   121→    """Return whether an objective backlog exists (open non-subjective issues)."""
   122→    if policy is not None:
   123→        return policy.has_objective_backlog
   124→    return any(
   125→        f.get("status") == "open"
   126→        and f.get("detector") not in _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   127→        and not f.get("suppressed")
   128→        for f in state.get("issues", {}).values()
   129→    )
   130→
   131→
   132→def _prune_subjective_ids(
   133→    order: list[str],
   134→    *,
   135→    keep_ids: set[str],
   136→    pruned: list[str],
   137→) -> None:
   138→    """Remove subjective IDs from *order* that are not in *keep_ids*, appending removed to *pruned*."""
   139→    to_remove = [
   140→        fid for fid in order
   141→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   142→        and fid not in keep_ids
   143→    ]
   144→    for fid in to_remove:
   145→        order.remove(fid)
   146→        pruned.append(fid)
   147→

   149→def _inject_subjective_ids(
   150→    order: list[str],
```

```
1→"""Workflow-specific synthetic work queue item builders."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations

     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.types import WorkQueueItem
     6→
     7→
     8→def build_score_checkpoint_item(plan: dict, state: dict) -> WorkQueueItem | None:
     9→    """Build a synthetic work item for ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` if queued."""
    10→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID
    11→
    12→    if WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
    13→        return None
    14→
    15→    from desloppify import state as state_mod
    16→
    17→    snapshot = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    18→    strict = snapshot.strict if snapshot.strict is not None else 0.0
    19→    plan_start = (plan.get("plan_start_scores") or {}).get("strict")
    20→    delta = round(strict - plan_start, 1) if plan_start is not None else None
    21→    delta_str = f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})" if delta else ""
    22→
    23→    return {
    24→        "id": WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID,
    25→        "tier": 1,
    26→        "confidence": "high",
    27→        "detector": "workflow",
```

```
150→    order: list[str],
   151→    plan: PlanModel,
   152→    *,
   153→    inject_ids: set[str],
   154→    to_front: bool,
   155→    injected: list[str],
   156→) -> None:
   157→    """Inject subjective IDs into *order* if not already present.
   158→
   159→    When *to_front* is True, inserts after promoted items (front of queue).
   160→    Otherwise appends to the back.
   161→    """
   162→    existing = set(order)
   163→    if to_front:
   164→        insert_at = promoted_insertion_index(order, plan)
   165→        for sid in reversed(sorted(inject_ids)):
   166→            if sid not in existing:
   167→                order.insert(insert_at, sid)
   168→                injected.append(sid)
   169→    else:
   170→        for sid in sorted(inject_ids):
   171→            if sid not in existing:
   172→                order.append(sid)
   173→                injected.append(sid)
   174→
   175→
   176→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   177→# Helpers for sync_triage_needed
   178→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   179→
   180→def _open_review_issue_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   181→    """Return set of open issue IDs whose detector is 'review' or 'concerns'."""
   182→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   183→    return {
   184→        fid for fid, f in issues.items()
   185→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   186→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   187→    }
   188→
   189→
   190→def _new_review_ids_since_triage(
   191→    state: StateModel,
   192→    meta: dict,
   193→) -> set[str]:
   194→    """Return review issue IDs that are new since the last triage."""
   195→    current_review_ids = _open_review_issue_ids(state)
   196→    triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
   197→    return current_review_ids - triaged_ids
   198→
   199→
   200→def _prune_all_triage_stages(order: list[str]) -> None:
   201→    """Remove all ``triage::*`` stage IDs from *order*."""
   202→    for sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:
   203→        while sid in order:
   204→            order.remove(sid)
   205→
   206→
   207→def _inject_pending_triage_stages(
   208→    order: list[str],
   209→    plan: PlanModel,
   210→    confirmed: set[str],
   211→) -> bool:
   212→    """Inject triage stages for pending (unconfirmed) items.
   213→
   214→    Returns True if any stages were injected.
   215→    """
   216→    insert_at = promoted_insertion_index(order, plan)
   217→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "commit")
   218→    existing = set(order)
   219→    injected_count = 0
   220→    for sid, name in zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names, strict=False):
   221→        if name not in confirmed and sid not in existing:
   222→            order.insert(insert_at + injected_count, sid)
   223→            injected_count += 1
   224→    return injected_count > 0
   225→
   226→
   227→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   228→# Unscored dimension sync (front of queue, unconditional)
   229→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   230→
   231→def sync_unscored_dimensions(
   232→    plan: PlanModel,
   233→    state: StateModel,
   234→) -> UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
   235→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions.
   236→
   237→    1. **Prune** — remove ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are
   238→       no longer unscored AND not stale (avoids pruning stale IDs — that is
   239→       ``sync_stale_dimensions``' responsibility).
   240→    2. **Inject** — unconditionally prepend currently-unscored IDs to the
   241→       *front* of ``queue_order`` so initial reviews are the first priority.
   242→    """
   243→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   244→    result = UnscoredDimensionSyncResult()
   245→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   246→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   247→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   248→
   249→    # --- Cleanup: prune subjective IDs that are no longer unscored --------
   250→    # Only prune IDs that are neither unscored nor stale (stale sync owns those).
   251→    _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=unscored_ids | stale_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   252→
   253→    # --- Inject: prepend unscored IDs after any promoted items -------------
   254→    _inject_subjective_ids(order, plan, inject_ids=unscored_ids, to_front=True, injected=result.injected)
   255→
   256→    return result
   257→
   258→
   259→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   260→# Stale dimension sync (back of queue, conditional)
   261→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   262→
   263→def sync_stale_dimensions(
   264→    plan: PlanModel,
   265→    state: StateModel,
   266→    *,
   267→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   268→    cycle_just_completed: bool = False,
   269→) -> StaleDimensionSyncResult:
   270→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with stale and under-target subjective dimensions.
   271→
   272→    1. Remove any ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are no
   273→       longer stale/under-target and not unscored (avoids pruning IDs owned
   274→       by ``sync_unscored_dimensions``).
   275→    2. Inject stale and under-target dimension IDs when either:
   276→       a. No objective items remain (mid-cycle: append to back), OR
   277→       b. A cycle just completed (post-cycle: insert at front so subjective
   278→          review takes priority over new objective issues).
   279→    """
   280→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   281→    result = StaleDimensionSyncResult()
   282→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   283→    under_target_ids = current_under_target_ids(state)
   284→    injectable_ids = stale_ids | under_target_ids
   285→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   286→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   287→
   288→    # --- Cleanup: prune resolved subjective IDs --------------------------
   289→    # Only prune IDs that are no longer injectable and not unscored.
   290→    _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=injectable_ids | unscored_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   291→
   292→    # --- Inject or evict stale + under-target dimensions -----------------
   293→    has_real_items = _has_objective_backlog(state, policy)
   294→    should_inject = not has_real_items or cycle_just_completed
   295→
   296→    if not should_inject:
   297→        # Mid-cycle with objective backlog: evict any stale/under-target IDs
   298→        # that are present in the queue.  They may have been grandfathered from
   299→        # the unscored phase and should not be visible until the objective
   300→        # backlog clears or a cycle completes.
   301→        to_evict = [
   302→            fid for fid in order
   303→            if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   304→            and fid in injectable_ids
   305→        ]
   306→        for fid in to_evict:
   307→            order.remove(fid)
   308→            result.pruned.append(fid)
   309→
   310→    if should_inject and injectable_ids:
   311→        to_front = cycle_just_completed and has_real_items
   312→        _inject_subjective_ids(order, plan, inject_ids=injectable_ids, to_front=to_front, injected=result.injected)
   313→
   314→    return result
   315→
   316→
   317→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   318→# Triage snapshot hash + sync
   319→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   320→
   321→def review_issue_snapshot_hash(state: StateModel) -> str:
   322→    """Hash open review issue IDs to detect changes.
   323→
   324→    Returns empty string when there are no open review issues.
   325→    """
   326→    return stale_policy_mod.review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   327→
   328→
   329→@dataclass
   330→class TriageSyncResult:
   331→    """What changed during a triage sync."""
   332→
   333→    injected: bool = False
   334→    pruned: bool = False
   335→
   336→    @property
   337→    def changes(self) -> int:
   338→        return int(self.injected) + int(self.pruned)
   339→
   340→
   341→def sync_triage_needed(
   342→    plan: PlanModel,
   343→    state: StateModel,
   344→) -> TriageSyncResult:
   345→    """Inject 4 triage stage IDs at front of queue when review issues change.
   346→
   347→    Only injects stages not already confirmed in ``epic_triage_meta``.
   348→
   349→    When stages are already present but all new issues have been resolved
```

```
413→def sync_score_checkpoint_needed(
   414→    plan: PlanModel,
   415→    state: StateModel,
   416→    *,
   417→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   418→) -> ScoreCheckpointSyncResult:
   419→    """Inject ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` when all initial reviews complete.
   420→
   421→    Injects when:
   422→    - No unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain
   423→    - ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` is not already in the queue
   424→
   425→    Positioned after subjective items but before triage/create-plan
   426→    so the user sees their updated strict score right after reviews finish.
   427→    """
   428→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   429→    result = ScoreCheckpointSyncResult()
   430→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   431→
   432→    if WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID in order:
   433→        return result
   434→
   435→    # Check that no unscored dimensions remain
   436→    if policy is not None:
   437→        if policy.unscored_ids:
   438→            return result
   439→    else:
   440→        unscored = current_unscored_ids(state)
   441→        if unscored:
   442→            return result
   443→
   444→    # Insert after any subjective items, before triage/workflow/issues
   445→    insert_at = 0
   446→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   447→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX):
   448→            insert_at = i + 1
   449→    order.insert(insert_at, WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID)
   450→    result.injected = True
   451→    return result
   452→
   453→
   454→@dataclass
   455→class CreatePlanSyncResult:
   456→    """What changed during a create-plan sync."""
   457→
   458→    injected: bool = False
   459→
   460→    @property
   461→    def changes(self) -> int:
   462→        return int(self.injected)
   463→
   464→
   465→def sync_create_plan_needed(
   466→    plan: PlanModel,
   467→    state: StateModel,
   468→    *,
   469→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   470→) -> CreatePlanSyncResult:
   471→    """Inject ``workflow::create-plan`` when reviews complete + objective backlog exists.
   472→
   473→    Only injects when:
   474→    - No unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain
   475→    - At least one objective issue exists
   476→    - ``workflow::create-plan`` is not already in the queue
   477→    - No triage stages are pending
   478→    """
   479→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   480→    result = CreatePlanSyncResult()
   481→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   482→
   483→    if WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID in order:
   484→        return result
   485→
   486→    # Don't inject if triage stages are pending
   487→    if any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS):
   488→        return result
   489→
   490→    # Check that no unscored dimensions remain
   491→    if policy is not None:
   492→        if policy.unscored_ids:
   493→            return result
   494→        has_objective = policy.has_objective_backlog
   495→    else:
   496→        unscored = current_unscored_ids(state)
   497→        if unscored:
   498→            return result
   499→        issues = state.get("issues", {})
   500→        has_objective = any(
   501→            f.get("status") == "open"
   502→            and f.get("detector") not in _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   503→            for f in issues.values()
   504→        )
   505→    if not has_objective:
   506→        return result
   507→
   508→    # Insert after any subjective/workflow items, at the end of the
   509→    # synthetic block (so create-plan comes after score-checkpoint).
   510→    insert_at = 0
   511→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   512→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) or fid.startswith(TRIAGE_PREFIX) or fid.startswith(WORKFLOW_PREFIX):
   513→            insert_at = i + 1
   514→    order.insert(insert_at, WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID)
   515→    result.injected = True
   516→    return result
   517→
   518→
   519→def compute_new_issue_ids(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   520→    """Return the set of open review/concerns issue IDs added since last triage.
   521→
   522→    Returns an empty set when no prior triage has recorded ``triaged_ids``.
   523→    """
   524→    return stale_policy_mod.compute_new_issue_ids(plan, state)
   525→
   526→
   527→def is_triage_stale(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> bool:
   528→    """Side-effect-free check: is triage needed?
   529→
   530→    Returns True when genuinely *new* review issues appeared since the
   531→    last triage.  Triage stage IDs being in the queue alone is not
   532→    sufficient — the new issues that triggered injection may have been
   533→    resolved since then.
   534→
   535→    When issues are merely resolved (current IDs are a subset of
   536→    previously triaged IDs), triage is NOT stale — the user is working
   537→    through the plan.
   538→    """
   539→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   540→    return stale_policy_mod.is_triage_stale(plan, state, triage_ids=TRIAGE_IDS)
   541→
   542→
```

```
1→"""Pure policy helpers for stale/unscored subjective planning decisions."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import hashlib
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
     8→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
     9→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import all_subjective_entries
    11→
    12→_REVIEW_DETECTORS = ("review", "concerns")
    13→
    14→
    15→def current_stale_ids(
    16→    state: StateModel,
    17→    *,
    18→    subjective_prefix: str = "subjective::",
    19→) -> set[str]:
    20→    """Return ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently stale."""
    21→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    22→    if not dim_scores:
    23→        return set()
    24→
    25→    stale: set[str] = set()
    26→    for entry in all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
    27→        if not entry.get("stale"):
    28→            continue
    29→        dim_key = entry.get("dimension_key", "")
    30→        if dim_key:
    31→            stale.add(f"{subjective_prefix}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    32→    return stale
    33→
    34→
    35→def current_unscored_ids(
    36→    state: StateModel,
    37→    *,
    38→    subjective_prefix: str = "subjective::",
    39→) -> set[str]:
    40→    """Return ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored."""
    41→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    42→    if isinstance(assessments, dict) and assessments:
    43→        unscored: set[str] = set()
    44→        for dim_key, payload in assessments.items():
    45→            if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    46→                continue
    47→            if not payload.get("placeholder"):
    48→                continue
    49→            if dim_key:
    50→                unscored.add(f"{subjective_prefix}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    51→        return unscored
    52→
    53→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    54→    if not dim_scores:
    55→        return set()
    56→
    57→    unscored = set()
    58→    for data in dim_scores.values():
    59→        if not isinstance(data, dict):
    60→            continue
    61→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
    62→        meta = detectors.get("subjective_assessment")
    63→        if not isinstance(meta, dict):
    64→            continue
    65→        if not meta.get("placeholder"):
    66→            continue
    67→        dim_key = meta.get("dimension_key", "")
    68→        if dim_key:
    69→            unscored.add(f"{subjective_prefix}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    70→    return unscored
    71→
    72→
    73→def current_under_target_ids(
    74→    state: StateModel,
    75→    *,
    76→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    77→    subjective_prefix: str = "subjective::",
    78→) -> set[str]:
    79→    """Return under-target subjective IDs that are neither stale nor unscored."""
    80→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    81→    if not dim_scores:
    82→        return set()
    83→
    84→    stale_ids = current_stale_ids(state, subjective_prefix=subjective_prefix)
    85→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state, subjective_prefix=subjective_prefix)
    86→
    87→    under_target: set[str] = set()
    88→    for entry in all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
    89→        if entry.get("placeholder") or entry.get("stale"):
    90→            continue
    91→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    92→        if strict_val >= target_strict:
    93→            continue
    94→        dim_key = entry.get("dimension_key", "")
    95→        if not dim_key:
    96→            continue
    97→        item_id = f"{subjective_prefix}{slugify(dim_key)}"
    98→        if item_id not in stale_ids and item_id not in unscored_ids:
    99→            under_target.add(item_id)
   100→    return under_target
   101→
   102→
   103→def review_issue_snapshot_hash(state: StateModel) -> str:
   104→    """Hash open review/concerns issue IDs to detect triage-relevant changes."""
   105→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   106→    review_ids = sorted(
   107→        issue_id
   108→        for issue_id, issue in issues.items()
   109→        if issue.get("status") == "open" and issue.get("detector") in _REVIEW_DETECTORS
   110→    )
   111→    if not review_ids:
   112→        return ""
   113→    return hashlib.sha256("|".join(review_ids).encode()).hexdigest()[:16]
   114→
   115→
   116→def compute_new_issue_ids(plan: dict, state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   117→    """Return open review/concerns IDs that appeared since the last triage."""
   118→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   119→    triaged = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
   120→    current = {
   121→        issue_id
   122→        for issue_id, issue in state.get("issues", {}).items()
   123→        if issue.get("status") == "open" and issue.get("detector") in _REVIEW_DETECTORS
   124→    }
   125→    return current - triaged if triaged else set()
   126→
   127→
   128→def is_triage_stale(
   129→    plan: dict,
   130→    state: StateModel,
   131→    *,
   132→    triage_ids: set[str] | frozenset[str] = frozenset(),
   133→) -> bool:
   134→    """Return True when triage should run because review work has changed."""
   135→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   136→
   137→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   138→    current_review_ids = {
   139→        issue_id
   140→        for issue_id, issue in issues.items()
   141→        if issue.get("status") == "open" and issue.get("detector") in _REVIEW_DETECTORS
   142→    }
   143→    triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
   144→    new_since_triage = current_review_ids - triaged_ids
   145→    if new_since_triage:
   146→        return True
   147→
   148→    confirmed = set(meta.get("triage_stages", {}).keys())
   149→    if confirmed:
   150→        order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   151→        if order & set(triage_ids):
   152→            return True
   153→    return False
   154→
   155→
   156→__all__ = [
   157→    "compute_new_issue_ids",
   158→    "current_stale_ids",
   159→    "current_under_target_ids",
   160→    "current_unscored_ids",
   161→    "is_triage_stale",
   162→    "review_issue_snapshot_hash",
   163→]
   164→
```

## Summary: Validation Patterns in Desloppify

Based on my thorough exploration of the desloppify source code, here's a complete understanding of the validation patterns used for gating workflow steps:

### **1. Core Validation Functions**

Located in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/helpers/attestation.py`:

```python
_REQUIRED_ATTESTATION_PHRASES = ("i have actually", "not gaming")
_MIN_NOTE_LENGTH = 50

def validate_attestation(attestation: str | None) -> bool:
    """Check if both required keywords are present"""
    normalized = " ".join((attestation or "").strip().lower().split())
    return all(phrase in normalized for phrase in _REQUIRED_ATTESTATION_PHRASES)

def validate_note_length(note: str | None) -> bool:
    """Return True if note meets minimum 50-character requirement"""
    return note is not None and len(note.strip()) >= _MIN_NOTE_LENGTH
```

**Key Pattern**: Uses keyword matching (case-insensitive, space-collapsed) rather than regex. The attestation must contain BOTH "i have actually" AND "not gaming" as exact substrings.

### **2. Skip Kind Policy (Validation Rules by Type)**

Located in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/skip_policy.py`:

```python
USER_SKIP_KINDS = ("temporary", "permanent", "false_positive")

def skip_kind_requires_attestation(kind: str) -> bool:
    """Return True when the skip kind requires attestation text"""
    return kind in {"permanent", "false_positive"}

def skip_kind_requires_note(kind: str) -> bool:
    """Return True when the skip kind requires a user note"""
    return kind == "permanent"

def skip_kind_state_status(kind: str) -> str | None:
    """Map to state layer status (permanent→wontfix, false_positive→false_positive, temporary→None)"""
```

**Validation Chain for Skip Operations** (from `override_handlers.py`):
- Temporary skip: no attestation/note required
- False positive: requires attestation only
- Permanent (wontfix): requires BOTH attestation AND note

### **3. Plan Resolve Command Validation Chain**

In `cmd_plan_resolve()` at `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/override_handlers.py`:

```python
def cmd_plan_resolve(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    # 1. Auto-generate attestation from --confirm flag
    if getattr(args, "confirm", False):
        if not note:
            return  # Error
        attestation = f"I have actually {note} and I am not gaming the score."
        args.attest = attestation

    # 2. Handle synthetic IDs (triage::*, workflow::*, subjective::*)
    synthetic_ids, real_patterns = _resolve_synthetic_ids(patterns)
    if synthetic_ids:
        plan = load_plan()
        # 3. CRITICAL: Validate triage dependency chain
        blocked_map = _blocked_triage_stages(plan)
        for sid in synthetic_ids:
            if sid in blocked_map:
                deps = ", ".join(b.replace("triage::", "") for b in blocked_map[sid])
                print(f"Cannot resolve {sid} — blocked by: {deps}")
                # 4. Check for --force-resolve flag to bypass
                if not getattr(args, "force_resolve", False):
                    return

    # 5. Validate note and attestation (pre-delegation)
    if not validate_note_length(note):
        return  # Error with hint
    if not validate_attestation(attestation):
        return  # Error with hint

    # 6. Cluster completion guard: block bulk-completing small clusters
    if _check_cluster_guard(patterns, plan, state):
        return
```

### **4. Triage Stage Dependency Model**

From `triage_playbook.py`:

```python
TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES: dict[str, set[str]] = {
    "observe": set(),           # No dependencies
    "reflect": {"observe"},     # Requires observe → confirmed
    "organize": {"reflect"},    # Requires reflect → confirmed
    "commit": {"organize"},     # Requires organize → confirmed
}
```

The `_blocked_triage_stages()` function checks:
- If a stage is in the queue (queue_order)
- If it's already confirmed (epic_triage_meta.triage_stages)
- All unmet dependencies block the stage from being resolved

### **5. Triage Confirmation Attestation Requirements**

Located in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/confirmations.py`:

```python
_MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN = 80

def _validate_attestation(
    attestation: str,
    stage: str,
    *,
    dimensions: list[str] | None = None,
    cluster_names: list[str] | None = None,
) -> str | None:
    """Return error message if attestation doesn't reference required data"""
    text = attestation.lower()

    if stage == "observe":
        # Must mention at least one dimension from the summary
        if dimensions:
            found = [d for d in dimensions if d.lower().replace("_", " ") in text]
            if not found:
                return f"Attestation must reference at least one dimension..."

    elif stage == "reflect":
        # Must mention dimension OR cluster name
        refs = [d for d in dimensions if d.lower() in text]
        refs.extend(n for n in cluster_names if n.lower() in text)
        if not refs:
            return f"Attestation must reference at least one dimension or cluster..."

    elif stage == "organize":
        # Must mention at least one cluster name
        if cluster_names:
            found = [n for n in cluster_names if n.lower() in text]
            if not found:
                return f"Attestation must reference at least one cluster..."
```

**Key Points**:
- Triage attestations are longer (80 chars minimum vs 50 for regular notes)
- Stage-specific validation: each stage validates attestation references actual data
- Auto-confirmation is available: can auto-confirm observe/reflect inline with `--attestation` if validation passes

### **6. Synthetic Item IDs (Workflow Checkpoints)**

From `synthetic_workflow.py` and `stale_dimensions.py`:

```python
WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID = "workflow::score-checkpoint"
WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID = "workflow::create-plan"
WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID = "workflow::import-scores"
WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID = "workflow::communicate-score"

# Score checkpoint requires:
# desloppify plan resolve "workflow::score-checkpoint" --note "..." --confirm

# Create plan requires:
# desloppify plan resolve "workflow::create-plan" --note "..." --confirm
```

These are synthetic items that appear in the queue when certain conditions are met. They **use the same validation chain as regular resolve** (note length + attestation).

### **7. Force-Resolve Bypass Pattern**

Located in argparse setup (`parser_groups_plan_impl.py`):

```python
p_resolve.add_argument(
    "--force-resolve",
    action="store_true",
    default=False,
    dest="force_resolve",
    help="Bypass triage guardrail when new issues are pending triage",
)
```

**Usage**: `desloppify plan resolve <id> --force-resolve --note "..." --confirm`

This **only bypasses** the triage dependency check (`_blocked_triage_stages`), not the attestation/note validation.

### **8. CLI Parser Setup**

The complete argparse chain is in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/cli_support/parser_groups_plan_impl.py`:

Key flags for `plan resolve`:
- `--note`: Free-form explanation (50+ chars)
- `--attest`: Attestation with keywords (required unless `--confirm`)
- `--confirm`: Auto-generate attestation from note
- `--force-resolve`: Bypass triage dependency check

---

### **Validation Chain Summary for Workflow::score-checkpoint and workflow::create-plan**

Both items are synthetic and **must go through the same `cmd_plan_resolve` chain**:

1. **Detect synthetic ID** → separate from real patterns
2. **Check triage dependencies** → if blocked, require `--force-resolve`
3. **Validate note length** → minimum 50 characters
4. **Validate attestation** → must contain both "i have actually" and "not gaming"
5. **Log the resolution** → record in plan history
6. **Delegate to cmd_resolve** → for state update and score movement

**To enforce gating on these workflow items**, you would:
- Block resolve if triage stages are pending (unless `--force-resolve`)
- Require non-empty note/attestation
- Reject missing keyword phrases
- Show helpful error messages with examples (`ATTEST_EXAMPLE` from helpers.py)

The pattern is **consistent across all resolve operations**: same validation functions, same error handling, same CLI flags.

```
def validate_plan(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    """Raise ValueError when plan invariants are violated."""
    if not isinstance(plan.get("version"), int):
        raise ValueError("plan.version must be an int")
    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
        raise ValueError("plan.queue_order must be a list")

    # No ID should appear in both queue_order and skipped
    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    overlap = set(plan["queue_order"]) & skipped_ids
    if overlap:
        raise ValueError(
            f"IDs cannot appear in both queue_order and skipped: {sorted(overlap)}"
        )

    # Validate skip entry kinds
    for fid, entry in plan.get("skipped", {}).items():
        kind = entry.get("kind")
        if kind not in VALID_SKIP_KINDS:
            raise ValueError(
                f"Invalid skip kind {kind!r} for {fid}; must be one of {sorted(VALID_SKIP_KINDS)}"
            )


__all__ = [
    "EPIC_PREFIX",
    "EpicTriageMeta",
    "ExecutionLogEntry",
    "PLAN_VERSION",
    "Cluster",
    "CommitRecord",
```

## Summary: Desloppify State & Plan API for Validation

### 1. **`score_snapshot()` Function**

**Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/state.py` (lines 72-79)

**Returns a `ScoreSnapshot` NamedTuple with four canonical scores:**

```python
class ScoreSnapshot(NamedTuple):
    """All four canonical scores from a single state dict."""
    overall: float | None
    objective: float | None
    strict: float | None
    verified: float | None

def score_snapshot(state: StateModel) -> ScoreSnapshot:
    """Load all four canonical scores from *state* in one call."""
    return ScoreSnapshot(
        overall=get_overall_score(state),
        objective=get_objective_score(state),
        strict=get_strict_score(state),
        verified=get_verified_strict_score(state),
    )
```

**The underlying accessors** (in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_state/schema_scores.py`):
- `get_overall_score(state)` → reads `state["overall_score"]`
- `get_objective_score(state)` → reads `state["objective_score"]`
- `get_strict_score(state)` → reads `state["strict_score"]`
- `get_verified_strict_score(state)` → reads `state["verified_strict_score"]`

All return `float | None` (safely handles missing/invalid values).
