### 2. **Plan Model Structure**

**Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/schema.py` (lines 145-163)

**PlanModel TypedDict contains:**

```python
class PlanModel(TypedDict, total=False):
    version: Required[int]                                  # Plan version (currently 7)
    created: Required[str]                                  # ISO timestamp
    updated: Required[str]                                  # ISO timestamp
    queue_order: list[str]                                  # Ordered list of issue IDs to work on
    deferred: list[str]                                     # Legacy, kept empty for migration compat
    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry]                          # Issues marked skip/temporary/permanent
    active_cluster: str | None                              # Currently focused cluster (if any)
    overrides: dict[str, ItemOverride]                      # Per-issue annotations (description, note, cluster)
    clusters: dict[str, Cluster]                            # Named/auto clusters for issue groups
    superseded: dict[str, SupersededEntry]                 # Remapped/dismissed issues
    promoted_ids: list[str]                                 # Issues user explicitly positioned
    plan_start_scores: PlanStartScores                      # Frozen scores at plan cycle start
    execution_log: list[ExecutionLogEntry]                 # Action history (done, skip, resolve, etc.)
    epic_triage_meta: EpicTriageMeta                        # Triage workflow metadata
    commit_log: list[CommitRecord]                          # Commit tracking history
    uncommitted_issues: list[str]                           # Issues since last commit record
    commit_tracking_branch: str | None                      # Branch for commit tracking
```

### 3. **Cluster Storage & Structure**

**Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/schema.py` (lines 42-63)

**Cluster TypedDict:**

```python
class Cluster(TypedDict, total=False):
    name: Required[str]                # Cluster name (auto/ or epic/ or manual)
    description: str | None            # Human-readable summary
    issue_ids: list[str]               # Members in this cluster
    created_at: str                    # ISO timestamp
    updated_at: str                    # Last modified time
    auto: bool                         # True if auto-generated, False if manual
    cluster_key: str                   # Deterministic grouping key (for regeneration)
    action: str | None                 # Primary resolution command/guidance
    user_modified: bool                # True when user manually edits membership
    optional: bool                     # Can be skipped if desired
    thesis: str                        # Root cause analysis
    direction: str                     # How to resolve (e.g., "delete", "merge", "inline")
    root_cause: str                    # Why this cluster exists
    supersedes: list[str]              # Earlier cluster IDs this replaces
    dismissed: list[str]               # Sub-clusters that were consolidated
    agent_safe: bool                   # Whether agent can auto-resolve
    dependency_order: int              # Execution order when multi-cluster
    action_steps: list[str]            # Step-by-step resolution guide
    source_clusters: list[str]         # Parent clusters (triage/epic only)
    status: str                        # Cluster lifecycle status
    triage_version: int                # Triage epoch/version
```

**Stored in:** `plan["clusters"]` as `dict[str, Cluster]`

**API functions** (in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/operations_cluster.py`):
- `create_cluster(plan, name, description, action)` → creates a new Cluster
- `add_to_cluster(plan, cluster_name, issue_ids)` → adds issues, updates overrides
- `remove_from_cluster(plan, cluster_name, issue_ids)` → removes issues
- `delete_cluster(plan, name)` → deletes cluster, clears refs
- `merge_clusters(plan, source_name, target_name)` → combines two clusters

### 4. **PlanStartScores: Snapshot at Plan Cycle Start**

**Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/schema.py` (lines 97-104)

**PlanStartScores TypedDict:**

```python
class PlanStartScores(TypedDict, total=False):
    """Frozen score snapshot captured when a plan cycle starts."""
    strict: float
    overall: float
    objective: float
    verified: float
    reset: bool  # Sentinel (True) = next scan should seed real scores
```

**Stored in:** `plan["plan_start_scores"]`

**Usage:** Used to compute score deltas during commit tracking (see `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/commit_tracking.py`, line 22):
- Compare `plan["plan_start_scores"]` against current `state` scores
- Shows progress within a plan cycle

### 5. **State Scores & Dimension Scores**

**Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_state/schema.py` (lines 116-128, 259-292)

**StateModel contains:**

```python
class StateModel(TypedDict, total=False):
    # Core state fields:
    version: Required[int]
    created: Required[str]
    last_scan: Required[str | None]
    scan_count: Required[int]
    # The four canonical scores:
    overall_score: Required[float]      # Combined objective + subjective
    objective_score: Required[float]    # Objective detectors only
    strict_score: Required[float]       # High-confidence score (tier 1-2)
    verified_strict_score: Required[float]  # Verified/attested score
    # Issue data:
    stats: Required[StateStats]         # Aggregates by status/tier
    issues: Required[dict[str, Issue]]  # All discovered issues
    # Dimension-level scoring:
    dimension_scores: dict[str, DimensionScore]  # Per-dimension metrics
    # History:
    scan_history: list[ScanHistoryEntry]  # Historical scores & diffs
    # Assessment/integrity:
    subjective_integrity: Required[SubjectiveIntegrity]  # Anti-gaming metadata
    subjective_assessments: Required[dict[str, SubjectiveAssessment]]  # Review dimensions
    # ... other fields (review_cache, attestation_log, etc.)
```

**DimensionScore per dimension:**

```python
class DimensionScore(TypedDict, total=False):
    score: float                        # Overall dimension score
    strict: float                       # High-confidence score for this dimension
    verified_strict_score: float        # Verified score
    checks: int                         # Total checks in this dimension
    failing: int                        # Failing checks
    tier: int                           # Minimum tier of issues affecting score
    carried_forward: bool               # Score from previous scan?
    detectors: dict[str, Any]          # Per-detector metadata
    coverage_status: str                # Coverage assessment
    coverage_confidence: float          # How confident in coverage?
    coverage_impacts: list[dict[str, Any]]  # Why coverage affects score
```

### 6. **Queue Order & Validation APIs**

**Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/schema.py` (lines 210-232)

**Plan Validation:**

```python
def validate_plan(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    """Raise ValueError when plan invariants are violated."""
    if not isinstance(plan.get("version"), int):
        raise ValueError("plan.version must be an int")
    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
        raise ValueError("plan.queue_order must be a list")

    # No ID should appear in both queue_order and skipped
    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    overlap = set(plan["queue_order"]) & skipped_ids
    if overlap:
        raise ValueError(
            f"IDs cannot appear in both queue_order and skipped: {sorted(overlap)}"
        )

    # Validate skip entry kinds
    for fid, entry in plan.get("skipped", {}).items():
        kind = entry.get("kind")
        if kind not in VALID_SKIP_KINDS:
            raise ValueError(
                f"Invalid skip kind {kind!r} for {fid}; must be one of {sorted(VALID_SKIP_KINDS)}"
            )
```

**Valid skip kinds:** `"temporary"`, `"permanent"`, `"false_positive"` (from `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/skip_policy.py`)

```python
def sync_score_checkpoint_needed(
    plan: PlanModel,
    state: StateModel,
    *,
    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
) -> ScoreCheckpointSyncResult:
    """Inject ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` when all initial reviews complete.
    
    Injects when:
    - No unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain
    - ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` is not already in the queue
    
    Returns ScoreCheckpointSyncResult(injected: bool)
    """
```

**For `workflow::create-plan`:**

```python
def sync_create_plan_needed(
    plan: PlanModel,
    state: StateModel,
    *,
    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
) -> CreatePlanSyncResult:
    """Inject ``workflow::create-plan`` when reviews complete + objective backlog exists.
    
    Only injects when:
    - No unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain
    - At least one objective issue exists
    - ``workflow::create-plan`` is not already in the queue
    - No triage stages are pending
    
    Returns CreatePlanSyncResult(injected: bool)
    """
```

```python
def enrich_plan_metadata(items: list[WorkQueueItem], plan: dict) -> None:
    """Check cluster membership & stamp plan cluster info on items."""
    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    for item in items:
        override = get_issue_override(plan, item_id)
        if override.get("cluster"):
            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
            # Verify cluster exists, validate issue_ids membership

def filter_cluster_focus(items: list[WorkQueueItem], plan: dict, cluster: str | None) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
    """Filter to only cluster members when a cluster focus is active."""
    effective_cluster = cluster or plan.get("active_cluster")
    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
    cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("issue_ids", []))
    # Validate cluster exists and has members

def collapse_clusters(items: list[WorkQueueItem], plan: dict) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
    """Replace cluster member items with single cluster meta-items.
    
    Validates:
    - Only auto clusters are collapsed (cluster.get("auto") == True)
    - Cluster has 2+ members to be meta-item-ized
    - All members exist in issue list
    """
```

## **Key Takeaways for Agents:**

1. **For `workflow::score-checkpoint` resolution:**
   - Check `current_unscored_ids(state)` — if empty, checkpoint can be injected
   - Verify `state["strict_score"]` is a valid float
   - Ensure no placeholder dimensions remain in `state["subjective_assessments"]`

2. **For `workflow::create-plan` resolution:**
   - Verify no unscored dimensions (`current_unscored_ids()` must be empty)
   - Verify objective backlog exists (open issues with non-subjective detectors)
   - Ensure no triage stages (`triage::*`) are in `plan["queue_order"]`
   - Check that plan version is valid and queue is consistent

3. **For cluster validation:**
   - Verify clusters exist in `plan["clusters"]` before accessing
   - Check that issue IDs in `cluster["issue_ids"]` exist in `state["issues"]`
   - Validate no overlap between `queue_order` and `skipped` IDs
   - Ensure manual cluster names don't start with reserved prefixes (`auto/`, `epic/`)

4. **Score data available:**
   - `state["overall_score"]`, `state["objective_score"]`, `state["strict_score"]`, `state["verified_strict_score"]`
   - Per-dimension scores in `state["dimension_scores"][dim_name]`
   - Historical scores in `state["scan_history"]` (with `ScanHistoryEntry.dimension_scores`)
   - Plan cycle start scores in `plan["plan_start_scores"]`

Design an implementation plan for adding hard enforcement to desloppify workflow steps (workflow::score-checkpoint and workflow::create-plan).

## Current Problem
When an agent runs `desloppify plan resolve "workflow::score-checkpoint" --note "Reviewed score checkpoint" --confirm`, it goes through `cmd_plan_resolve()` in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/override_handlers.py` lines 500-523. The synthetic ID path just calls `purge_ids()` with zero content validation. The `--confirm` flag auto-generates attestation from the note, so any note over 50 chars works.

Triage stages have real enforcement: observe requires 100+ char analysis, reflect checks for recurring dimension mentions, organize validates enriched clusters exist. But workflow steps have none of this.

## Architecture Context

### Files involved:
- `override_handlers.py` (lines 486-583): `cmd_plan_resolve()` - main entry point
- `synthetic_workflow.py`: builds display items with `primary_command` hints  
- `stale_dimensions.py`: sync functions that inject/prune workflow IDs
- `parser_groups_plan_impl.py`: argparse setup for `plan resolve`
- `triage/stage_flow_commands.py`: model of how triage enforces real work
- `triage/confirmations.py`: model of attestation validation
- `triage/_stage_validation.py`: model of stage-specific validation

### Available state data:
- `score_snapshot(state)` → ScoreSnapshot(overall, objective, strict, verified)
- `plan["plan_start_scores"]` → frozen scores at cycle start
- `plan["clusters"]` → dict of named clusters with description, action_steps, issue_ids
- `plan["queue_order"]` → ordered list of work items
- `state["dimension_scores"]` → per-dimension metrics with score, strict, tier
- `state["issues"]` → all issues by ID

### What the triage model does right:
1. Each stage has its own dedicated command (`--stage observe --report "..."`)
2. Reports have minimum length requirements (100 chars)
3. Content is validated against actual data (must mention specific dimensions, cluster names)
4. Stages have dependencies (can't reflect without observe)
5. Confirmation is a separate step (`--confirm observe`)

## Design Requirements

1. **workflow::score-checkpoint** should require:
   - The note must reference the actual strict score number (validated against state)
   - Should mention at least 1-2 specific dimension names from the scorecard
   - Minimum note length of 150+ chars (force actual analysis)

2. **workflow::create-plan** should require:
   - Manual clusters must exist in the plan (at least 1)
   - Clusters should have descriptions and action_steps (enriched)
   - The note must reference at least one cluster name
   - Minimum note length of 200+ chars

3. **Skip mechanism**: If an agent truly wants to skip, they should need `--force-resolve` AND a note explaining why they're skipping the planning step. This should be logged prominently.

4. **Updated primary_command hints**: The synthetic_workflow.py display items should show the correct new command format.

5. Keep the implementation consistent with existing patterns (triage validation style).

## Design Constraints
- Changes should be in the desloppify source at /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify
- Don't add new CLI subcommands — enhance validation within the existing `plan resolve` flow
- The validation should happen in `cmd_plan_resolve()` in the synthetic ID handling block (lines 500-523)
- Follow existing code style (type hints, docstrings, colorize for output)

```
   155→    print(colorize(f"  Set note on {len(issue_ids)} issue(s).", "green"))
   156→
   157→
   158→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   159→# Skip / unskip
   160→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   161→
   162→
   163→def _validate_skip_requirements(
   164→    *,
   165→    kind: str,
   166→    attestation: str | None,
   167→    note: str | None,
   168→) -> bool:
   169→    if not skip_kind_requires_attestation(kind):
   170→        return True
   171→    if not validate_attestation(attestation):
   172→        show_attestation_requirement(
   173→            "Permanent skip" if kind == "permanent" else "False positive",
   174→            attestation,
   175→            ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
   176→        )
   177→        return False
   178→    if skip_kind_requires_note(kind) and not note:
   179→        print(
   180→            colorize("  --permanent requires --note to explain the decision.", "yellow"),
   181→            file=sys.stderr,
   182→        )
   183→        return False
   184→    return True
   185→
   186→
   187→def _apply_state_skip_resolution(
   188→    *,
   189→    kind: str,
   190→    state_file: Path | None,
   191→    issue_ids: list[str],
   192→    note: str | None,
   193→    attestation: str | None,
   194→) -> dict | None:
   195→    status = skip_kind_state_status(kind)
   196→    if status is None:
   197→        return None
   198→    state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   199→    for fid in issue_ids:
   200→        state_mod.resolve_issues(
   201→            state_data,
   202→            fid,
   203→            status,
   204→            note or "",
   205→            attestation=attestation,
   206→        )
   207→    return state_data
   208→
   209→
   210→def cmd_plan_skip(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   211→    """Skip issues — unified command for temporary/permanent/false-positive."""
   212→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   213→    state = runtime.state
   214→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   215→        return
   216→
   217→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   218→    reason: str | None = getattr(args, "reason", None)
   219→    review_after: int | None = getattr(args, "review_after", None)
   220→    permanent: bool = getattr(args, "permanent", False)
   221→    false_positive: bool = getattr(args, "false_positive", False)
   222→    note: str | None = getattr(args, "note", None)
   223→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attest", None)
   224→
   225→    kind = skip_kind_from_flags(permanent=permanent, false_positive=false_positive)
   226→    if not _validate_skip_requirements(
   227→        kind=kind,
   228→        attestation=attestation,
   229→        note=note,
   230→    ):
   231→        return
   232→
   233→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   234→    plan_file = _plan_file_for_state(state_file)
   235→    plan = load_plan(plan_file)
   236→    issue_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
   237→    if not issue_ids:
   238→        print(colorize("  No matching issues found.", "yellow"))
   239→        return
   240→
   241→    # For permanent/false_positive: delegate to state layer for score impact
   242→    state_data = _apply_state_skip_resolution(
   243→        kind=kind,
   244→        state_file=state_file,
   245→        issue_ids=issue_ids,
   246→        note=note,
   247→        attestation=attestation,
   248→    )
   249→
   250→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   251→    count = skip_items(
   252→        plan,
   253→        issue_ids,
   254→        kind=kind,
   255→        reason=reason,
   256→        note=note,
   257→        attestation=attestation,
   258→        review_after=review_after,
   259→        scan_count=scan_count,
   260→    )
   261→
   262→    # Log the skip action
   263→    append_log_entry(
   264→        plan,
   265→        "skip",
   266→        issue_ids=issue_ids,
   267→        actor="user",
   268→        note=note,
   269→        detail={"kind": kind, "reason": reason},
   270→    )
   271→    if state_data is not None:
   272→        _save_plan_state_transactional(
   273→            plan=plan,
   274→            plan_path=plan_file,
   275→            state_data=state_data,
   276→            state_path_value=state_file,
   277→        )
   278→    else:
   279→        save_plan(plan, plan_file)
   280→
   281→    print(colorize(f"  {SKIP_KIND_LABELS[kind]} {count} item(s).", "green"))
   282→    if review_after:
   283→        print(colorize(f"  Will re-surface after {review_after} scan(s).", "dim"))
   284→    print_user_message(
   285→        "Hey — if skipping was the right call, just continue with"
   286→        " what you were doing. If you think a broader re-triage is"
   287→        " needed, use `desloppify plan triage`. Run `desloppify"
   288→        " plan --help` to see all available plan tools. Otherwise"
   289→        " no need to reply, just keep going."
   290→    )
   291→
   292→
   293→def cmd_plan_unskip(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   294→    """Unskip issues — bring back to queue."""
   295→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   296→    state = runtime.state
   297→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   298→        return
   299→
   300→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   301→
   302→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   303→    plan_file = _plan_file_for_state(state_file)
   304→    plan = load_plan(plan_file)
   305→    # For unskip we need to match against all statuses (skipped items may be wontfix/fp)
   306→    issue_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan, status_filter="all")
   307→    if not issue_ids:
   308→        print(colorize("  No matching issues found.", "yellow"))
   309→        return
   310→
   311→    count, need_reopen = unskip_items(plan, issue_ids)
   312→    append_log_entry(
   313→        plan, "unskip", issue_ids=issue_ids, actor="user",
   314→        detail={"need_reopen": need_reopen},
   315→    )
   316→
   317→    # Reopen permanent/false_positive items in state
   318→    reopened: list[str] = []
   319→    if need_reopen:
   320→        state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   321→        for fid in need_reopen:
   322→            reopened.extend(state_mod.resolve_issues(state_data, fid, "open"))
   323→        _save_plan_state_transactional(
   324→            plan=plan,
   325→            plan_path=plan_file,
   326→            state_data=state_data,
   327→            state_path_value=state_file,
   328→        )
   329→        print(colorize(f"  Reopened {len(reopened)} issue(s) in state.", "dim"))
   330→    else:
   331→        save_plan(plan, plan_file)
   332→
   333→    print(colorize(f"  Unskipped {count} item(s) — back in queue.", "green"))
   334→
   335→
   336→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   337→# Reopen
   338→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   339→
   340→def cmd_plan_reopen(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   341→    """Reopen resolved issues from plan context."""
   342→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   343→
   344→    raw_state_path = state_path(args)
   345→    state_file = raw_state_path if isinstance(raw_state_path, Path) else Path(raw_state_path) if raw_state_path else None
   346→    state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   347→    plan_file = _plan_file_for_state(state_file)
   348→
   349→    reopened: list[str] = []
   350→    for pattern in patterns:
   351→        reopened.extend(
   352→            state_mod.resolve_issues(state_data, pattern, "open")
   353→        )
   354→
   355→    if not reopened:
   356→        print(colorize("  No resolved issues matching: " + " ".join(patterns), "yellow"))
   357→        return
   358→
   359→    # Remove from skipped if present, and ensure all reopened IDs are in queue
   360→    plan = load_plan(plan_file)
   361→
   362→    # Remove from commit tracking uncommitted list
   363→    purge_uncommitted_ids(plan, reopened)
   364→
   365→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   366→    count = 0
   367→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   368→    for fid in reopened:
   369→        if fid in skipped:
   370→            skipped.pop(fid)
   371→            count += 1
   372→        if fid not in order:
   373→            plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   374→            order.add(fid)
   375→            count += 1
   376→    append_log_entry(
   377→        plan, "reopen", issue_ids=reopened, actor="user",
   378→    )
   379→    _save_plan_state_transactional(
   380→        plan=plan,
   381→        plan_path=plan_file,
   382→        state_data=state_data,
   383→        state_path_value=state_file,
   384→    )
   385→
   386→    print(colorize(f"  Reopened {len(reopened)} issue(s).", "green"))
   387→    if count:
   388→        print(colorize("  Plan updated: items moved back to queue.", "dim"))
   389→
   390→
   391→_CLUSTER_INDIVIDUAL_THRESHOLD = 10
   392→
   393→
   394→def _check_cluster_guard(patterns: list[str], plan: dict, state: dict) -> bool:
   395→    """Return True if blocked by cluster guard, False if OK to proceed."""
   396→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   397→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   398→    for pattern in patterns:
   399→        if pattern in clusters:
   400→            cluster = clusters[pattern]
   401→            # Filter to alive issues only — stale IDs should not count
   402→            ids = [
   403→                fid for fid in cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   404→                if fid in issues and issues[fid].get("status") == "open"
   405→            ]
   406→            if len(ids) == 0:
   407→                print(colorize(
   408→                    f"\n  Cluster '{pattern}' is empty — add items before marking it done.\n",
   409→                    "yellow",
   410→                ))
   411→                print(colorize(
   412→                    f"  Use: desloppify plan cluster add {pattern} <issue-id>",
   413→                    "dim",
   414→                ))
   415→                return True  # blocked
   416→            if len(ids) <= _CLUSTER_INDIVIDUAL_THRESHOLD:
   417→                _print_cluster_guard(pattern, ids, state)
   418→                return True  # blocked
   419→    return False  # OK
   420→
   421→
   422→def _print_cluster_guard(cluster_name: str, issue_ids: list[str], state: dict) -> None:
   423→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   424→    print(colorize(
   425→        f"\n  Cluster '{cluster_name}' has {len(issue_ids)} item(s) — mark them done individually first:\n",
   426→        "yellow",
   427→    ))
   428→    for fid in issue_ids:
   429→        f = issues.get(fid, {})
   430→        summary = f.get("summary", "(no summary)")[:80]
   431→        detector = f.get("detector", "?")
   432→        print(f"    {fid}  [{detector}]  {summary}")
   433→    print(colorize(
   434→        "\n  Use: desloppify resolve <id> --status fixed --note '...' --attest '...'",
   435→        "dim",
   436→    ))
   437→    print(colorize(
   438→        "  Or mark each resolved: desloppify plan resolve <id> --note '...' --confirm\n",
   439→        "dim",
   440→    ))
   441→
   442→
   443→def _is_synthetic_id(fid: str) -> bool:
   444→    """Return True if the ID is a synthetic workflow/triage item, not a real issue."""
   445→    return fid.startswith("triage::") or fid.startswith("workflow::") or fid.startswith("subjective::")
   446→
   447→
   448→def _resolve_synthetic_ids(patterns: list[str]) -> tuple[list[str], list[str]]:
   449→    """Separate synthetic IDs from real issue patterns.
   450→
   451→    Returns (synthetic_ids, remaining_patterns).
   452→    """
   453→    synthetic = [p for p in patterns if _is_synthetic_id(p)]
   454→    remaining = [p for p in patterns if not _is_synthetic_id(p)]
   455→    return synthetic, remaining
   456→
   457→
   458→def _blocked_triage_stages(plan: dict) -> dict[str, list[str]]:
   459→    """Return ``{stage_id: [blocked_by_ids]}`` for triage stages that can't run yet.
   460→
   461→    Uses the dependency graph and confirmed-stage metadata directly —
   462→    no state needed, no queue item construction.
   463→    """
   464→    order_set = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   465→    present = order_set & TRIAGE_IDS
   466→    if not present:
   467→        return {}
   468→
   469→    confirmed = set(plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {}).get("triage_stages", {}).keys())
   470→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "commit")
   471→
   472→    blocked: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
   473→    for sid, name in zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names, strict=False):
   474→        if sid not in present or name in confirmed:
   475→            continue
   476→        deps = TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES.get(name, set())
   477→        unmet = sorted(
   478→            f"triage::{dep}" for dep in deps
   479→            if f"triage::{dep}" in present and dep not in confirmed
   480→        )
   481→        if unmet:
   482→            blocked[sid] = unmet
   483→    return blocked
   484→
   485→
   486→def cmd_plan_resolve(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   487→    """Mark issues as fixed — delegates to cmd_resolve for rich UX."""
   488→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   489→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attest", None)
   490→    note: str | None = getattr(args, "note", None)
   491→
   492→    # --confirm: auto-generate attestation from --note
   493→    if getattr(args, "confirm", False):
   494→        if not note:
   495→            print(colorize("  --confirm requires --note to describe what you did.", "red"))
   496→            return
   497→        attestation = f"I have actually {note} and I am not gaming the score."
   498→        args.attest = attestation
   499→
   500→    # Handle synthetic IDs (triage::*, workflow::*, subjective::*) directly
   501→    synthetic_ids, real_patterns = _resolve_synthetic_ids(patterns)
   502→    if synthetic_ids:
   503→        plan = load_plan()
   504→        # Validate triage dependency chain
   505→        blocked_map = _blocked_triage_stages(plan)
   506→        for sid in synthetic_ids:
   507→            if sid in blocked_map:
   508→                deps = ", ".join(b.replace("triage::", "") for b in blocked_map[sid])
   509→                print(colorize(f"  Cannot resolve {sid} — blocked by: {deps}", "red"))
   510→                print(colorize("  Complete those stages first, or use --force-resolve to override.", "dim"))
   511→                if not getattr(args, "force_resolve", False):
   512→                    return
   513→        purge_ids(plan, synthetic_ids)
   514→        append_log_entry(
   515→            plan, "done", issue_ids=synthetic_ids, actor="user", note=note,
   516→        )
   517→        save_plan(plan)
   518→        for sid in synthetic_ids:
   519→            print(colorize(f"  Resolved: {sid}", "green"))
   520→        if not real_patterns:
   521→            return
   522→        # Continue with remaining real patterns
   523→        patterns = real_patterns
   524→        args.patterns = patterns
   525→
   526→    # Validate note length
   527→    if not validate_note_length(note):
   528→        show_note_length_requirement(note)
   529→        return
   530→
   531→    # Pre-validate attestation before delegating (avoids stale hint in resolve)
   532→    if not validate_attestation(attestation):
   533→        show_attestation_requirement("Plan resolve", attestation, ATTEST_EXAMPLE)
   534→        return
   535→
   536→    # Cluster completion guard: block bulk-completing small clusters
   537→    try:
   538→        runtime = command_runtime(args)
   539→        state = runtime.state
   540→        plan = load_plan()
   541→        if _check_cluster_guard(patterns, plan, state):
   542→            return
   543→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   544→        plan = None
   545→
   546→    # Log the done action (best-effort)
   547→    try:
   548→        if plan is None:
   549→            plan = load_plan()
   550→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   551→        cluster_name = None
   552→        for p in patterns:
   553→            if p in clusters:
   554→                cluster_name = p
   555→                break
   556→        append_log_entry(
   557→            plan,
   558→            "done",
   559→            issue_ids=patterns,
   560→            cluster_name=cluster_name,
   561→            actor="user",
   562→            note=note,
   563→        )
   564→        save_plan(plan)
   565→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   566→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, "append plan resolve log entry", exc)
   567→        print(colorize(f"  Note: unable to append plan resolve log entry ({exc}).", "dim"))
   568→
   569→    # Build a Namespace that cmd_resolve expects
   570→    resolve_args = argparse.Namespace(
   571→        status="fixed",
   572→        patterns=patterns,
   573→        note=note,
   574→        attest=attestation,
   575→        confirm_batch_wontfix=False,
   576→        force_resolve=bool(getattr(args, "force_resolve", False)),
   577→        state=getattr(args, "state", None),
   578→        lang=getattr(args, "lang", None),
   579→        path=getattr(args, "path", None),
   580→        exclude=getattr(args, "exclude", None),
   581→    )
   582→
   583→    cmd_resolve(resolve_args)
   584→
   585→
   586→def cmd_plan_focus(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   587→    """Set or clear the active cluster focus."""
   588→    clear_flag = getattr(args, "clear", False)
   589→    cluster_name: str | None = getattr(args, "cluster_name", None)
   590→
   591→    plan = load_plan()
   592→    if clear_flag:
   593→        prev = plan.get("active_cluster")
   594→        clear_focus(plan)
   595→        append_log_entry(
   596→            plan, "focus", actor="user",
   597→            detail={"action": "clear", "previous": prev},
   598→        )
   599→        save_plan(plan)
   600→        print(colorize("  Focus cleared.", "green"))
   601→        return
   602→
   603→    if not cluster_name:
   604→        active = plan.get("active_cluster")
   605→        if active:
   606→            print(f"  Focused on: {active}")
   607→        else:
   608→            print("  No active focus.")
   609→        return
   610→
   611→    try:
   612→        set_focus(plan, cluster_name)
   613→    except ValueError as ex:
   614→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
   615→        return
   616→    append_log_entry(
   617→        plan, "focus", cluster_name=cluster_name, actor="user",
   618→        detail={"action": "set"},
   619→    )
   620→    save_plan(plan)
   621→    print(colorize(f"  Focused on: {cluster_name}", "green"))
   622→
   623→
   624→__all__ = [
   625→    "cmd_plan_describe",
   626→    "cmd_plan_resolve",
   627→    "cmd_plan_focus",
   628→    "cmd_plan_note",
   629→    "cmd_plan_reopen",
   630→    "cmd_plan_skip",
   631→    "cmd_plan_unskip",
   632→]
   633→
```

```
1→"""Workflow-specific synthetic work queue item builders."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.types import WorkQueueItem
     6→
     7→
     8→def build_score_checkpoint_item(plan: dict, state: dict) -> WorkQueueItem | None:
     9→    """Build a synthetic work item for ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` if queued."""
    10→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID
    11→
    12→    if WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
    13→        return None
    14→
    15→    from desloppify import state as state_mod
    16→
    17→    snapshot = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    18→    strict = snapshot.strict if snapshot.strict is not None else 0.0
    19→    plan_start = (plan.get("plan_start_scores") or {}).get("strict")
    20→    delta = round(strict - plan_start, 1) if plan_start is not None else None
    21→    delta_str = f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})" if delta else ""
    22→
    23→    return {
    24→        "id": WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID,
    25→        "tier": 1,
    26→        "confidence": "high",
    27→        "detector": "workflow",
    28→        "file": ".",

    34→            "delta": delta,
```

```
1→"""Validation and guardrail helpers for triage stage workflow."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.plan.triage_playbook import TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE
     9→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    10→from desloppify.engine.plan import (
    11→    collect_triage_input,
    12→    detect_recurring_patterns,
    13→    extract_issue_citations,
    14→    save_plan,
    15→)
    16→from desloppify.state import utc_now
    17→
    18→from .confirmations import _MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN, _validate_attestation
    19→from .display import show_plan_summary
    20→from .helpers import manual_clusters_with_issues, observe_dimension_breakdown
    21→from .stage_helpers import _unenriched_clusters
    22→from ._stage_rendering import _print_new_issues_since_last
    23→
    24→
    25→def _auto_confirm_observe_if_attested(
    26→    *,
    27→    plan: dict,
    28→    stages: dict,
    29→    attestation: str | None,
    30→    triage_input,
    31→) -> bool:
    32→    if stages["observe"].get("confirmed_at"):
    33→        return True
    34→    if not attestation or len(attestation.strip()) < _MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN:
    35→        print(colorize("  Cannot reflect: observe stage not confirmed.", "red"))
    36→        print(colorize("  Run: desloppify plan triage --confirm observe", "dim"))
    37→        print(colorize("  Or pass --attestation to auto-confirm observe inline.", "dim"))
    38→        return False
    39→    _by_dim, dim_names = observe_dimension_breakdown(triage_input)
    40→    validation_err = _validate_attestation(
    41→        attestation.strip(),
    42→        "observe",
    43→        dimensions=dim_names,
    44→    )
    45→    if validation_err:
    46→        print(colorize(f"  {validation_err}", "red"))
    47→        return False
    48→    stages["observe"]["confirmed_at"] = utc_now()
    49→    stages["observe"]["confirmed_text"] = attestation.strip()
    50→    save_plan(plan)
    51→    print(colorize("  ✓ Observe auto-confirmed via --attestation.", "green"))
    52→    return True
    53→
    54→
    55→def _validate_recurring_dimension_mentions(
    56→    *,
    57→    report: str,
    58→    recurring_dims: list[str],
    59→    recurring: dict,
    60→) -> bool:
    61→    if not recurring_dims:
    62→        return True
    63→    report_lower = report.lower()
    64→    mentioned = [dim for dim in recurring_dims if dim.lower() in report_lower]
    65→    if mentioned:
    66→        return True
    67→    print(colorize("  Recurring patterns detected but not addressed in report:", "red"))
    68→    for dim in recurring_dims:
    69→        info = recurring[dim]
    70→        print(
    71→            colorize(
    72→                f"    {dim}: {len(info['resolved'])} resolved, "
    73→                f"{len(info['open'])} still open — potential loop",
    74→                "yellow",
    75→            )
    76→        )
    77→    print(
    78→        colorize(
    79→            "  Your report must mention at least one recurring dimension name.",
    80→            "dim",
    81→        )
    82→    )
    83→    return False
    84→
    85→
    86→def _require_reflect_stage_for_organize(stages: dict) -> bool:
    87→    if "reflect" in stages:
    88→        return True
    89→    if "observe" not in stages:
    90→        print(colorize("  Cannot organize: observe stage not complete.", "red"))
    91→        print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report "..."', "dim"))
    92→        return False
    93→    print(colorize("  Cannot organize: reflect stage not complete.", "red"))
    94→    print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage reflect --report "..."', "dim"))
    95→    return False
    96→
    97→
    98→def _auto_confirm_reflect_for_organize(
    99→    *,
   100→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   101→    plan: dict,
   102→    stages: dict,
   103→    attestation: str | None,
   104→) -> bool:
   105→    if stages["reflect"].get("confirmed_at"):
   106→        return True
   107→    if not attestation or len(attestation.strip()) < _MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN:
   108→        print(colorize("  Cannot organize: reflect stage not confirmed.", "red"))
   109→        print(colorize("  Run: desloppify plan triage --confirm reflect", "dim"))
   110→        print(colorize("  Or pass --attestation to auto-confirm reflect inline.", "dim"))
   111→        return False
   112→
   113→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   114→    triage_input = collect_triage_input(plan, runtime.state)
   115→    recurring = detect_recurring_patterns(
   116→        triage_input.open_issues,
   117→        triage_input.resolved_issues,
   118→    )
   119→    _by_dim, observe_dims = observe_dimension_breakdown(triage_input)
   120→    reflect_dims = sorted(set((list(recurring.keys()) if recurring else []) + observe_dims))
   121→    reflect_clusters = [
   122→        name for name in plan.get("clusters", {}) if not plan["clusters"][name].get("auto")
   123→    ]
   124→    validation_err = _validate_attestation(
   125→        attestation.strip(),
   126→        "reflect",
   127→        dimensions=reflect_dims,
   128→        cluster_names=reflect_clusters,
   129→    )
   130→    if validation_err:
   131→        print(colorize(f"  {validation_err}", "red"))
   132→        return False
   133→    stages["reflect"]["confirmed_at"] = utc_now()
   134→    stages["reflect"]["confirmed_text"] = attestation.strip()
   135→    save_plan(plan)
   136→    print(colorize("  ✓ Reflect auto-confirmed via --attestation.", "green"))
   137→    return True
   138→

   140→def _manual_clusters_or_error(plan: dict) -> list[str] | None:
   141→    manual_clusters = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   142→    if manual_clusters:
   143→        return manual_clusters
   144→    any_clusters = [
   145→        name for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items() if cluster.get("issue_ids")
   146→    ]
   147→    if any_clusters:

   149→        print(colorize("  Create manual clusters that group issues by root cause:", "dim"))
   150→    else:

   152→    print(colorize('    desloppify plan cluster create <name> --description "..."', "dim"))

   320→    return None
   321→
   322→
   323→def _completion_strategy_valid(strategy: str) -> bool:
   324→    if strategy.strip().lower() == "same":
   325→        return True
   326→    if len(strategy.strip()) >= 200:
   327→        return True
   328→    print(colorize(f"  Strategy too short: {len(strategy.strip())} chars (minimum 200).", "red"))
   329→    print(colorize("  The strategy should describe:", "dim"))
   330→    print(colorize("    - Execution order and priorities", "dim"))
   331→    print(colorize("    - What each cluster accomplishes", "dim"))
   332→    print(colorize("    - How to verify the work is correct", "dim"))
   333→    return False
   334→
   335→
   336→def _require_prior_strategy_for_confirm(meta: dict) -> bool:
   337→    if meta.get("strategy_summary", ""):
   338→        return True
   339→    print(colorize("  Cannot confirm existing: no prior triage has been completed.", "red"))
   340→    print(colorize("  The full OBSERVE → REFLECT → ORGANIZE → COMMIT flow is required the first time.", "dim"))
   341→    print(colorize(f"  Create and enrich clusters, then: {TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE}", "dim"))
   342→    return False
   343→
   344→
   345→def _confirm_existing_stages_valid(
   346→    *,
   347→    stages: dict,
   348→    has_only_additions: bool,
   349→    si,
   350→) -> bool:
   351→    if has_only_additions:
   352→        _print_new_issues_since_last(si)
   353→        return True
   354→    if "observe" not in stages:
   355→        print(colorize("  Cannot confirm existing: observe stage not complete.", "red"))
   356→        print(colorize("  You must read issues first.", "dim"))
   357→        print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report "..."', "dim"))
   358→        return False
   359→    if "reflect" not in stages:
   360→        print(colorize("  Cannot confirm existing: reflect stage not complete.", "red"))
   361→        print(colorize("  You must compare against completed work first.", "dim"))
   362→        print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage reflect --report "..."', "dim"))
   363→        return False
   364→    return True
   365→
   366→
   367→def _confirm_note_valid(note: str | None) -> bool:
   368→    if not note:
   369→        print(colorize("  --note is required for confirm-existing.", "red"))
   370→        print(colorize('  Explain why the existing plan is still valid (min 100 chars).', "dim"))
   371→        return False
   372→    if len(note) < 100:
   373→        print(colorize(f"  Note too short: {len(note)} chars (minimum 100).", "red"))
   374→        return False
   375→    return True
   376→
   377→
   378→def _resolve_confirm_existing_strategy(
   379→    strategy: str | None,
   380→    *,
   381→    has_only_additions: bool,
   382→    meta: dict,
   383→) -> str | None:
   384→    if strategy:
   385→        return strategy
   386→    if has_only_additions:
   387→        return "same"
   388→    print(colorize("  --strategy is required.", "red"))
   389→    existing = meta.get("strategy_summary", "")
   390→    if existing:
   391→        print(colorize('  Use --strategy "same" to keep it, or provide a new summary.', "dim"))
   392→    return None
   393→
   394→
   395→def _confirm_strategy_valid(strategy: str) -> bool:
   396→    if strategy.strip().lower() == "same":
   397→        return True
   398→    if len(strategy.strip()) >= 200:
   399→        return True
   400→    print(colorize(f"  Strategy too short: {len(strategy.strip())} chars (minimum 200).", "red"))
   401→    return False
   402→
   403→
   404→def _confirmed_text_or_error(
   405→    *,
   406→    plan: dict,
   407→    state: dict,
   408→    confirmed: str | None,
   409→) -> str | None:
   410→    if confirmed and len(confirmed.strip()) >= _MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN:
   411→        return confirmed.strip()
   412→    print(colorize("  Current plan:", "bold"))
   413→    show_plan_summary(plan, state)
   414→    if confirmed:
   415→        print(
   416→            colorize(
   417→                f"\n  --confirmed text too short ({len(confirmed.strip())} chars, min {_MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN}).",
   418→                "red",
   419→            )
   420→        )
   421→    print(colorize('\n  Add --confirmed "I validate this plan..." to proceed.', "dim"))
   422→    return None
   423→
   424→
   425→def _note_cites_new_issues_or_error(note: str, si) -> bool:
   426→    new_ids = si.new_since_last
   427→    if not new_ids:
   428→        return True
   429→    valid_ids = set(si.open_issues.keys())
   430→    cited = extract_issue_citations(note, valid_ids)
   431→    new_cited = cited & new_ids
   432→    if new_cited:
   433→        return True
   434→    print(colorize("  Note must cite at least 1 new/changed issue.", "red"))
   435→    print(colorize(f"  {len(new_ids)} new issue(s) since last triage:", "dim"))
   436→    for fid in sorted(new_ids)[:5]:
   437→        print(colorize(f"    {fid}", "dim"))
   438→    if len(new_ids) > 5:
   439→        print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(new_ids) - 5} more", "dim"))
   440→    return False
```

```
    24→def _validate_attestation(
    25→    attestation: str,
    26→    stage: str,
    27→    *,
    28→    dimensions: list[str] | None = None,
    29→    cluster_names: list[str] | None = None,

    32→    text = attestation.lower()
    33→
    34→    if stage == "observe":
    35→        if dimensions:
    36→            found = [d for d in dimensions if d.lower().replace("_", " ") in text or d.lower() in text]
    37→            if not found:
    38→                dim_list = ", ".join(dimensions[:6])
    39→                return f"Attestation must reference at least one dimension from the summary. Mention one of: {dim_list}"
    40→
    41→    elif stage == "reflect":
    42→        refs: list[str] = []
    43→        if dimensions:
    44→            refs.extend(d for d in dimensions if d.lower().replace("_", " ") in text or d.lower() in text)
    45→        if cluster_names:
    46→            refs.extend(n for n in cluster_names if n.lower() in text)
    47→        if not refs and (dimensions or cluster_names):
    48→            return (
    49→                f"Attestation must reference at least one dimension or cluster name.\n"
    50→                f"  Valid dimensions: {', '.join((dimensions or [])[:6])}\n"
    51→                f"  Valid clusters: {', '.join((cluster_names or [])[:6]) if cluster_names else '(none yet)'}"
    52→            )
    53→
    54→    elif stage == "organize":
    55→        if cluster_names:
    56→            found = [n for n in cluster_names if n.lower() in text]
    57→            if not found:
    58→                names = ", ".join(cluster_names[:6])
    59→                return f"Attestation must reference at least one cluster from the plan. Mention one of: {names}"
    60→
    61→    return None

   316→def _cmd_confirm_stage(

   324→    attestation = getattr(args, "attestation", None)
   325→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   326→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   327→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   328→
   329→    if confirm_stage == "observe":
   330→        _confirm_observe(args, plan, stages, attestation, services=resolved_services)
   331→    elif confirm_stage == "reflect":
   332→        _confirm_reflect(args, plan, stages, attestation, services=resolved_services)
   333→    elif confirm_stage == "organize":
   334→        _confirm_organize(args, plan, stages, attestation, services=resolved_services)
   335→
   336→
   337→MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN = _MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN
   338→validate_attestation = _validate_attestation
   339→
   340→

   343→    *,
   344→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
```

```
     3→Two independent sync functions:
     4→
     5→- **sync_unscored_dimensions** — prepend never-scored (placeholder) dimensions
     6→  to the *front* of the queue unconditionally (onboarding priority).

    22→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    23→
    24→SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX = "subjective::"
    25→TRIAGE_ID = "triage::pending"  # deprecated, kept for migration
    26→
    27→TRIAGE_PREFIX = "triage::"
    28→TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS = (
    29→    "triage::observe",
    30→    "triage::reflect",
    31→    "triage::organize",
    32→    "triage::commit",
    33→)
    34→TRIAGE_IDS = set(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS)
    35→WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID = "workflow::create-plan"
    36→WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID = "workflow::score-checkpoint"
    37→WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID = "workflow::import-scores"
    38→WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID = "workflow::communicate-score"
    39→WORKFLOW_PREFIX = "workflow::"
    40→SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES = ("triage::", "workflow::", "subjective::")
    41→
    42→
    43→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    44→# Result dataclasses
    45→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    46→
    47→@dataclass
    48→class StaleDimensionSyncResult:
    49→    """What changed during a stale-dimension sync."""
    50→
    51→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    52→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    53→
    54→    @property
    55→    def changes(self) -> int:
    56→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    57→
    58→
    59→@dataclass
    60→class UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
    61→    """What changed during an unscored-dimension sync."""
    62→
    63→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    64→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    65→
    66→    @property
    67→    def changes(self) -> int:
    68→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    69→
    70→
    71→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    72→# ID helpers
    73→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    74→
    75→def _current_stale_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    76→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently stale."""
    77→    return stale_policy_mod.current_stale_ids(
    78→        state,
    79→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    80→    )
    81→
    82→
    83→def current_unscored_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    84→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored (placeholder).
    85→
    86→    Checks ``subjective_assessments`` first; when that dict is empty
    87→    (common before any reviews have been run), falls through to
    88→    ``dimension_scores`` which carries placeholder metadata from scan.
    89→    """
    90→    return stale_policy_mod.current_unscored_ids(
    91→        state,
    92→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    93→    )
    94→
    95→
    96→def current_under_target_ids(
    97→    state: StateModel,
    98→    *,
    99→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
   100→) -> set[str]:
   101→    """Return ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are under target but not stale or unscored.
   102→
   103→    These are dimensions whose assessment is still current (not needing refresh)
   104→    but whose score hasn't reached the target yet.
   105→    """
   106→    return stale_policy_mod.current_under_target_ids(
   107→        state,
   108→        target_strict=target_strict,
   109→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
   110→    )
   111→
   112→
   113→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   114→# Helpers for sync_stale_dimensions / sync_unscored_dimensions
   115→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   116→
   117→def _has_objective_backlog(
   118→    state: StateModel,
   119→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
   120→) -> bool:
   121→    """Return whether an objective backlog exists (open non-subjective issues)."""
   122→    if policy is not None:
   123→        return policy.has_objective_backlog
   124→    return any(
   125→        f.get("status") == "open"
   126→        and f.get("detector") not in _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   127→        and not f.get("suppressed")
   128→        for f in state.get("issues", {}).values()
   129→    )
   130→
   131→
   132→def _prune_subjective_ids(
   133→    order: list[str],
   134→    *,
   135→    keep_ids: set[str],
   136→    pruned: list[str],
   137→) -> None:
   138→    """Remove subjective IDs from *order* that are not in *keep_ids*, appending removed to *pruned*."""
   139→    to_remove = [
   140→        fid for fid in order
   141→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   142→        and fid not in keep_ids
   143→    ]
   144→    for fid in to_remove:
   145→        order.remove(fid)
   146→        pruned.append(fid)
   147→
   148→
   149→def _inject_subjective_ids(
   150→    order: list[str],
   151→    plan: PlanModel,
   152→    *,
   153→    inject_ids: set[str],
   154→    to_front: bool,
   155→    injected: list[str],
   156→) -> None:
   157→    """Inject subjective IDs into *order* if not already present.
   158→
   159→    When *to_front* is True, inserts after promoted items (front of queue).
   160→    Otherwise appends to the back.
   161→    """
   162→    existing = set(order)
   163→    if to_front:
   164→        insert_at = promoted_insertion_index(order, plan)
   165→        for sid in reversed(sorted(inject_ids)):
   166→            if sid not in existing:
   167→                order.insert(insert_at, sid)
   168→                injected.append(sid)
   169→    else:
   170→        for sid in sorted(inject_ids):
   171→            if sid not in existing:
   172→                order.append(sid)
   173→                injected.append(sid)
   174→
   175→
   176→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   177→# Helpers for sync_triage_needed
   178→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   179→
   180→def _open_review_issue_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   181→    """Return set of open issue IDs whose detector is 'review' or 'concerns'."""
   182→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   183→    return {
   184→        fid for fid, f in issues.items()
   185→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   186→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   187→    }
   188→
   189→
   190→def _new_review_ids_since_triage(
   191→    state: StateModel,
   192→    meta: dict,
   193→) -> set[str]:
   194→    """Return review issue IDs that are new since the last triage."""
   195→    current_review_ids = _open_review_issue_ids(state)
   196→    triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
   197→    return current_review_ids - triaged_ids
   198→
   199→
   200→def _prune_all_triage_stages(order: list[str]) -> None:
   201→    """Remove all ``triage::*`` stage IDs from *order*."""
   202→    for sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:
   203→        while sid in order:
   204→            order.remove(sid)
   205→
   206→
   207→def _inject_pending_triage_stages(
   208→    order: list[str],
   209→    plan: PlanModel,
   210→    confirmed: set[str],
   211→) -> bool:
   212→    """Inject triage stages for pending (unconfirmed) items.
   213→
   214→    Returns True if any stages were injected.
   215→    """
   216→    insert_at = promoted_insertion_index(order, plan)
   217→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "commit")
   218→    existing = set(order)
   219→    injected_count = 0
   220→    for sid, name in zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names, strict=False):
   221→        if name not in confirmed and sid not in existing:
   222→            order.insert(insert_at + injected_count, sid)
   223→            injected_count += 1
   224→    return injected_count > 0
   225→
   226→
   227→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   228→# Unscored dimension sync (front of queue, unconditional)
   229→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   230→
   231→def sync_unscored_dimensions(
   232→    plan: PlanModel,
   233→    state: StateModel,
   234→) -> UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
   235→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions.
   236→
   237→    1. **Prune** — remove ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are
   238→       no longer unscored AND not stale (avoids pruning stale IDs — that is
   239→       ``sync_stale_dimensions``' responsibility).
   240→    2. **Inject** — unconditionally prepend currently-unscored IDs to the
   241→       *front* of ``queue_order`` so initial reviews are the first priority.
   242→    """
   243→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   244→    result = UnscoredDimensionSyncResult()
   245→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   246→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   247→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   248→
   249→    # --- Cleanup: prune subjective IDs that are no longer unscored --------
   250→    # Only prune IDs that are neither unscored nor stale (stale sync owns those).
   251→    _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=unscored_ids | stale_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   252→
   253→    # --- Inject: prepend unscored IDs after any promoted items -------------
   254→    _inject_subjective_ids(order, plan, inject_ids=unscored_ids, to_front=True, injected=result.injected)
   255→
   256→    return result
   257→
   258→
   259→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   260→# Stale dimension sync (back of queue, conditional)
   261→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   262→
   263→def sync_stale_dimensions(
   264→    plan: PlanModel,
   265→    state: StateModel,
   266→    *,
   267→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   268→    cycle_just_completed: bool = False,
   269→) -> StaleDimensionSyncResult:
   270→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with stale and under-target subjective dimensions.
   271→
   272→    1. Remove any ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are no
   273→       longer stale/under-target and not unscored (avoids pruning IDs owned
   274→       by ``sync_unscored_dimensions``).
   275→    2. Inject stale and under-target dimension IDs when either:
   276→       a. No objective items remain (mid-cycle: append to back), OR
   277→       b. A cycle just completed (post-cycle: insert at front so subjective
   278→          review takes priority over new objective issues).
   279→    """
   280→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   281→    result = StaleDimensionSyncResult()
   282→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   283→    under_target_ids = current_under_target_ids(state)
   284→    injectable_ids = stale_ids | under_target_ids
   285→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   286→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   287→
   288→    # --- Cleanup: prune resolved subjective IDs --------------------------
   289→    # Only prune IDs that are no longer injectable and not unscored.
   290→    _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=injectable_ids | unscored_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   291→
   292→    # --- Inject or evict stale + under-target dimensions -----------------
   293→    has_real_items = _has_objective_backlog(state, policy)
   294→    should_inject = not has_real_items or cycle_just_completed
   295→
   296→    if not should_inject:
   297→        # Mid-cycle with objective backlog: evict any stale/under-target IDs
   298→        # that are present in the queue.  They may have been grandfathered from
   299→        # the unscored phase and should not be visible until the objective
   300→        # backlog clears or a cycle completes.
   301→        to_evict = [
   302→            fid for fid in order
   303→            if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   304→            and fid in injectable_ids
   305→        ]
   306→        for fid in to_evict:
   307→            order.remove(fid)
   308→            result.pruned.append(fid)
   309→
   310→    if should_inject and injectable_ids:
   311→        to_front = cycle_just_completed and has_real_items
   312→        _inject_subjective_ids(order, plan, inject_ids=injectable_ids, to_front=to_front, injected=result.injected)
   313→
   314→    return result
   315→
   316→
   317→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   318→# Triage snapshot hash + sync
   319→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   320→
   321→def review_issue_snapshot_hash(state: StateModel) -> str:
   322→    """Hash open review issue IDs to detect changes.
   323→
   324→    Returns empty string when there are no open review issues.
   325→    """
   326→    return stale_policy_mod.review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   327→
   328→
   329→@dataclass
   330→class TriageSyncResult:
   331→    """What changed during a triage sync."""
   332→
   333→    injected: bool = False
   334→    pruned: bool = False
   335→
   336→    @property
   337→    def changes(self) -> int:
   338→        return int(self.injected) + int(self.pruned)
   339→
   340→
   341→def sync_triage_needed(
   342→    plan: PlanModel,
   343→    state: StateModel,
   344→) -> TriageSyncResult:
   345→    """Inject 4 triage stage IDs at front of queue when review issues change.
   346→
   347→    Only injects stages not already confirmed in ``epic_triage_meta``.
   348→
   349→    When stages are already present but all new issues have been resolved
   350→    since injection, auto-prunes the stale stages and updates the hash.
   351→
   352→    When issues are *resolved* (current IDs are a subset of previously
   353→    triaged IDs), the snapshot hash is updated silently — no re-triage
   354→    is needed since the user is working through the plan.
   355→    """
   356→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   357→    result = TriageSyncResult()
   358→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   359→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   360→    confirmed = set(meta.get("triage_stages", {}).keys())
   361→
   362→    # Check if any triage stage is already in queue
   363→    already_present = any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS)
   364→
   365→    current_hash = review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   366→    last_hash = meta.get("issue_snapshot_hash", "")
   367→
   368→    if already_present:
   369→        # Stages present — check if the reason for injection still applies.
   370→        # Only auto-prune when triage was completed before (hash exists),
   371→        # all new issues have been resolved, and no triage work is in
   372→        # progress.  This avoids pruning the initial triage or a
   373→        # user-started triage session.
   374→        if last_hash and not confirmed:
   375→            new_since_triage = _new_review_ids_since_triage(state, meta)
   376→
   377→            if not new_since_triage:
   378→                # No new issues remain — prune stale stages
   379→                _prune_all_triage_stages(order)
   380→                if current_hash:
   381→                    meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   382→                    plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   383→                result.pruned = True
   384→        return result
   385→
   386→    if current_hash and current_hash != last_hash:
   387→        # Distinguish "new issues appeared" from "issues were resolved".
   388→        # Only re-triage when genuinely new issues exist.
   389→        new_since_triage = _new_review_ids_since_triage(state, meta)
   390→
   391→        if new_since_triage:
   392→            # New review issues appeared — re-triage needed
   393→            result.injected = _inject_pending_triage_stages(order, plan, confirmed)
   394→        else:
   395→            # Only resolved issues changed the hash — update silently
   396→            meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   397→            plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   398→
   399→    return result
   400→
   401→
   402→@dataclass
   403→class ScoreCheckpointSyncResult:
   404→    """What changed during a score-checkpoint sync."""
   405→
   406→    injected: bool = False
   407→
   408→    @property
   409→    def changes(self) -> int:
   410→        return int(self.injected)
   411→
   412→
   413→def sync_score_checkpoint_needed(
   414→    plan: PlanModel,
   415→    state: StateModel,
   416→    *,
   417→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   418→) -> ScoreCheckpointSyncResult:
   419→    """Inject ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` when all initial reviews complete.
   420→
   421→    Injects when:
   422→    - No unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain
   423→    - ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` is not already in the queue
   424→
   425→    Positioned after subjective items but before triage/create-plan
   426→    so the user sees their updated strict score right after reviews finish.
   427→    """
   428→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   429→    result = ScoreCheckpointSyncResult()
   430→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   431→
   432→    if WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID in order:
   433→        return result
   434→
   435→    # Check that no unscored dimensions remain
   436→    if policy is not None:
   437→        if policy.unscored_ids:
   438→            return result
   439→    else:
   440→        unscored = current_unscored_ids(state)
   441→        if unscored:
   442→            return result
   443→
   444→    # Insert after any subjective items, before triage/workflow/issues
   445→    insert_at = 0
   446→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   447→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX):
   448→            insert_at = i + 1
   449→    order.insert(insert_at, WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID)
   450→    result.injected = True
   451→    return result
   452→
   453→
   454→@dataclass
   455→class CreatePlanSyncResult:
   456→    """What changed during a create-plan sync."""
   457→
   458→    injected: bool = False
   459→
   460→    @property
   461→    def changes(self) -> int:
   462→        return int(self.injected)
   463→
   464→
   465→def sync_create_plan_needed(
   466→    plan: PlanModel,
   467→    state: StateModel,
   468→    *,
   469→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   470→) -> CreatePlanSyncResult:
   471→    """Inject ``workflow::create-plan`` when reviews complete + objective backlog exists.
   472→
   473→    Only injects when:
   474→    - No unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain
   475→    - At least one objective issue exists
   476→    - ``workflow::create-plan`` is not already in the queue
   477→    - No triage stages are pending
   478→    """
   479→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   480→    result = CreatePlanSyncResult()
   481→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   482→
   483→    if WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID in order:
   484→        return result
   485→
   486→    # Don't inject if triage stages are pending
   487→    if any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS):
   488→        return result
   489→
   490→    # Check that no unscored dimensions remain
   491→    if policy is not None:
   492→        if policy.unscored_ids:
   493→            return result
   494→        has_objective = policy.has_objective_backlog
   495→    else:
   496→        unscored = current_unscored_ids(state)
   497→        if unscored:
   498→            return result
   499→        issues = state.get("issues", {})
   500→        has_objective = any(
   501→            f.get("status") == "open"
   502→            and f.get("detector") not in _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   503→            for f in issues.values()
   504→        )
   505→    if not has_objective:
   506→        return result
   507→
   508→    # Insert after any subjective/workflow items, at the end of the
   509→    # synthetic block (so create-plan comes after score-checkpoint).
   510→    insert_at = 0
   511→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   512→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) or fid.startswith(TRIAGE_PREFIX) or fid.startswith(WORKFLOW_PREFIX):
   513→            insert_at = i + 1
   514→    order.insert(insert_at, WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID)
   515→    result.injected = True
   516→    return result
   517→
   518→
   519→def compute_new_issue_ids(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   520→    """Return the set of open review/concerns issue IDs added since last triage.
   521→
   522→    Returns an empty set when no prior triage has recorded ``triaged_ids``.
   523→    """
   524→    return stale_policy_mod.compute_new_issue_ids(plan, state)
   525→
   526→
   527→def is_triage_stale(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> bool:
   528→    """Side-effect-free check: is triage needed?
   529→
   530→    Returns True when genuinely *new* review issues appeared since the
   531→    last triage.  Triage stage IDs being in the queue alone is not
   532→    sufficient — the new issues that triggered injection may have been
   533→    resolved since then.
   534→
   535→    When issues are merely resolved (current IDs are a subset of
   536→    previously triaged IDs), triage is NOT stale — the user is working
   537→    through the plan.
   538→    """
   539→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   540→    return stale_policy_mod.is_triage_stale(plan, state, triage_ids=TRIAGE_IDS)
   541→
   542→
   543→@dataclass
   544→class ImportScoresSyncResult:
   545→    """What changed during an import-scores sync."""
   546→
   547→    injected: bool = False
   548→
   549→    @property
   550→    def changes(self) -> int:
   551→        return int(self.injected)
   552→
   553→
   554→def sync_import_scores_needed(
   555→    plan: PlanModel,
   556→    state: StateModel,
   557→    *,
   558→    assessment_mode: str | None = None,
   559→) -> ImportScoresSyncResult:
   560→    """Inject ``workflow::import-scores`` after issues-only import.
   561→
   562→    Only injects when:
   563→    - Assessment mode was ``issues_only`` (scores were skipped)
   564→    - ``workflow::import-scores`` is not already in the queue
   565→    - There are assessments in the payload that could be imported
   566→
   567→    Positioned after score-checkpoint, before create-plan.
   568→    """
   569→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   570→    result = ImportScoresSyncResult()
   571→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   572→
   573→    if WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID in order:
   574→        return result
   575→
   576→    # Only inject when scores were skipped (issues-only mode)
   577→    if assessment_mode != "issues_only":
   578→        return result
   579→
   580→    # Insert after any subjective/workflow items
   581→    insert_at = 0
   582→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   583→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) or fid.startswith(WORKFLOW_PREFIX):
   584→            insert_at = i + 1
   585→    order.insert(insert_at, WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID)
   586→    result.injected = True
   587→    return result
   588→
   589→
   590→@dataclass
   591→class CommunicateScoreSyncResult:
   592→    """What changed during a communicate-score sync."""
   593→
   594→    injected: bool = False
   595→
   596→    @property
   597→    def changes(self) -> int:
   598→        return int(self.injected)
   599→
   600→
   601→def sync_communicate_score_needed(
   602→    plan: PlanModel,
   603→    state: StateModel,
   604→    *,
   605→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   606→    scores_just_imported: bool = False,
   607→) -> CommunicateScoreSyncResult:
   608→    """Inject ``workflow::communicate-score`` when scores should be shown.
   609→
   610→    Injects when either:
   611→    - All initial subjective reviews are complete (no unscored dimensions), OR
   612→    - Scores were just imported (trusted/attested/override)
   613→
   614→    And ``workflow::communicate-score`` is not already in the queue.
   615→    Positioned after subjective items but before triage/create-plan.
   616→    """
   617→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   618→    result = CommunicateScoreSyncResult()
   619→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   620→
   621→    # Also treat legacy score-checkpoint as already-present
   622→    if WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID in order or WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID in order:
   623→        return result
   624→
   625→    # Trigger 1: scores just imported
   626→    should_inject = scores_just_imported
   627→
   628→    # Trigger 2: all initial reviews complete (no unscored dimensions)
   629→    if not should_inject:
   630→        if policy is not None:
   631→            should_inject = not policy.unscored_ids
   632→        else:
   633→            should_inject = not current_unscored_ids(state)
   634→
   635→    if not should_inject:
   636→        return result
   637→
   638→    # Insert after any subjective items, before triage/workflow/issues
   639→    insert_at = 0
   640→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   641→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX):
   642→            insert_at = i + 1
   643→    order.insert(insert_at, WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID)
   644→    result.injected = True
   645→    return result
   646→
   647→
   648→__all__ = [
   649→    "SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX",
   650→    "TRIAGE_ID",
```

```
1→"""Shared attestation and note validation helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import sys
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     8→
     9→_REQUIRED_ATTESTATION_PHRASES = ("i have actually", "not gaming")
    10→_ATTESTATION_KEYWORD_HINT = ("I have actually", "not gaming")
    11→_MIN_NOTE_LENGTH = 50
    12→
    13→
    14→def _emit_warning(message: str) -> None:
    15→    """Write resolve preflight warnings to stderr consistently."""
    16→    print(colorize(message, "yellow"), file=sys.stderr)
    17→
    18→
    19→def _missing_attestation_keywords(attestation: str | None) -> list[str]:
    20→    normalized = " ".join((attestation or "").strip().lower().split())
    21→    return [
    22→        phrase for phrase in _REQUIRED_ATTESTATION_PHRASES if phrase not in normalized
    23→    ]
    24→
    25→
    26→def validate_attestation(attestation: str | None) -> bool:
    27→    return not _missing_attestation_keywords(attestation)
    28→
    29→
    30→def show_attestation_requirement(
    31→    label: str,
    32→    attestation: str | None,
    33→    example: str,
    34→) -> None:
    35→    missing = _missing_attestation_keywords(attestation)
    36→    if not attestation:
    37→        _emit_warning(f"{label} requires --attest.")
    38→    elif missing:
    39→        missing_str = ", ".join(f"'{keyword}'" for keyword in missing)
    40→        _emit_warning(
    41→            f"{label} attestation is missing required keyword(s): {missing_str}."
    42→        )
    43→    _emit_warning(
    44→        f"Required keywords: '{_ATTESTATION_KEYWORD_HINT[0]}' and '{_ATTESTATION_KEYWORD_HINT[1]}'."
    45→    )
    46→    print(colorize(f'Example: --attest "{example}"', "dim"), file=sys.stderr)
    47→
    48→
    49→def validate_note_length(note: str | None) -> bool:
    50→    """Return True if the note meets the minimum length requirement."""
    51→    return note is not None and len(note.strip()) >= _MIN_NOTE_LENGTH
    52→
    53→
    54→def show_note_length_requirement(note: str | None) -> None:
    55→    """Emit a warning about minimum note length."""
    56→    current = len((note or "").strip())
    57→    _emit_warning(
    58→        f"Note must be at least {_MIN_NOTE_LENGTH} characters (got {current}). "
    59→        f"Describe what you actually did."
    60→    )
    61→
```
